# AGENTS.md — Technical Guide & Rules for AI Contributors

This repository contains Max for Live (`.amxd`) devices for **Ableton Live 12 Suite (Max 9.1+)**.

---

## 1. Core Project Rules

1. **Directory Structure**:
   - **`devices/`** — Output directory for all compiled `.amxd` devices (e.g., `devices/Better Shaper.amxd`). Never leave generated `.amxd` files in the project root.
   - **`src/`** — JavaScript (`v8ui` / `v8.codebox`) and DSP source files (e.g., `src/mseg_editor.js`).
   - **`scripts/`** — Build and visual preview scripts (`scripts/build_shaper.py`, `scripts/render_preview.py`).
   - **`tests/`** — Automated verification and unit/interaction test suites (e.g., `tests/test_shaper.js`).
   - **`previews/`** — Rendered 2x Retina plugin screenshots named per plugin (e.g., `previews/better_shaper.png`).
2. **Never Hand-Edit `.amxd` Binary Files**:
   - `.amxd` files in `devices/` are build artifacts generated by `scripts/`. Always edit `src/` and `scripts/`, then run the build script to regenerate `devices/*.amxd`.
3. **Mandatory Build, Test & Visual Screenshot Inspection Gate**:
   - Every change to `src/` or `scripts/` **must** be followed by running the build, test, and preview pipeline for that plugin (any step that launches Max or Live, such as `run_envtrig_ui_tests.py`, runs in the `macos-01` VM, section 1a):
     ```bash
     # Better Shaper
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_shaper.py && node tests/test_shaper.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Better Shaper.amxd"

     # XO Sample Map
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_xo_map.py && node tests/test_xo_map.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/XO Sample Map.amxd"

     # Tiny Space
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_tinyspace.py && node tests/test_tinyspace.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Tiny Space.amxd"

     # Sand
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_sand.py && node tests/test_sand.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Sand.amxd"

     # Reciprocator
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_reciprocator.py && node tests/test_reciprocator.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Reciprocator.amxd"

     # Percipitator
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_percipitator.py && node tests/test_percipitator.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Percipitator.amxd"

     # Degrade
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_degrade.py && node tests/test_degrade.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Degrade.amxd"

     # Multiband Split
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_mbsplit.py && node tests/test_mbsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Multiband Split.amxd"
     scripts/vm_mbsplit.sh audio && scripts/vm_mbsplit.sh livetest   # Max suite, then Live (rack sum, linking, work sharing) in the VM, own checkout ~/ableton-mbsplit (section 11)

     # Cepstra
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_cepstra.py && node tests/test_cepstra.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Cepstra.amxd"

     # Ableton MCP Bridge
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_ableton_mcp.py && node tests/test_ableton_mcp.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd"

     # Better Sampler
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_sampler.py && node tests/test_sampler.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Better Sampler.amxd"

     # Delay+ (one build writes Delay+, Delay+ End, the .adg preset and delayplus_test.als)
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_delayplus.py && node tests/test_delayplus.js && for v in "Delay+" "Delay+ End"; do python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/$v.amxd"; done

     # Envelope Trigger
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_envtrig.py && node tests/test_envtrig.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Envelope Trigger.amxd"
     scripts/vm_envtrig.sh audio && scripts/vm_envtrig.sh ui   # Max tests, in the VM (section 1a)

     # Mega Modulator
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_megamod.py && node tests/test_megamod.js && node tests/test_megamod_ui.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Mega Modulator.amxd"
     # Glitch (Max and Live tests run in the VM only, section 1a: scripts/run_glitch_audio_tests.py, scripts/vm_glitch.sh run)
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_glitch.py && node tests/test_glitch.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Glitch.amxd"

     # Spectral Split suite (sections 22-26); Max audio in the VM: scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio <slug>, Live: scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_transplit.py && node tests/test_transplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Transient Split.amxd"
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_loudsplit.py && node tests/test_loudsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Loud Split.amxd"
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_harmsplit.py && node tests/test_harmsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Harmonic Split.amxd"
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_freqsplit.py && node tests/test_freqsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Freq Split.amxd"

     # Reverb+ (one build writes Reverb+, Reverb+ End, the .adg and reverbplus_test.als; tests/test_reverbplus.js = DSP / build, tests/test_reverbplus_face.js = the face; look at previews/reverb.png and reverb_end.png)
     npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py && node tests/test_reverbplus.js && node tests/test_reverbplus_face.js && for v in "Reverb+" "Reverb+ End"; do python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/$v.amxd"; done
     scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh audio && M4L_LIVE_LOCK_WAIT=7200 scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh run   # full Max audio suite, then Live (live + test under the Live lock), in the VM (section 1a)
     ```
   - **Visual Screenshot Inspection**: After generating `previews/<plugin_slug>.png`, agents **must** view the image in `previews/` using the `read` tool and visually verify that:
     - No text labels or toolbar buttons are truncated, squished, or overlapping.
     - Compact toolbar buttons use clean geometric vector icons (`mgraphics` paths) rather than multi-character text strings when cell width is `< 28px`, displaying full titles in the HUD tooltip on hover.
     - All `live.*` widgets, panels, and `v8ui` canvases have balanced padding and alignment.
   - Any visual defect identified in the screenshot must be fixed and re-verified before finishing.
4. **Presentation Mode Layout & Z-Order Rules**:
   - Max for Live devices must maintain a fixed presentation height of **`169.0` px** (`openrect: [0.0, 0.0, <devicewidth>, 169.0]`, `openinpresentation: 1`).
   - **Never overlap `live.*` interactive widgets (`live.numbox`, `live.text`, `live.dial`, `live.tab`) with a `v8ui` / `jsui` canvas in Presentation Mode.** Max's `v8ui` captures all mouse clicks over its `presentation_rect`. Keep `live.*` widgets in non-overlapping header/side regions (verified automatically by `tests/test_shaper.js`).
   - **Exception (ignoreclick face)**: a `live.*` widget MAY overlap a `v8ui` only when that `v8ui` has `"ignoreclick": 1` (it only draws) **and** the `live.*` widget sits in front of it in z-order (later in `boxes`). This is how a custom-drawn control stays automatable / mappable (right-click > Show Automation, Map to Macro, MIDI/Key map): the real `live.*` parameter object sits over the drawing as a fully transparent widget (every colour attribute alpha `0`, `showname 0`, `shownumber 0`, no text), and the `v8ui` redraws from the parameter via `prepend set_<x>`. Any interactive `v8ui` (no `ignoreclick`) must still have zero `live.*` overlap. Enforced by `tests/test_cepstra.js`.
5. **Instance Isolation (`#0-` Naming)**:
   - Any `buffer~`, `send`, or `receive` that belongs to a single device instance **must** be prefixed with `#0-` (e.g., `buffer~ #0-mseg`, `index~ #0-mseg`) so multiple instances of the device across an Ableton Live Set never collide.
6. **Backward-Compatible State Serialization (`getvalueof` / `setvalueof`)**:
   - `v8ui` state is saved in Ableton Live Sets (`.als`) and presets (`.adv`) via blob parameter `ShapeData` (`parameter_type: 3`).
   - Always version the array returned by `getvalueof()` (first element = version integer) and keep fallback parsing for earlier versions inside `setvalueof()` so existing Live Sets never lose user curves.
7. **Undo Stack Discipline**:
   - Any user interaction that mutates MSEG points, segment curves, S-curve flags, or loop markers must call `pushUndoState()` once at the start of the gesture (`onclick` or `ondblclick`)—never on every `ondrag` frame—and call `notifyclients()` on gesture completion.
8. **`gen.codebox~`, `v8ui` Pixel Coordinates, Background Panels & Real Max Audio Verification**:
   - **Never declare or assign Max's own `v8ui` / `js` globals** (`box`, `patcher`, `max`, `messagename`, `inlet`, `jsarguments`) as script variables. `var box = null` in Delay+'s filter display crashed Ableton Live on the first mouse drag (SIGTRAP in `v8wrap_getter_ext` from `v8_onpointerevent`: Max reads its `box` accessor during pointer events). `tests/test_delayplus.js` scans the Delay+ scripts for this.
   - **`v8ui` Pixel Coordinates**: Always call `mgraphics.init(); mgraphics.relative_coords = 0; mgraphics.autofill = 0;` at the top of every `v8ui` script. Without `relative_coords = 0`, Max 9 clips pixel drawing to `0..1` and renders a blank panel in Live.
   - **Background Panel Z-Order**: All decorative `panel` boxes must appear **at the end** of the `boxes` list and include `"background": 1`, or they will cover interactive controls in Ableton Live.
  - **Integer Parameter Ranges `> 255` in Live**: Ableton Live's native `Int` parameter type (`"parameter_type": 1`) is an 8-bit integer limited to `0..255`. Any `live.numbox` or `live.dial` whose integer range exceeds `255` (such as `Seed` `0..1000`) **must** set `"parameter_type": 0` (`Float`) and `"parameter_unitstyle": 0` (`Int`).
   - **`live.numbox` Slider Fill Opacity & Bipolar Appearance (`appearance: 2` vs `3`)**:
     - `appearance: 2` (`Slider`) fills from the left edge; `appearance: 3` (`Bipolar`) fills outward from the center midpoint (`0` or `50%`).
     - Ableton Live ignores alpha in `activeslidercolor` and paints the slider fill opaque. Always pre-blend `activeslidercolor` (`38%` accent color + `62%` dark `activebgcolor`) so value text in `textcolor` remains high-contrast and legible over the fill bar inside Ableton Live.
   - **GenExpr Ordering & Lexer Rules**:
     - Every helper function must appear **before** all `Buffer`, `Param`, `History`, and `Delay` declarations, which in turn must appear **before** all expression statements.
     - **Never name identifiers `e0`–`e9`** (or `e` followed by digits): GenExpr's lexer parses `e0` as Euler's constant `e` (`2.71828...`) rather than an identifier.
     - Use `Delay d(size); d.write(x); y = d.read(samps, interp="linear");` instead of `delay()`.
     - **No early `return` inside an `if` / loop block.** Measured in Cepstra (Max 9, `gen.codebox~`): statements after a `return` nested in a block still execute, including `poke` / `History` side effects, and **the last `return` reached wins** (confirmed with a harness test), so both the "skipped" code and the value are wrong. Keep a single `return` at function level and use `if / else` (or a guarded value such as `depth = cond ? x : 0`). `tests/test_cepstra.js`, `tests/test_percipitator.js` and `tests/test_reciprocator.js` enforce this. It had silently broken Percipitator Freeze and silence sleep, and made Reciprocator's Soft/Hard Clip run as the Limiter.
     - Bind `Buffer varName("extName");` on `loadbang` using the **GenExpr variable name with no `set` prefix** (e.g., `scope #0-recipscope`).
   - **Real Max Audio Test Harness**: Every DSP device must have an automated Max audio test runner (`scripts/run_<device>_audio_tests.py`) built on the shared harness `scripts/max_harness.py` (see section 1b). It verifies audio behaviour and the live UI telemetry buffer updates.

---

## 1a. Max & Live Run Only on the Tart VM (`macos-01`) — Never Locally

**Never launch Max or Ableton Live on the host machine.** Every test that runs Max (standalone harness runners, `run_*_audio_tests.py`, `run_*_ui_tests.py`, the harness self-test) and every Ableton Live session (MCP bridge, `test_*_live.js`, `open -a "Ableton Live ..."`) runs inside the tart VM `macos-01` (`tart list` shows it as `macos-1`).
- **Login**: user `admin`, password `admin` (tart's default test VM credentials, not a real account).
- **Reach it**: `tart ip macos-1` (if it prints "no IP address found" while the VM runs, use `tart ip --resolver=arp macos-1`; the `scripts/vm_*.sh` wrappers fall back to it) (currently `192.168.64.4`), then `ssh admin@$(tart ip macos-1)`. Start it with `tart run macos-1` if `tart list` shows it stopped.
- **Workflow**: build and run pure-Node checks (`npm run -s typecheck`, `scripts/build_*.py`, `node tests/test_*.js`, `scripts/render_preview.py`) on the host as before; copy the repo (or the changed `devices/`, `scripts/`, `src/`, `tests/`) into the VM, then run Max / Live tests there over ssh. Point MCP bridge calls at the VM's IP, not `127.0.0.1`.
- **Live lock (every Live session goes through it)**: the VM has one Live, and restarting it ends whatever set another session is testing. `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh <label> -- <command...>` holds a VM-wide queueing lock (`/tmp/m4l_live.lock`, `lockf` over a long-lived ssh) for the whole command: it prints the holder while waiting (default wait 30 min, `M4L_LIVE_LOCK_WAIT=<s>`), and releases on exit, Ctrl-C or a dropped connection. `scripts/vm_<device>.sh run` (delayplus, sampler, envtrig, xo) = lock -> `live` -> `test` -> release; their `live` and `test` alone also take it (nested calls see `M4L_LIVE_LOCK_HELD=1`). Never `open` Live or restart it in the VM any other way. `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh status` shows the holder (label, host pid, start, set); `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh break` kills a stale holder; `FORCE=1` runs a command without the lock (loud warning, last resort). Standalone Max harness runs (`audio` / `max` / `ui`) keep using only `/tmp/m4l_real_max.lock` and do not wait for Live: they exec a Max by patch path and never touch Live's session. If a test ever needs both (an `audio_device` case while a Live test runs), take the Live lock first, then the Max lock.
- Everything in sections 1b and 13 about launching Max or Live, locks, ports and multiple sessions describes behaviour **inside the VM**.

---

## 1b. Real Max Test Harness (`scripts/max_harness.py`) — Mandatory for Every Max Test

All Max runs happen inside the `macos-01` VM (section 1a). Several agents may run standalone Max there at once. Every runner that launches Max must go through `scripts/max_harness.py`. Never start Max, `open` it, or write a launch loop yourself.

**What the harness does, and why** (all measured on this machine, 2026-10-19):
- **NonRealTime driver.** Each case renders on Max's `NonRealTime` audio driver (`; dsp setdriver NonRealTime`, `dsp sr 48000`). There's no hardware audio device to contend for, and renders run far faster than real time (most cases take 5–10 s, mostly launch time). On the hardware driver, overlapping runs produced partial or silent recordings.
- **One harness Max at a time, machine-wide.** Every case holds `/tmp/m4l_real_max.lock` (the same file `scripts/max_lock.py` uses) for the whole life of its Max. Max hands a newly launched patch to an already-running Max instance, even with a direct launch, so concurrent harness launches end up in one process: shared `rec` buffers, results read from another run, and one SIGTERM ending both. Separate parallel Max instances are not achievable by launching more processes of the same app. Runners may still call `run_case` from several threads or processes; the lock queues them. Before each launch and after each run, the harness kills any standalone harness Max still alive, so the next patch can't be handed to it.
- **Overdrive forced on.** Every patch sends `; max overdrive 1`. With Overdrive off, the scheduler-in-audio-thread setting (`takeover 1`) does nothing, and mid-run events never land: the host had Overdrive on in its prefs, the `macos-1` VM had it off, and every event was lost there.
- **No scheduler timing.** Under NonRealTime, scheduler milliseconds do not follow audio time: a "1000 ms" `delay` window held 144,000–244,000 samples. So no `delay`, `metro`, `pipe` or wall-clock timing anywhere in a harness patch. The scaffold records from DSP start and counts samples in gen. It copies the measured window (settle period dropped) into buffer `rec`, stamps it with the real DSP rate, then sends `analyze <dur_ms>` to the probe.
- **Direct launch.** Max is launched by exec'ing the binary with the patch in argv, plus `-ApplePersistenceIgnoreState YES`.
  - `open -n` makes the already-running Max quit, so parallel runs killed each other.
  - A direct exec from a background script keeps every instance alive and does not take screen focus.
  - A launch may forward its patch to a running instance and exit, so the launch PID is not the rendering process. The harness tracks Max by its unique temp patch path (`pgrep -f`). It finishes on the done marker, or on a non-empty result file that has stopped growing for 1 s.
  - Patch windows can't be hidden. Hiding the patch window (`this.patcher.wind.visible = 0`) stalled rendering completely. The Max Console window still appears briefly; leave it.
  - The flag skips the "reopen windows after a crash?" alert. Once Max had crashed a few times, that alert aborted every new launch.
- **No `; max quit`.** On this macOS / Max build it aborts in `maxapplication_quit` (`+[NSEvent startPeriodicEventsAfterDelay:...]` throws), which writes a crash report and shows a "Max quit unexpectedly" dialog. The scaffold writes a done marker instead, and the harness ends Max with SIGTERM, which is not a crash.
- **Timeouts.** Each case gets 300 s (`timeout=`).
- **Per-run isolation.**
  - Each run gets a unique temp dir and result path; the probe receives it as `resultpath <file>`.
  - Runner self-check: `scripts/run_max_harness_selftest.py` starts three runners at once with different durations, and each must get exactly its own window.
  - A result whose settle and duration don't match its own run is rejected.
  - Crash Recovery is cleared only when no other harness Max is running.
  - Max's `audioprefs.txt` is set back to Core Audio after the last harness Max exits, because `setdriver` persists.

**Writing a runner:**
```python
import max_harness as mh   # mh.box, mh.msg, mh.gen_box, mh.line, mh.samples_for_ms
r = mh.run_case(name, device_boxes, device_lines, probe_js=PROBE, probe_args="",
                settle_ms=300, dur_ms=500, channels=2, sr=48000, mode="nrt",
                events=[(at_ms_from_dsp_start, "target-id", inlet, "message")])
```
- The scaffold provides these ids, so don't reuse them:
  - `obj-load`: loadbang that fires before DSP starts. Send parameter and buffer-bind messages from it. If their order matters, sequence them yourself with a `t b b b`.
  - `obj-record`: connect the signals to analyze to inlets `0..channels-1`.
  - `obj-mute-l` / `obj-mute-r`: `*~ 0` into `dac~`.
  - `obj-js`: your probe.
  - `hx-*`.
- Probe engine: classic `js` by default; `probe_object="v8"` for ES6 probes.
- Name device-level buffers, sends and receives with `#0-`, as in the devices themselves. `rec` / `rec_full` belong to the scaffold.
- Probe contract:
  - `function resultpath(p) { resultPath = String(p); }`
  - `analyze <dur_ms>`: read buffer `rec`, write `key value` lines (one token per value) to `resultPath`, then bang the outlet.
  - Extra probe files go next to `resultPath`; read them with `keep_dir=True` and `r["harness_dir"]`.
- Reference consumer: `scripts/run_max_harness_selftest.py` (exact window, event timing, 3 parallel cases). Run it after changing the harness.

**Modes:**
- `nrt` (default): fastest.
- `stepped`: NonRealTime with `takeover 1`, so `events` land within one vector (measured 1 sample late on the host and in the VM). `run_case` switches any case with `events` to `stepped` automatically; under `nrt` the events miss their time.
- `audio_device`: the real CoreAudio driver, in real time (same machine-wide lock). Only for the few tests that depend on the hardware driver itself, such as real-time CPU load or device sample-rate switching. It must stay rare. Say why in a comment.

`scripts/max_lock.py` remains only for legacy, unmigrated runners; it uses the same lock. New runners never need it.

---

## 1c. Type-Checking Plain JS (`tsc --noEmit`, no TypeScript build)

Sources stay plain `.js`; nothing is compiled or emitted. TypeScript only checks them (`package.json` dev dependencies, run `npm install` once), so a typo in a Max API name or a cross-file function name fails before the build. `npm run typecheck` checks every device.
- **Max API types** come from DefinitelyTyped: `@types/maxmsp` (js / jsui / v8 / v8ui globals: `mgraphics`, `outlet`, `post`, `Buffer`, `Dict`, `Global`, `LiveAPI`, `Task`, `Patcher`, `Maxobj`, ...) and `@types/max-api` (Node for Max). `@types/node` is pinned to 22, the Node that Max 9 bundles (`v22.18.0`). What these lack or mistype goes in `src/types/max-extras.d.ts`; never silence an error with `any` or `@ts-ignore` in a source file.
- **One config per script group**: `tsconfig.<device>[-<part>].json` lists, in build order, `src/types/max-extras.d.ts`, the device's own `src/types/<device>.d.ts` (globals the build script injects, e.g. `FACE_REGION`) and every `.js` file the build concatenates into that one `v8ui` / `js` script. It extends `tsconfig.base.json` (`checkJs`, `lib: es2022`, no DOM, `strict` off), or `tsconfig.node.base.json` for `node.codebox` / `node.script` scripts. Groups stay separate because devices define the same globals (`paint`, `onclick`, ...).
- **Every gate starts with `npm run -s typecheck`** (all 28 configs, about 2 s). A new device or script group needs its own config before its first build, or it is silently unchecked.
- **Keep shared config files global scripts.** A top-level `module.exports = ...` makes TypeScript treat the file as a CommonJS module, and its functions vanish from the scripts it is prepended to. Export through a local: `var nodeModule = (typeof module !== "undefined") ? module : undefined; if (nodeModule && nodeModule.exports) nodeModule.exports = {...};`.
- **Fix inference with JSDoc**, e.g. `/** @type {Array<number | string>} */` on a mixed array. Untyped JS parameters are optional, so argument-count mistakes are only caught once a function has `@param` tags; add them to shared helpers as you touch them.

---

## 1d. Fast Device Loading — Apply to Every Device with a Large Gen Core

Live blocks while a device instantiates, and almost all of that time is the **gen compile**: the v8ui faces and the patcher itself cost next to nothing. Measure first: `scripts/measure_sampler_loadtime.py` runs in the VM and gives harness wall time for a trivial gen, the core, and the core plus faces. Better Sampler went from a 7 s load to an instant GUI, with DSP ready about 2 s later, using the steps below. Reference implementation: `src/sampler_ui.js` (`loadDsp`, `dspConfirm`, `dsp_diag`) and `scripts/build_sampler.py` (`gendsp_json`, the `obj-gen-in` hub).

1. **Compile each piece of voice code once.** GenExpr inlines every call, so 16 calls to a big `voice_proc` compile it 16 times. Run voices in a `for (v = 0; v < N; v += 1)` loop around a single call site. A call site's `History` is shared across loop iterations, so move per-voice state into a global `Data` indexed by voice (e.g. `Data vhist(N * 256)`) and write non-zero initial values once in an `initDone` block. Skip idle voices (released, silent, no tail) and output exact zeros: CPU often halves. Never add a second call site of a big helper. Gotcha: a function-local variable with the same name as a global `Data` silently breaks the compile.
2. **Ship the device without its `gen~` and create it after load (GUI first).**
   - The build embeds the gen patch as `.gendsp` JSON in the main v8ui script only (`GEN_DSP_TEXT`, plus a content hash).
   - On `live.thisdevice`'s ready bang, wait one Task tick (about 30 ms) so the GUI paints. Then write `/tmp/<device>_core_<hash>.gendsp` (only if missing), create `gen~ @gen <file>` with `this.patcher.newdefault`, and wire it with `patcher.connect`. Give the objects it connects to scripting names (`varname`).
   - Route every gen-bound message (params, buffer binding, tempo) through one static hub that the new gen is connected to. **Use `gate 1 1` (open; data into the right inlet), never `t a`**: a scripted `connect()` from `t a` silently makes no cord.
   - **Don't** pass the code as `@code` / `setattr("code")` to a new `gen.codebox~`: the text is re-tokenised (newlines and comments lost, commas split) and a large core never runs.
   - The gen keeps compiling after creation and drops messages until it runs. Re-send (a `t b b` that re-bangs the buffer-binding message and every gen-bound `live.*` parameter) every 150 ms until the gen's telemetry frame counter moves, with a 10 s timeout. Only then mark the DSP ready.
   - Show a small header pill: `DSP loading…`, or `DSP failed: <reason>`. Notes before that are silent.
3. **Defer heavy UI work too.** Analysis such as a full mono copy of a sample runs lazily, in chunks on a `Task`, only when a feature needs it.
4. **Verify in Live, not only in the harness.** The harness's offline driver never adds objects created mid-run to its DSP chain (not even a plain `cycle~`). Prove the deferred path with a Live set in the VM and the bridge, like `scripts/vm_sampler.sh run` (= `live` + `test` under the Live lock, section 1a). Add a debug receiver (`receive #0-<dev>debug` → face; `#0` is literal in a top-level device, so the bridge's `messnamed` reaches it) with a `dsp_diag` message that traces the gen's class, its cord counts and the state.
5. **Split by code, not by size; hot-load instead of creating** (measured on Glitch, section 19):
   - Gen charges for every function definition, even uncalled (about 1 ms per line), so only the code a stage needs should be in its gen. The control engine must exist once; share its output with other gens through a ring buffer indexed by a tick-counter signal.
   - **Never add or remove a gen~ or a cord in a running device**: any structural change silences the whole device for the new gen~'s compile time (0.1–0.7 s, measured with a tail tick counter against the wall clock; ring telemetry hides it). Create all shells and cords once right after load, then `setattr gen <file>` on an existing shell (loses only 2–24 ms).
   - Embedded gen~ shells cost ~28 ms each at patcher load; file-backed identical shells share one compile. `gen~` caches compiles per process, not on disk. Textual inlining of single-call functions was slower. `delay~ N` is exact below a vector, so a `delay~` can stand in for a not-yet-loaded stage at the same latency.

## 2. `.amxd` Binary Format Reference

An unencrypted Max for Live `.amxd` file consists of a 32-byte binary header followed by UTF-8 JSON patcher data:
- Bytes `0..7`: `b"ampf\x04\x00\x00\x00"`
- Bytes `8..11`: Device type 4CC:
  - `b"aaaa"` (`amxdtype: 1633771873` / `0x61616161`) — Max Audio Effect (`plugin~` / `plugout~`)
  - `b"mmmm"` (`amxdtype: 1835887981` / `0x6d6d6d6d`) — Max MIDI Effect (`midiin` / `midiout`)
  - `b"iiii"` (`amxdtype: 1768515945` / `0x69696969`) — Max Instrument
- Bytes `12..23`: `b"meta\x04\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00\x00"`
- Bytes `24..27`: `b"ptch"`
- Bytes `28..31`: `uint32_le` length of the following JSON byte payload.
- Bytes `32..end`: UTF-8 encoded patcher JSON ending with `\n`.

---

## 3. Architecture of `devices/Better Shaper.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`436 x 169` px — Native 2-Panel Footprint)
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..278`) — Toggles Between MSEG View & 8-Slot Mod View**:
  - **Top Row (`y = 8..23`, Always Visible)**:
    - Slot 1 (`Map[1]`, `Unmap[1]`, `ModMode[1]`, `Bipolar[1]` + `ModAmount[1]` or `TargetMin[1]` + `TargetMax[1]`) on the left, and the Multimap Show/Hide button (`obj-expand-map`, `multimap-closed-off.svg` / `multimap-open-off.svg` at `x = 255, y = 8`) on the top-right.
  - **MSEG View (`ShowModPanel == 0`, default)**:
    - MSEG Editor (`obj-mseg`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 26, w = 262, h = 134`):
      - `y = 0..11`: Loop Start / End region bar.
      - `y = 18..106`: Main MSEG plot area (`7px` horizontal & top padding so `0%` and `100%` points never clip).
      - `y = 112..134`: 9-item vector icon toolbar (`Undo`, `Redo`, `Draw`, `Brush`, `Sine`, `Randomize Ramps`, `Randomize Steps`, `Randomize Smooth Curve`, `Flip X`) on `x = 0..162`, with the right `100px` (`x = 162..262`) rendering and controlling `Grid` and `Snap` natively inside `v8ui` (synced bidirectionally with `obj-grid` and `obj-snap-btn`).
  - **8-Slot Mod View (`ShowModPanel == 1`)**:
    - Hides `obj-mseg` (`hidden 1`, which also hides the built-in `Grid` and `Snap` controls).
    - Displays Slots 2–8 (`row_y = 8 + i * 16.5` via `p SlotVis_2..8`) and the bottom column labels (`Parameter`, `Mode`, `— Range —` at `y = 143`).
    - In each slot `1..8`, `ModMode == 0` (`Mod`, `live.modulate~`) shows `Bipolar` (`+` / `±`) and `ModAmount` (`-100%..+100%`), while `ModMode == 1` (`Remote`, `live.remote~ @normalized 1`) shows `TargetMin` (`0%..100%`) and `TargetMax` (`0%..100%`).
- **Right Panel (`x = 282..432` — Live 12 Native Shaper Layout)**:
  - Top dark LCD panel (`y = 5..82`):
    - `live.scope~` (`x = 284..430, y = 7..46`).
    - LCD Row 1 (`y = 47..63`): `Jitter` (`0 %`) and `Smooth` (`0 %`) borderless `live.numbox` controls (`appearance: 4`).
    - LCD Row 2 (`y = 63..79`): Circular `(R)` Retrigger button (`obj-retrig`), `Ø` Phase label + `Phase` (`0.00 %`) borderless `live.numbox`, and `Offset` (`0 %`) borderless `live.numbox`.
  - Middle playback mode row (`y = 88..103`): `[ Loop ] [ 1-Shot ] [ Manual ]` horizontal `live.tab` (`obj-play-mode`, driving `selector~ 2 1` between `phasor~`, one-shot `line~`, and manual `Ø` phase scrubbing).
  - Bottom controls (`y = 110..158`): `Rate` (`Hz` dial and `Sync` dial toggled via `hidden $1`), horizontal connecting line (`obj-rate-line`), stacked `Hz` / Metronome `live.tab` (`obj-rate-mode`), and `Depth` dial.

### `v8ui` + Audio-Rate `buffer~` Integration
- `obj-mseg` (`maxclass: "v8ui"`) embeds the ES6+ script inline via `"textfile": { "text": ..., "filename": "none", "flags": 0, "embed": 1, "autowatch": 1 }`.
- `obj-buf` (`buffer~ #0-mseg @samps 4096`) holds the 4096-sample lookup table. On startup, `loadmess setbuffer #0-mseg` passes the unique `#0-mseg` buffer name to `v8ui`, which writes all 4096 samples via `new Buffer(bufName).poke(1, 0, samples)` whenever the curve changes.
- `v8ui` Outlet 0 emits `["loop", loopStart, loopSpan]` to window the MSP `phasor~` phase into `[loopStart, loopStart + loopSpan]`, and `["syncphase", phase]` to align `phasor~` with Ableton Live's 1-PPQ arrangement timeline position (`plugsync~` outlet 6) in Sync mode.
- `v8ui` Outlet 1 emits the LFO frequency in Hz to `phasor~` (automatically scaled by `1 / loopSpan` when a loop sub-region is active so tempo-synced subdivisions keep constant musical speed).

## 4. Architecture of `devices/XO Sample Map.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`776 x 169` px — Dynamically Shrinks down to `256 x 169` px via `live.thisdevice` `setwidth`)
- **Section collapse** (`src/xo_layouts/default.js`, `computeXoLayout(theme, saved, filters, playback, options)`, see "Themes, the Options section and the left toolbar" below): visible panels pack left to right with a 6 px gap and the 2D map pane always stays `236` px wide (hiding `Saved` removes only its `136` px). The mini-map v8ui applies it (`applyXoLayout()`: `getnamed` + `presentation_rect` / `hidden`) on every toggle and on `live.thisdevice` (`apply_layout`); the build gives every laid-out box a scripting name and injects `XO_LAYOUT_NAMES`. `obj-expr-width` (`expr 278 + $i1 * 136 + $i4 * 196 + $i2 * 214 + $i3 * (170 - 12 * ($i5 > 0))`; inputs saved filters playback options theme, theme 0 = LCD / 1 = Focus / 2 = Live; `($i5 > 0)` keeps the comma out of the expr) sets the device width and must match the layout (`tests/test_xo_map.js` checks 3 themes x 16 combinations). The numbers in the next bullets describe the LCD theme.
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..386`, or `6..250` when `Saved` is collapsed)**:
  - Top bar (`y = 9..24`, no title: Live's header names the device): 3 section Collapse/Show toggles starting at `x = 10`, 3 px apart (`Saved`, `Filters`, `Playback` — wired to `live.thisdevice` `setwidth` and `thispatcher` so hiding any section shrinks the M4L device width in Ableton Live).
  - Mini Editor (`obj-minimap`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 27, w = 372, h = 134`):
    - **Left Pane (`x = 0..236`)**:
      - `y = 0..20`: Selected sample badge, name, and an **Ableton-style whole-file waveform** (filled min/max envelope, brighter RMS core, centre line, linear amplitude; Reverse mirrors it; `Trim/Dec` dims the part that will be cut) with a **playhead line that follows the real playback position** (see "Waveform playhead" below). Node decodes the file (`computeWaveformFromBuffer`, all channels reduced to the largest-magnitude sample, small LRU `waveformCache`) and sends `selected_wave <idx> <n> <min0> <max0> <rms0> ...` (256 columns, 3 decimals, `n = 0` = undecodable) after `selected_info`, through the last `obj-route-node` selector and `obj-prep-sel-wave`; until it arrives (or if it fails) the 24 cached peaks are drawn as a smooth envelope (click to audition/replay; drag to export the single Raw sample directly from its original disk path).
      - `y = 22..112`: Interactive 2D sample constellation map (up to `Map Samples` samples displayed at once, default `1,500`, 100..5000, Options > Map Samples, from the full background-indexed corpus) with k-NN neighbor lines and filter-aware drag-to-scrub auditioning.
        - **Zoom / pan (plot area only)**: wheel / trackpad scroll zooms around the cursor (`1x..16x`, `1x` is exactly the full view; `onwheel` follows Max's documented `onwheel(x, y, inc_x, inc_y, cmd, shift, capslock, option, ctrl)`: with up to 9 arguments the deltas are args 2 / 3 — taking the last two read the option / ctrl flags and a real wheel never zoomed — 10+ arguments use the last two, an event object its `deltaX / deltaY`; `WHEEL_ZOOM_SIGN` flips the direction; the zoom per wheel unit is `exp(0.96 * Zoom Speed)` (Options > Zoom Speed, see "Options section"; `wheelZoomFactor`: dy clamped to +-3 units, event exponent capped at `max(1.8, 0.96 * speed)` so a single mouse step is never cut at any speed while trackpad flicks cannot jump); Max 9's v8 binary dispatches `onwheel` and `onpointerdown/move/up` events with `buttons` / `altKey` / `metaKey`); **middle-button drag**, **Cmd-drag** or **Option-drag** pans (legacy mouse handlers get a PointerEvent as 9th argument, `buttons & 4`; `cmd` / `option` are onclick args 3 / 6); double-click on empty map space (no sample within 26 px) or a click on the `N.Nx` pill (bottom-left of the plot) resets the view. The real argument lists of `onwheel` / `onclick` have not been seen in Live (no real mouse in the VM; `xoRawLog` records them for the test report). k-NN lines are drawn only from the point the neighbour list belongs to (`selectedMeta.nbrFor`): when a filter hides the selection, `ensureValidSelection()` moves it and announces it with `select_index <idx> 0`. Everything goes through `normToScreen` / `screenToNorm`; dots, rings and k-NN lines are culled / clipped to the plot rect while zoomed, and a selection that leaves the view (arrow keys, history) is panned into view. Zoom is not persisted.
        - **Dot groups (round-robin audition)**: points whose SCREEN centres overlap almost entirely are drawn as ONE dot (`ensureGroups()`, `src/xo_strip_ui.js`). Rule: a point joins the nearest existing group whose first member is within `GROUP_DIST_FACTOR = 1.05` x the dot radius (2.2 px with up to 250 points, 1.8 px above; tuned in Live so the 1500-point Core Library map draws as about 1000 dots: 0.6 -> 1471, 1.0 -> 1043, 1.05 -> 996, 1.5 -> 638, 2.0 -> 391; `test_groupdist <factor>` sets it at runtime); leader based, not transitive; visible and filtered-out (dim) points never mix; points outside the plot while zoomed are culled. A spatial hash (cell = that distance) keeps it O(n); the result is cached by a key of the point set (`pointsVer`, bumped by `minimap_clear` / `minimap_batch` / the default constellation), category, MaxLen / Tone / Tonal / Drums / search, view (zoom / pan) and plot size, so paint, hit-testing and hover never regroup, zooming in splits groups and zooming out merges them. A group's identity and dot position are its lowest-index member; the dot has that member's category colour and looks exactly like a single dot (no ring or other marker: a ring on groups made dense areas look odd in Live). Hit-testing works on groups (`findNearestGroup`; `findNearestVisiblePoint` returns the group's lead sample without advancing anything). **What plays**: a click and a drag-scrub landing, nothing else (plain hover only highlights the group's dot and shows the HUD; it plays nothing, as before). Each click on a group, and each scrub landing on a different group (or on the same one after leaving every dot), plays the group's NEXT member (`groupAdvance`, counter in `groupRR` keyed by the lead index, reset when the groups are rebuilt); staying on one group during a drag does not re-trigger (`scrubGroupLeader`); a double-click replays the member its click just played. The selection ring, hover ring and k-NN lines refer to the chosen member but are drawn at its group's dot (`dotPosOf`); arrow keys, history, Random and saved-list recall still select individual samples. The hover HUD shows the member the next click plays plus `+N` (`Kick 808 (118ms) +3`). Measured (Node mock, clustered layout): 1500 points -> 1105 dots, 5000 -> 2425; first grouping 1.5 / 3.3 ms, cached lookup 0.001 ms, paint 0.6 / 1.1 ms. `test_report.groups` = `{count, multi, maxSize, drawn, builds, sample[{leader, n, x, y, members}]}`; `scripts/test_xo_live.js` check `n` clicks a group repeatedly and requires its members in order.
      - `y = 112..134`: 13-item geometric vector icon toolbar (`All`, `Kick`, `Snare`, `Hi-Hat`, `Clap`, `Perc`, `Shaker`, `Cymbal`, `Voice`, `Prev Similar`, `Next Similar`, `Random`, `Re-Seed`; cells `236 / 13` = 18.2 px, icons are `mgraphics` paths, the full title shows in the HUD tooltip). **Toolbar `Re-Seed` applies the Filters section's filters**: it sends `reseed_filtered` (the same message as `obj-reseed-filtered-btn`), so the node re-seeds the map from the corpus samples that pass search text, MaxLen, Tone, Tonal, Drums and the enabled folders (the toolbar's category selection is not part of that, same as the Filters button); tooltip `Re-Seed Map with active Filters (N max)` with the current Map Samples number (`reseedTitle()`, also set as the Filters button's `annotation`). The old unfiltered `reseed_map` shuffle is no longer sent by the UI (the node handler stays). **The category buttons select by WORDS, not by the classifier** (next section: "Category search by words"); the classifier only places the dots on the map.
    - **Right Pane (`x = 236..372`) — Scrollable Saved Samples List & OS File Drag-Out Dock**:
      - `y = 0..20`: `SAVED (N)` header (starts empty on new instances), `+Save` button (`S` hotkey), and `×` clear-all button.
      - `y = 21..109`: Scrollable window of saved sample rows (with right-edge scrollbar thumb) supporting click-to-select/recall, vertical drag-to-reorder (`reorder_saved`), per-row `[×]` delete button (`Delete`/`Backspace` key), and separate per-item **`[R]` (Raw)** and **`[E]` (Edited)** OS file drag handles.
      - `y = 112..134`: Bottom batch OS file drag-out zones **`[Drag Raw]`** (`all_raw`) and **`[Drag Edit]`** (`all_edited`).
- **Hotkeys** (`key` -> `obj-sel-key` `sel 115 83 127 8 -5 -3 28 29 30 31 114 82 27` -> message boxes -> `obj-minimap`, plus v8ui `onkeydown`; both paths can deliver one key press, so every action is de-duplicated, the arrows, `r` and Esc within `ARROW_DEDUP_MS = 20`): `S` save, **`r` / `R`** (114 / 82) = Random Sample (`hotkey_random` -> `applyToolbarAction("random")`, the toolbar button's code path, so the active category and filters apply), **Esc** (27) = stop playback (`hotkey_stop`, see "Waveform playhead"), `Delete` / `Backspace` delete saved, **Left / Right** = Prev / Next Similar (same `applyToolbarAction("sim_prev" / "sim_next")` path as the toolbar), **Down / Up** = selection history Back / Forward. Max `key` reports the arrows as `28` (left), `29` (right), `30` (up), `31` (down) on macOS; `onkeydown(keycode, textcharacter, down, ...)` is also accepted with those codes or `ArrowLeft` / `left` style names (its exact argument is not documented by Cycling '74, verify in Live); key-up events (`down = 0`) are ignored. **Esc through `onkeydown` arrives as keycode `-3` with textcharacter `27`** (measured for Mega Modulator): the character is checked first, because `-3` alone is a delete code here and an Esc must never delete a saved row. The patcher `key` object reports Esc as `27`. **Esc and typing**: Max's `textedit` consumes keys while focused, but whether the patcher `key` object still hears an Esc typed in the search box / Options terms field could not be checked in the VM (no real keyboard input), and a `textedit` cannot report its focus: in the worst case Esc in a text field also stops the audition (harmless). History lives in the node engine (`selectionHistory`, file paths, capped at `200`): every selection that is not a history move (`emitSelectedToMax`) pushes and truncates the forward part, a duplicate of the current entry is skipped, entries no longer in the map are skipped when navigating, and `history_back` / `history_forward` audition the sound.
- **Center Panel — `FILTERS` (`x = 392..600`)**:
  - Visibly separate cyan-bordered panel containing `FILTERS` header, `Re-Seed` button (to the left of `Clr`, re-seeds and fills the map exclusively with corpus samples matching active filters), `Clr` button, `obj-search-box` (`textedit` for filtering by name), 4 filter dials (`MaxLen` `50 ms..Inf`, `Tone` `%`, `Tonal` `%`, and `Drums` / Druminess `%` selecting between Pads/Clusters vs. Drums), and the scrollable `FOLDERS` list (`obj-folder-list`, `v8ui` in `src/xo_folder_ui.js`, automatically including Ableton Core Drums, remembering added folders, and supporting click-to-toggle and `[×]` delete from index).
- **Right Panel — `PLAYBACK` (`x = 606..770`)**:
  - Visibly separate amber-bordered panel containing `PLAYBACK` header, `Reverse` toggle button (`obj-reverse-btn`, where `Trim/Dec` trims and sweeps the start of the reversed sample), and 6 dials in a 3×2 grid: `Pitch` (`st`), `Speed` (`10..400%`), `Grain` (`5..250 ms`), `Trim/Dec` (`20..20000 ms` / `20 s`), `Mode` (`obj-stretch-mode`, enum `StretchMode`: `Grain` / `Cmplx` / `Textr`, default `Cmplx`) and `Gain` (`dB`).
  - **`Mode` selects the offline stretch engine** (all in `src/xo_stretch.js`, tested by `tests/test_xo_stretch.js`; the node engine's `renderEditedAudioSamples` calls `stretchAudioEx`). **Pitch and Speed are independent (Ableton Complex behaviour) in every mode: `Pitch` changes pitch only, `Speed` changes duration only (output length = input length / Speed, minus `Trim/Dec`), whatever the Pitch.** The render-needed rule (`stretchEngineWanted(mode, speedPct, pitchSt)`) is `Speed != 100%` OR `Pitch != 0` OR mode Texture; only `Speed 100%` + `Pitch 0` (non-Texture) is played untouched by `sfplay~` (speed 1.0). `sfplay~` is never repitched on a rendered file (`play_speed` is always 1; `set_pitch` no longer sends it), the engine is asked for `speed = Speed` and `pitchSt = Pitch`, and `play_scan_dur` (the waveform scan line) is the rendered file's length (`renderEditedSampleWavEx` returns `durationMs`/`frames`). Renders are synchronous and cached by source file + settings (`renderCache`, 64 entries; `renderStats`); dial drags (`set_pitch`/`set_speed`/`set_grain`/`set_stretchmode`/`set_decay`/`set_reverse`) call `requestRenderPrewarm()`, which waits `renderDebounce.ms` (150 ms) of quiet, supersedes earlier requests and pre-renders only the latest setting into the cache, so the next audition (`emitSelectedToMax`) is instant. Saved items and Edited exports use the same rule. The node also sends `audition_info <file> <ms> <frames> <rendered> <engine> <pitch> <speed> <mode>` (route outlet 22 -> `obj-prep-audition-info` -> v8ui) for the HUD and the tests.
    - `Grain` (granular): 4x-overlap Hann grains; `Grain` = grain length.
    - `Cmplx` (complex): phase vocoder (FFT 4096, phase-locked, onset-aware).
    - `Textr` (texture): PaulStretch-style frozen smear; always rendered, also at Speed 100%.
    - **`Grain` per mode**: grain size in `Grain`; in `Textr` the same dial is the window size (`5..250 ms` maps linearly to `amount 0..1` = a `4096..32768` sample window); in `Cmplx` it is unused and dimmed (`active 0`, set by the mini-map v8ui in `set_stretchmode`).
    - The old `Spectral` dial / `SpectralStretch` parameter and its 512-point STFT smear are gone. Saved items store `stretchMode` (0..2: Grain, Cmplx, Textr; `STRETCH_MODES` in the node engine is the one index -> engine table, `stretchModeIndex()` clamps); old items with `spectralPct > 0` load as Granular, items without a mode as Complex. Rendered file names include the mode (`_gr`/`_cx`/`_tx`) and, for Grain and Textr, the grain value.
  - **Signalsmith Stretch was removed** (it was a fourth engine, `Smith`, between Cmplx and Textr; it needed a 98 KB WASM payload, an async loader and a `SMITH N/A` HUD fallback, and the device was 364 KB with it). `vendor/signalsmith-stretch/` is gone, and so are `loadSignalsmith`, `stretchAudioAsync`, the `stretch_status` route outlet and the HUD banner. `src/xo_stretch.js` is wrapped in a function and prepended as `XO_STRETCH_EMBEDDED` (the codebox never `require`s it).
  - **Compatibility after the removal**: old Textr moved from index 3 to 2. Saved items (`stretchMode` in `savedList`, which is not written to the Live Set) carry current indices, so nothing numeric needs migrating; a text mode `"signalsmith"` / `"smith"` loads as Complex and `"texture"` as Texture (`LEGACY_STRETCH_NAMES`, the only remaining mention of the name in the built node code). **Live Sets that saved the `StretchMode` parameter cannot be remapped**: a set saved on Smith (index 2) now loads as Textr, and a set saved on Textr (index 3) clamps to Textr (index 2), so a set saved on Smith needs its Mode set to Cmplx by hand. Do not add hacks for this; the enum index is what Live stores.
  - Device size went from 376 KB (with Signalsmith) to 305 KB.

### In Live (tart VM) — `scripts/vm_xo.sh`
Max and Live run only in the VM (section 1a). `vm_xo.sh` is modelled on `vm_sampler.sh` / `vm_envtrig.sh`; sync excludes `.git`, `node_modules`, `previews` and the VM's generated `*_test.als` sets.
```bash
python3 scripts/build_xo_map.py && node tests/test_xo_map.js           # host gate first
scripts/vm_xo.sh max      # Max runtime part of tests/test_xo_map.js (see below)
scripts/vm_xo.sh run      # recommended: live + test under one Live-lock hold (section 1a)
scripts/vm_xo.sh live     # sync, rebuild in the VM, write xo_test.als (scripts/make_xo_set.py), restart Live on it, wait for the bridge
scripts/vm_xo.sh test     # scripts/test_xo_live.js on the host through an ssh tunnel (local 19879 -> VM 9877; --ssh lets it stat rendered files)
scripts/vm_xo.sh shot     # screencapture of the VM screen, copied back to the scratchpad
```
- `max`: `tests/test_xo_map.js` no longer launches Max itself. With `XO_RUN_MAX_RUNTIME=1` it only writes `tests/out/xo_runtime/xo_m4l_runtime_harness.maxpat` (the VM has no `node`); `scripts/run_xo_runtime_test.py` runs it in the VM through `scripts/max_harness.py` (machine-wide `/tmp/m4l_real_max.lock`, direct exec, stale-Max cleanup, SIGTERM, crash-recovery clearing) and checks node boot in Presentation Mode, the `s` hotkey, the four drag-out zones and the Cocoa drag helper. Deviation from 1b: it is a UI / node test, so it uses a plain launch with `delay` driving the clicks, not the NonRealTime scaffold.
- `live`: `xo_test.als` = audio track `XO Sample Map` (the device directly on it) + the MCP Bridge on Main. It refuses to restart a Live that has another set open (`FORCE=1` overrides).
- Test hook: `receive #0-xo-test` -> `obj-minimap` (`#0` is literal in a top-level device; `messnamed("#0-xo-test", ...)` from `ableton_eval_lom_js`): `test_report` writes `{selection, wave n, view, section flags, every laid-out box's presentation_rect/hidden, device width, last audition_info, raw event arguments}` to `Global("xo_test")`; `test_select`, `test_replay`, `test_wheel`, `test_click`, `test_drag`, `test_release`, `test_clear_log`, `test_section saved|filters|playback|options 0|1`, `test_theme LCD|Focus`, `test_mirror <box id> <value>` (the report also carries `theme`, `options`, the mirrors' values and a few styled attributes); the `hotkey_*` functions are called directly. The v8ui keeps the raw arguments of the last real `onwheel` / `onclick` / `ondrag` (`raw`, `rawCount`).
- `scripts/test_xo_live.js` checks: (a) node booted and indexed; (b) both themes x all 16 Saved/Filters/Playback/Options combinations against `computeXoLayout` (rects, hidden, width, map pane 236 in both themes, Theme selector, theme style), (b2) LOM parameter <-> mirror sync both ways in both themes; (c) sample selected, `selected_wave` n > 0; (d) arrow actions fire once and history back/forward retraces; (e) zoom/pan; (f) Speed 50 % + Pitch +7 in every mode (length = input/0.5, scan duration, file exists, track meter > 0); (g) Pitch +12 at Speed 100 % renders with unchanged length.

### Multi-Engine Integration (`node.codebox` + `v8ui` + 104-Byte Binary Cache + Native Cocoa Drag Helper)
- `obj-node` (`node.codebox`, placed at the end of `boxes` with `background: 1` and a `1×1` `presentation_rect` inside `0..169`, at `[13, 152, 1, 1]` = under the centre of the Options (gear) toggle: an opaque foreground button (background / on colours alpha 1 in all three themes) that the layout never moves or hides, so the node boots in presentation mode without a stray mark showing; it used to sit behind `obj-panel-left` and showed at the toolbar edge once the toolbar moved to the left) embeds `src/xo_node_engine.js`, indexing large directories asynchronously in the background into a compact 104-byte per-sample binary cache (`~/.xo_sample_map_cache_v3.bin` + `.idx.json`).
- **No pop-out window.** The old `Open Map` button, `p XOMapWindow` / `jweb`, the HTML UI, and the node HTTP/SSE server (`server_url`) were removed; the in-device `v8ui` is the only UI. `obj-route-node` has 22 outlets (`minimap_clear` first); `tests/test_xo_map.js` asserts the removed boxes are gone.
- **Hotkeys only act on the selected instance.** The patcher's `key` object hears every keystroke in Live, so the `hotkey_*` / `delete_selected_saved` messages are gated in the mini-map v8ui on `hotkeyAllowed()`: this device must be the `selected_device` of the selected track's view (`init_selection` from `live.thisdevice`; observers on `live_set view selected_track` and `id <track view> selected_device`, notifications filtered by property name because a new LiveAPI first reports its own `["id", N]`, work deferred with `Task.schedule(0)`). A device inside a rack chain is reported by its own id when it is the selected one. Unknown LOM = not selected = no action. `onkeydown` (focused v8ui) is never gated. Test hooks: `test_selected 1|0|-1`, `test_only <device id|0>` (aims the other `test_*` hooks at one instance), `receive #0-xo-testkey` -> `obj-sel-key`; `test_report` has `isSelected`. `scripts/make_xo_set.py` writes 3 instances (2 tracks + one in a rack).
- **Waveform playhead = the real `sfplay~` position** (replaces the old constant-speed `line` animation, `obj-scan-msg` / `obj-scan-line` / `obj-prep-playhead` are gone):
  - **Source of truth**: `obj-sfplay` is `sfplay~ 2 0 1` (2 channels, default disk buffer, position-outlet flag 1): outlet 2 is a signal with the playing file's position in ms, outlet 3 the bang on finish / halt. `obj-snap-pos` (`snapshot~ 33 @active 0`) samples outlet 2 about every 33 ms; it is started by `start` (from `obj-trig-play`'s last bang, together with the `1` that plays) and stopped by `stop` (sfplay~'s finish bang), so nothing polls while idle. `obj-prep-playpos` (`prepend play_pos`) -> `obj-minimap`; the finish bang also goes out as `play_done` (message box `obj-msg-play-done`). `snapshot~` reads the signal value, so timing jitter of the scheduler only delays a sample, it never accumulates.
  - **Mapping** (`playheadFraction(map, posMs)` in `src/xo_strip_ui.js`, pure, unit-tested): the banner shows the SOURCE file; the node sends a map with every audition as extra `audition_info` arguments (`rate skipMs srcMs endMs rev`, built by `buildPlayMap()` in `src/xo_node_engine.js`): fraction = `(skipMs + t) * rate / srcMs`, mirrored when `rev`. Source played directly: `rate 1`, `endMs` = file length, or the Trim/Dec cut when Trim/Dec fades the direct play (the file keeps running silently, the line stops at the cut). Rendered file (Speed / Pitch / Texture / Reverse): `rate` = Speed ratio (source ms per rendered ms), `endMs` = rendered length; Reverse moves the line right to left and, with Trim/Dec, `skipMs = source / ratio - rendered length` (the render keeps the LAST part of the stretched reversed source).
  - **v8ui**: `audition_info` starts a run (line at the start, right end when reversed); `play_pos <ms>` moves the line (redraw only when the fraction changed by 0.0004 or more) and, at `endMs`, ends the run with the line left where it stopped; `play_done` ends it too, but is ignored for `PLAY_DONE_GUARD_MS = 150` after a start (the halt bang of the file it replaced). Positions arriving while no run is active are counted (`idlePosMsgs`) and ignored. `play_scan_dur` is still emitted by the node (informational, `route` index unchanged) but nothing uses it: there is no fallback animation, with DSP off nothing plays anyway.
  - **Esc** (`stopPlayback()`): the v8ui hides the line (`playheadPos = -1`), ends the run and sends `stop_playback` on outlet 1 -> `obj-route-uiout` -> `obj-trig-stop`: `0 8` to the `line~` envelope (8 ms fade), 12 ms later (`obj-del-stop`) `0` to `sfplay~` and `1` back to the envelope (unity for the next audition). Test hooks: `test_search <text>` (Filters search), `test_report.playhead` = `{pos, active, map, posMsgs, idlePosMsgs, stops, doneAfterMs, trace[[ms since start, file position ms, fraction]]}` and `mapNames` / `mapDurs`; `scripts/test_xo_live.js` sections j (slope / end / idle checks at Speed 100 %, 50 %, Reverse), k (Esc: meter and playhead) and l (toolbar Re-Seed with a search filter).
- `obj-sfplay` (`sfplay~ 2`) routes outlet 0 (Left/Mono) to both the Left multiplier and `obj-r-chan-sel` (`selector~ 2 1`) inlet 1, while outlet 1 (Stereo R) feeds `selector~ 2 1` inlet 2. `node.codebox` emits `play_mono 1` for 1-channel files and `play_mono 2` for stereo files, ensuring mono samples play on both channels.
- Raw exports (`[R]`, `[Drag Raw]`, and Sample Preview drag) point directly to the original sample file path on disk without copying, while Edited exports (`[E]`, `[Drag Edit]`) render a 2-channel stereo 16-bit PCM WAV file in `/tmp/xo_sample_map_exports/Edited/` with `Pitch`, `Speed`, `Grain`, `Mode`, `Trim/Dec`, `Reverse`, and `Gain` baked in, arming a compiled macOS Cocoa `NSDraggingSource` helper (`xo_drag_helper_v6_bin`).

### Themes, the Options section and the left toolbar (`devices/XO Sample Map.amxd` is ONE device with three switchable looks: LCD, Focus, Live)
- A slim 22 px **left toolbar** (`obj-panel-toolbar`, x = 3..25) is the leftmost, always visible column: four icon toggles (`live.text`, Toggle mode, built-in SVGs via `usepicture`/`remapsvgcolors`, tooltips in `annotation`). **Top:** Saved (`menu_closed.svg`), Filters (`bandpass.svg`), Playback (`sharkTooth.svg`) = the hidden-automatable `ShowSavedList` / `ShowFiltersSection` / `ShowPlaybackSection`. **Bottom (y = 145):** Options (`gear.svg`, from Max's `interfaces` folder like Cepstra's lock icons; tooltip "Options") = `ShowOptions` (default closed).
- **Options section** (`XO_OPTIONS_W` 190 px, `obj-panel-options`): a section like the others, sliding in immediately right of the toolbar (the map panel and everything after it move right, the device width grows by 190 + 6). Rows: the `OPTIONS` header, **Theme** (`obj-lbl-theme` y 30, `obj-theme-tab` `live.tab` y 44 x 70 x 18, parameter `Theme`, enum `LCD` / `Focus`, default LCD, full labels) with **Zoom Speed** beside it on the same row (`obj-lbl-zoom` `ZOOM` label x +80 y 30 w 48, click-through; `obj-zoom-speed`, ONE `live.numbox` x +80 y 44 x 48 x 18 for both themes, no mirror: `THEME_STYLE` gives LCD `appearance 4` amber and Focus slate / violet focus (both `appearance 4`: appearance 0 makes Live clamp the height to 14 px); parameter `Zoom Speed`, short `Zoom Spd`, float 0.25..4, default 1, `parameter_exponent 2.32` so 1.0x sits mid-range, custom unit `%.2fx` ("1.00x"), `parameter_invisible 1` = Stored Only: saved with the Set and presets, not automatable / not in the LOM; `prepend set_zoomspeed` -> v8ui, also on load; test hook `test_zoomspeed <x>`, `test_report.zoomSpeed`; 1.0x = exponent 0.96 per wheel unit, i.e. the former 2.0x (earlier bases: 0.3, then 0.48)) and **Map Samples** on the same row (`obj-lbl-map` `SAMPLES` label x +132 y 30 w 52, click-through; `obj-map-samples`, ONE `live.numbox` x +132 y 44 x 52 x 18, styled by `THEME_STYLE` like Zoom Speed; parameter `Map Samples`, short `Map Smpl`, float (`parameter_type 0`, `parameter_unitstyle 0` = Int display, because Live's native Int stops at 255) 100..5000, default 1500, `parameter_invisible 1` = stored only, appended LAST after Zoom Speed; `prepend set_mapsamples` -> v8ui (rounds / clamps, updates the Re-Seed tooltips, sends `map_samples <n>` to the node engine; re-sent on `minimap_done` and `apply_layout`, so a node that starts later still gets it); node `setMapSamples` stores `engineState.maxDisplaySamples` and re-picks the map (`rebuildActiveMapSubset`, same seed, unfiltered) after `MAP_SAMPLES_DEBOUNCE_MS` 300 ms of quiet (dragging does not re-seed per step); the selected sample stays selected when it is still on the new map (matched by path); the initial map, Re-Seed, filtered Re-Seed and folder changes all use the value; test hook `test_mapsamples <n>`, `test_report.mapSamples` / `reseedTitle`. The v8ui paints the dots with one path and one fill per colour (5000 points: 4.5 ms of JS per paint in the Node mock; the real mgraphics cost was not measured) and **Searches** (`obj-lbl-searches` y 68; `obj-searches-cat` `live.menu` picker y 80 x 178 x 15; `obj-searches-terms` multi-line `textedit` y 97 x 178 x 40; `obj-searches-reset` `live.text` button and `obj-searches-folders` `live.text` toggle at y 141, 44 and 100 px wide), see "Category search by words" below. `ShowOptions` and `Theme` were **appended** as parameters 18 / 19, the hidden picker parameter `Searches Category` (`parameter_invisible 2`, long name `Searches Category`, short `Search Cat`, enum = the 8 category names, default Kick) next, `Zoom Speed` and `Map Samples` as the last ones (every existing long name, range, default and order is unchanged). Both themes style every new box through `THEME_STYLE` (LCD: amber mono on glass; Focus: violet accents). The column is full down to y = 157: more options need a wider Options section (update `computeXoLayout`, `layout_boxes`, `obj-expr-width` and the tests).
- **Themes** (`src/xo_layouts/default.js`): `computeXoLayout(theme, saved, filters, playback, options)` (theme = `"LCD"` | `"Focus"` | `"Live"` or its index; a new instance starts in Live) is the single authority on where every themed box sits and whether it is shown. The mini-map v8ui applies it (`applyXoLayout`: `getnamed` + `presentation_rect` / `hidden`) on `set_show_*`, `set_theme` and `apply_layout` (device load); the old per-section `prepend hidden` chains were removed (a second authority raced with it). The device width comes from the patcher: `obj-toggle-*` / `obj-theme-tab` -> `pak 1 0 0 0 2` (saved filters playback options theme; the initial values = the new-instance default, see below) -> `obj-expr-width` (`expr 278 + $i1 * 136 + $i4 * 196 + $i2 * 214 + $i3 * (170 - 12 * ($i5 > 0))` (no `if()`: a comma inside an `expr` box must be escaped in Max, which is what broke the first version in Live)) -> `setwidth`; tests prove it equals the layout for 2 themes x 16 section combinations.
  - **The map pane is `XO_MAP_PANE_W` 236 px (+ Saved 136) and 154 px high in BOTH themes**; only Filters / Playback change: **LCD** (amber `live.numbox` readouts, `appearance 4`, in a labelled matrix, LCD buttons `RESEED` / `CLR` / `FWD`, dark bezels) Filters 208 / Playback 164 px, all sections 798 px wide (+196 with Options = 994); **Focus** (E's slim columns: small `live.dial` grids, coloured edge ticks, `Re-Seed` / `Clr` / `Reverse`) Filters 134 / Playback 152 px, all sections 712 px (+196 = 908). Closed it is 278 px in both. **A NEW instance starts with the toolbar, map and Saved list only (`DEFAULT_SECTIONS = (1, 0, 0, 0)` in `scripts/xo_layouts/default.py`): 414 px, Filters / Playback / Options closed.** The build sets `parameter_initial` of `ShowFiltersSection` / `ShowPlaybackSection` to 0 (existing Live Sets keep what they saved), the `pak` initial values, the v8ui's starting `showFiltersSection` / `showPlaybackSection` flags, and the saved presentation state (hidden flags, rects, `devicewidth` / `openrect` 414)..
  - **Theme-specific boxes** are separate presentation boxes shown / hidden by the layout (`themeOnly` in the layout result): LCD cells (`obj-lcd-f-cell-*`, `obj-lcd-p-cell-*`, title / search cells), LCD labels and the `>` prompt, Focus labels (`obj-focus-f-lbl-*`, `obj-focus-p-lbl-*`) and the Focus ticks (`obj-focus-tick-filters` / `-playback`). The saved state is **the Live theme (`DEFAULT_THEME`, Theme parameter initial 2), `DEFAULT_SECTIONS` (Saved only)**: the build applies the Live style with its `@live_` colour tokens resolved to the default-dark fallbacks (`resolve_style`, values parsed from `XO_LIVE_FALLBACK` in `src/xo_palette.js`) and the `hidden` flags statically; the v8uis start on the Live palette (`xoThemeIdx` / `xoPaletteSet("Live")` set by the build) and apply the real `max.getcolor` colours at load. Existing Sets keep the Theme index they saved (a new instance gets Live, the 414 px width is the same in every theme).
  - **Colours / fonts / texts per theme** are `THEME_STYLE` in `scripts/xo_layouts/default.py` (`{theme: {box id: {attribute: value}}}`), injected into the v8ui as `XO_THEME_STYLE` and applied with `setattr` when the theme changes and on load (`applyXoThemeStyle`): section panels, toolbar toggles, headers, the Reseed / Clr / Reverse buttons (the texts too), the search box, the Theme tab and the LCD numbox colours (`lcdcolor` ...).
- **Live theme** (third `Theme` enum value `Live`, appended so saved sets keep their index; `THEMES = ["LCD", "Focus", "Live"]`, `parameter_mmax 2`): Ableton's own device look in the user's Live theme colours (section 22 "Ableton-native look", Harmonic Split as the reference).
  - **Geometry** = Focus's (same map pane 236 px, same section packing; Playback 152 px), so `obj-expr-width` only needs `($i5 > 0)` for the Playback width. **Filters is 208 px in all three themes** (Focus was 134 px with two rows of dials, which put each first-row dial value 1 px above the next row's label): in Focus / Live it is ONE row of four 48 x 37 dials (x 6 / 55 / 104 / 153, y 50, labels 10 px above) under a 150 px search field, with a 196 x 68 folder list below (y 92); Re-Seed sits at the right end of the title row. Focus's all-sections width is 786 px (was 712). `only` tags of `computeXoLayout` (`xoOnlyOk`): `""` all themes, `"LCD"` / `"Focus"` / `"Live"` one theme, `"Dials"` = Focus + Live (the live.dial mirrors and the small Focus labels). Live-only boxes: four 2 px `live.line` hairlines (`obj-rule-filters` / `-playback` / `-options` / `-searches`, drawn right of the section title, click-through; a 1 px box draws nothing); the Focus edge ticks are Focus only. Titles sit at x + 4 in Filters / Playback (Options header at y 6). Options row: Theme tab is 86 px wide (three cells), Zoom 42, Samples 43 px.
  - **Look**: sections are flat `live_desktop` panels (rounded 3, no frame; the device body between them is Live's own), titles `live_lcd_title` Ableton Sans Medium 9.5 px in title case (the `text` of the header / label comments is part of every theme's style: upper case in LCD / Focus), dial and field labels `live_control_fg` (9 / 8.5 px), values (Zoom, Samples) borderless `live_lcd_control_fg_alt` LCD numboxes, the search / terms fields `live_lcd_bg` with a `live_lcd_frame` border, menu / tab / buttons `live_control_bg` with `live_control_fg` text and the one accent `live_control_selection` (selected Theme cell, toggles on; Reverse / Match folders are outlined accent toggles like Reverb's Lo Cut, Re-Seed / Clr / Reset quiet buttons), toolbar icons `live_control_fg` / on = accent. The dials are the existing hidden mirror `live.dial`s; their arc colour (`activedialcolor`, the only dial colour attribute the mirrors carry) is `live_lcd_control_fg_alt` in Live and each dial's own coloured arc in Focus (restored from the style table; the table entries are added by `customize`). Buttons (Re-Seed, Clr, Reset, Reverse, Match folders) use `live_control_bg` fill, `live_control_fg` text (`activetextcolor` and `textcolor`), `live_lcd_frame` border; only an ON toggle takes the accent (`live_control_selection` fill, `live_lcd_bg` text). Typed text in the search and terms fields is `live_control_fg`.
  - **Runtime colours**: `THEME_STYLE.Live` holds colour TOKENS `"@live_<name>"` (literal lists such as alpha 0 pass through). `applyXoThemeStyle` resolves them with `xoLiveColor(name)` (`max.getcolor`, Live-default-dark fallback `XO_LIVE_FALLBACK` in `src/xo_palette.js`, measured values, also what node tests / the preview use) and applies them with `setattr` when the theme is selected and on load; setattr replaces a saved themecolor binding, so nothing relies on bindings. Only attribute families LCD / Focus also use are set (no leftovers when switching back; each theme's own table restores its attributes, `tests/test_xo_map.js` walks LCD -> Live -> Focus -> Live). **Theme change in Live**: while the Live palette is active (`xoPaletteSet("Live")`, from `set_theme` in both v8uis) a 1 s `Task` (`xoLiveWatch`, one per v8ui, stopped when another theme is selected) fingerprints 10 theme colours (`xoLiveThemeKey`); on a change it re-reads the palette cache and calls `xoLiveThemeChanged()` (map v8ui: clears `xoStyledTheme`, `applyXoLayout()` re-applies the style with the new colours, redraw; folder list: redraw). No polling in LCD / Focus.
  - **Live palette** (`XO_PALETTES.Live`, `xoLiveMap` / `xoLiveCat`): neutrals map by luminance onto a ramp `live_lcd_bg` (near-black on dark themes, light on light ones) .. text colour (`live_lcd_title` pushed 30 % away from the background, forced to 0.85 / 0.15 grey when the contrast is under 0.35), alpha kept; saturated colours go by hue: blue / cyan = `live_control_selection` (the accent: rings, selection, links), amber = `live_lcd_control_fg`, red / pink = `live_alert`, green = `live_display_line_two`. Chrome of the map / Saved / folder-list v8uis goes through `xoRole(role, r, g, b, a)` (titles `live_lcd_title`, mini-button fill a lifted `live_lcd_bg`, button text `live_control_fg`, the folder list frame `live_lcd_frame`; other palettes paint the given colour as before) and `xoTitleText(upper, mixed)` ("Saved (2)" / "Folders (1/1)" in Live); the accent stays for selection rings and the selected row. Category colours are fixed hues (kick 0, snare 45, hihat 90, perc 140, shaker 185, cymbal 225, voice 275, clap 320 degrees, saturation 0.6, lightness 0.62 on a dark LCD / 0.40 on a light one with a small per-category offset); `ALL` = the text colour, `none` = a quiet neutral. Tested on a dark and a light mock theme: pairwise RGB distance > 0.18, contrast with the LCD background > 0.25.
  - **Checked**: layout 3 themes x 16 combinations, expr width, style application / restore / theme-change re-apply with a mocked `max.getcolor` and `Task` (`tests/test_xo_map.js`), and in Live (`scripts/test_xo_live.js` b: all three themes x 16 combinations; the Live theme's panel, search field, Reverse (on), title and Theme-tab colours equal `max.getcolor` read in the same report). **Not verified in Live**: a real Live theme switch (the VM's Live only has the default dark theme, and the Preferences > Look / Feel colour theme was not changed), so the light-theme look and the 1 s watcher firing are only covered by the mocks; `setattr("text", ...)` on the `live.comment` titles is checked only through the Filters title in the Live test.
- **Parameter / mirror pairs** (Max cannot change `maxclass` at runtime and a Live parameter is one object): each of the 10 value controls (`MaxLen`, `Tone`, `Tonal`, `Drums`, `Pitch`, `Speed`, `Grain`, `Trim/Dec`, `Mode`, `Gain`) has ONE parameter object, `live.numbox` (the former `live.dial` ids `obj-maxlen-dial` ... are unchanged; automation, mapping, presets and every `prepend set_*` wire stay on it), shown in LCD, plus a **`live.dial` mirror** (`<id>-mir`, `showname 0`; labels are separate comments) shown in Focus. The mirror is a real but hidden parameter (`parameter_enable 1`, `parameter_invisible 2`: not automatable, not stored, not listed in Live) whose `parameter_*` attributes (type, mmin / mmax, unitstyle, exponent, enum, steps, initial) are copied from its source in `_mirror()` (`scripts/xo_layouts/default.py`), so they cannot drift, with its own unique names (`<LongName>View`, `<ShortName>~`). **Why:** a `live.dial` with `parameter_enable 0` ignores every `parameter_*` attribute and falls back to a 0..127 range with no units or enum text (Focus showed no value text, a wrong arc position and no Mode name in Live). `scripts/test_xo_live.js` b2 reads `_parameter_range` / `_unitstyle` / `_type` / `_exponent` / `_steps` of each mirror through the `test_report` hook and compares them with the source object, then checks value and display for min / quarter / mid / max, both directions. Sync without a loop: parameter outlet -> `obj-prep-mset-<name>` (`prepend set`) -> mirror (`set` is silent); mirror outlet -> parameter object (which outputs to the engine and re-sets the mirror silently). `Grain`'s dimming (`active`) is set on both. The one exception is `Grain`: while dimmed (`active 0`, Mode Cmplx) the parameter does not output and neither mirror nor parameter take `set`, so the v8ui copies the parameter's value into the mirror when the dimming starts (`applyGrainDialState`); a LOM / automation write to a dimmed Grain only shows in Focus after the next Mode change. **Limit:** in Focus the visible widget is the mirror, so Live's click-to-map needs the LCD theme (or the device's parameter list / automation chooser, which always has the parameter).
- **Palette** (`src/xo_palette.js`, prepended to the mini-map and folder-list v8uis): every colour they paint goes through `xoSrc(r, g, b, a)`; `XO_PALETTES` (`default`, `Focus` = identity, `LCD` = amber monochrome: luminance mapped onto one amber ramp, alpha kept, plus distinct warm tones per sample category via `xoPaletteCat` / `xoSrcRaw`). `set_theme <index|name>` picks it in both v8uis (`obj-theme-tab` -> `prepend set_theme` -> map v8ui + folder list; the built scripts start on the LCD palette).
- **Category search by words** (`src/xo_search.js`; matcher shared by the node engine and the v8ui, tests `tests/test_xo_search.js` + the last block of `tests/test_xo_map.js`, `tsconfig.xo-search.json`): the toolbar category buttons select samples by words in their NAME and FOLDERS, never by the audio classifier or the map position.
  - **Categories** (order = toolbar order = first-match colour priority; each has a distinct colour in both palettes, LCD ones in `XO_PALETTES.LCD.cats`, pairwise RGB distance > 0.18, `none` = neutral for no match): `Kick` (kick, bd, kik, bassdrum), `Snare` (snare, rim, sd, snr), `Hi-Hat` (hihat, hi hat, hi-hat, hh, hat), `Clap` (clap, snap), `Perc` (tom, perc, conga, bongo, cowbell, clave, woodblock, block, triangle, agogo, djembe, cajon, tabla, timbale, darbuka), `Shaker` (shaker, tamb, tambourine, cabasa), `Cymbal` (crash, cymbal, ride, splash, china, cym), `Voice` (vox, voice, vocal, chant, choir, adlib). Tom and FX are gone (Tom is part of Perc); the old classifier categories (`kick snare hihat clap tom rim cymbal shaker perc fx`) still drive only the map layout and the stratified map subset.
  - **Text searched**: the file name without extension plus (Match folders, default on) the folder names BELOW the indexed root folder (`xoRelFolders`; the root's own name and its parents never match, so `.../Splice/...` or `Core Library/Samples/One Shots/Drums` above the root do nothing).
  - **Tokens**: camelCase and digit boundaries split (`ClosedHat` -> closed hat, `Kick808` -> kick 808, `HHClosed` -> hh closed), then everything that is not a letter or digit separates; lower case. A term is tokenised the same way and matches when its tokens, concatenated, equal a run of 1..4 adjacent tokens concatenated, the LAST token of the run optionally plural (`s` / `es`). So `kick` matches `Kicks`, `hihat` matches `Hi Hat` / `hi-hat` / `HiHat`, `hi hat` / `hi-hat` match `hihat`, `bassdrum` matches `Bass Drum`; `tom` never matches `custom` / `bottom`, `hat` never `chat` / `what`, `ride` never `pride` / `override`, `rim` never `prime` / `trim`.
  - **Membership**: a sample belongs to EVERY category whose terms match (a bit mask, bit i = category i; `Rim Click Perc` is Snare and Perc). A category button shows its members; All shows everything; it is ANDed with the search box, MaxLen, Tone, Tonal, Drums and the folder switches as before. Dots are coloured by the FIRST matching category (Kick > Snare > Hi-Hat > Clap > Perc > Shaker > Cymbal > Voice), neutral for none; the selected-sample badge and saved rows use the same. Prev / Next Similar, Random and Re-Seed with filters use the same membership (`isSamplePassingFilters`).
  - **Speed**: the node tokenises each sample once (`getSampleMask`: `s.skeys` = run keys of the name and of the folders, cached on the sample object) and compiles the terms into one `Map key -> category mask` (`xoBuildSearchIndex`); a sample's mask is a few Map lookups, recomputed lazily when `engineState.searchVer` changes (terms or Match folders). 20k synthetic names: tokenising 45 ms once, re-matching all after a terms edit about 9 ms. The 104-byte binary cache is unchanged (the classifier category is still stored in it).
  - **Node <-> v8ui**: the node sends each map point's category as ONE number in the existing `minimap_batch` category field, `mask * 16 + first category (15 = none)` (the v8ui decodes `pt.mask` / `pt.catIdx`; a plain index 0..7 still works), `selected_info` carries the first category id (`none`) and index (`-1`), and the saved list the same index. The v8ui filters with `pt.mask`, so both sides agree by construction (tested). A terms change makes the node re-send the points WITHOUT `minimap_clear` (selection stays) plus `minimap_done`; the v8ui answers every `minimap_done` and `apply_layout` with `search_config` (the node ignores an unchanged config), so a node that started after the v8ui still gets the saved terms.
  - **Options > Searches**: the picker chooses the category (`prepend set_terms_cat`), the text field shows that category's terms comma separated (`textedit`, `outputmode 1` = one symbol so commas survive, `keymode 1` Enter outputs, `tabmode 1`; `route text` -> `prepend set_terms_text`), `Reset` (`set_terms_reset`) restores only the selected category's defaults, `Match folders` (`set_match_folders`). A `textedit` has no focus-lost output, so the v8ui banges it (`searchesCommitField`, bang outputs the typed text) when the picker changes, Match folders changes, the Options section closes or any click lands on the map: an uncommitted edit lands on ITS category (`searchesTextTarget`). Edits apply at once (`search_config` -> node re-match -> re-colour / re-filter).
  - **Storage**: the v8ui owns the terms and Match folders and saves them in the existing `XOStateData` blob, **version 3**: after the saved entries `"XS", matchFolders, <8 encoded term lists>` (`xoEncodeTerms`: terms joined by `|`, spaces as `~`, empty = `-`, so each element is one token with no space or comma). Versions 1 / 2 (and a short v3 tail) load with the default terms and Match folders on; the saved category index of v1 / v2 is mapped to the new set (tom -> Perc, rim -> Snare, perc -> Perc, shaker -> Shaker, fx -> All). Match folders lives in the blob, not in a Live parameter, so it stays with the terms it belongs to; only the picker is a (hidden, UI-state) parameter, appended last.
  - **Test hook** (`receive #0-xo-test`): `test_toolbar <id>` (a real click on the button), `test_terms_cat i`, `test_terms_text <terms>`, `test_field_text <terms>` (through the real textedit chain), `test_terms_reset`, `test_match_folders v`, `test_blob`; `test_report` adds `activeCategory`, `searches` (terms, matchFolders, field text) and `cats` (per-category counts, visible count and names). `scripts/test_xo_live.js` check `h` drives them in Live.
- **Layout files** (`scripts/xo_layouts/default.py` + `src/xo_layouts/default.js`, contract in `scripts/xo_layouts/README.md`): there are no layout variants; the earlier look experiments A..E were removed. Build: `python3 scripts/build_xo_map.py` (`--all` is accepted and does the same). Tests: `node tests/test_xo_map.js` (themes, mirrors, style, palette, 2 x 16 layouts, the v8ui with a fake patcher; it also runs `tests/test_xo_layout.js`, which compares `layout_boxes` in `default.py` with the JS layout for every combination and checks the built device's structure). Live: `scripts/vm_xo.sh run`.
