## 5. Architecture of `devices/Tiny Space.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`292 x 169` px — `devices/Tiny Space.amxd`)
- **Left Column (`x = 6..62`)**: `PreDly` numbox + `HP` & `LP` dials.
- **Center Column (`x = 66..226`)**: Top header (`tinyspace`, `Seed` 0–1000 numbox using `parameter_type: 0, parameter_unitstyle: 0`, and `Character` 8-model dropdown `live.menu`: `COMB`, `HYBRID`, `ROOM`, `PLATE`, `TANK`, `BOX`, `SPRING`, `BOUNCE`), center `152 x 74` `v8ui` overlaid L+R string screen (`obj-viz`), and bottom 3 dials (`Size`, `Fbk`, `XFeed`).
- **Right Column (`x = 230..286`)**: `Damp` & `Mod` numboxes + `Width` & `Mix` dials.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/tinyspace_core.genexpr` — FDN: predelay → HP/LP → seeded allpasses → 20 delay lines (10 L / 10 R) with linear interpolation, per-line damping, soft clip, an orthogonal per-model mix (identity, sum-preserving rotation, Householder, Householder-cycle, sign-flipping ring, Householder wall-swap, spring lattice, or reversal), a unitary crossfeed rotation → width (M/S) → equal-power dry/wet.
- Audio is `in1`/`in2`. Knobs are Gen `Param`s (`size`, `damp`, `width`, `wet`, `fbk`, `predly`, `modamt`, `xfeed`, `hp`, `lp`, `duck`), sent with `prepend`.
- `Buffer cfg("tsconfig")` reads the 70-float seed config. `Buffer meters("tsmeters")` gets one envelope per line every 32 samples. Loadbang binds them with `cfg #0-tsconfig, meters #0-tsmeters` (the GenExpr variable, with no `set` prefix). An unbound buffer peeks as zeros, every delay line stays off, and Mix at 100% is silent.
- Percent knobs are scaled by `/ 100.` before they hit gen. Feedback uses a delay-style RT60 curve (`feedbackRt60`): about 0.7 s at 50%, about 2.7 s at 75%, and about 27.5 s at 100% (60 dB down). Every room model shares that tail because the mix is orthogonal and the all-ones mode has eigenvalue magnitude 1. The per-loop coefficient is higher on short lines so a small Size rings about as long as a long one, and it stays under 1. Feedback at 0 writes no recirculation. Delay taps are whole samples, and Damp at 0 is a bypass (`pole = 0`), so short combs stay metallic. Above 0, Damp is a per-pass one-pole on every delay tap, including the first reflection: about 5 kHz at 20%, about 1.8 kHz at 50%, and a few hundred hertz at 100% on an 8 ms line. Short lines use a higher cutoff so a 1 ms comb is not filtered much harder than a long room line. Every room model shares that filter. Crossfeed is a rotation (`xfeed * pi/2`), so a partial XFeed does not burn energy.

### Seed → config
- `src/tinyspace_config.js` (mulberry32, `Seed` 0–1000) is prepended into the v8ui script and required by `tests/test_tinyspace.js`. Lower seeds use fewer active delay lines (`4` at Seed `0`), scaling monotonically up to the `20`-line cap (`10 L + 10 R`) as Seed approaches `1000`.
- The `Seed` numbox calls `set_seed`. The UI pokes `buffer~ #0-tsconfig`. User knobs are not rewritten.
- A later seed change bangs a `line` that ramps Gen `duck` from 0 to 1 over 160 ms so the old tail does not thump.
- The seed is a Live parameter, so sets and presets recall it without a separate blob.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_tinyspace.py && node tests/test_tinyspace.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Tiny Space.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render): `python3 scripts/run_tinyspace_audio_tests.py`

## 6. Architecture of `devices/Sand.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`296 x 169` px — Compact Single-Column Edition)
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..206`)**:
  - Top header (`y = 7..22`): Title (`SAND`) + `Order` (−1..15) and `Stages` (1..4) numboxes.
  - Graphic HUD Control Strip (`y = 26..42`): Automatable `Amount` value box (`0..140 dB`), `Freq` value box (`20 Hz..18 kHz`), and `Out` selection slider/box (`Off` / `Hard` / `Limit`, `appearance: 1`).
  - Audio-Reactive XY Scope (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 46, w = 192, h = 114`): Draws stereo wet grit trace, dry input ghost silhouette, audio-reactive sand grain particles, compander transfer curve inset, and dual `G` (pre-mix wet grit) / `O` (post-mix output) meters. Dragging vertically adjusts `Amount`; dragging horizontally adjusts `Freq` (synced bidirectionally with `obj-amount` and `obj-freq`).
- **Right Panel (`x = 210..290` — Single Visual Column)**:
  - `Filt` (`highcut.svg` / `bandpass.svg` / `lowcut.svg` filter curve icons, `usepicture: 1`) and `Place` (`Pre` / `Diff` / `Post`) selector rows at the top.
  - `Soft` (`%`) and `Debrick` (`%`) horizontal value bars (`live.numbox`, `appearance: 2`) in the middle, with the `Mix` (`%`) radial dial at the bottom.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/sand_core.genexpr` — per channel: optional pre-filter, derivative order or a leaky integrator, optional mid filter, 1–4 compander stages, optional post filter, equal-power dry/wet mix, then the output stage.
- Each differentiator peaks at unity at Nyquist and is clipped, so order 15 cannot amplify rounding error into a square wave. Amount 0 is unity gain on order 0. Amount 140 dB is about 10^7 of extra gain on quiet samples, then a soft or hard knee.
- `Out` is bypass (`Off`), hard clip at ±1 (`Hard`), or a fast limiter with a ceiling near −1 dB (`Limit`). The limiter history always runs.
- `Buffer wave("sandwave")` gets stereo output, dry input, wet grit, and peak envelopes every 32 samples. Loadbang binds it with `wave #0-sandwave`. Audio does not depend on the buffer.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_sand.py && node tests/test_sand.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Sand.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render): `python3 scripts/run_sand_audio_tests.py`

## 7. Architecture of `devices/Reciprocator.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`448 x 169` px — 3-Stage Signal-Flow Modules)
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..176`)**:
  - Top bar (`y = 7..23`): Title (`Reciprocator`).
  - Interactive Scrolling Waveform + Envelope & Gate HUD (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 28, w = 162, h = 132`): Draws the input waveform silhouette, detected envelope (`ENV` cyan), flattened reciprocal output (`OUT` amber), draggable `Gate` and `Ceiling` threshold lines, and a right-hand `1/E` gain + `OPEN`/`MUTE` meter bar.
- **Stage 1 — `1. DETECT` (`x = 180..272`, Cyan-bordered panel)**:
  - `Attack` and `Release` dials on top, with stacked `HP` (`SC HP`), `LP` (`SC LP`), `RMS` (`Detect`), and `Look` (`Lookahead`) numboxes below.
- **Stage 2 — `2. 1 / ENV` (`x = 276..356`, Coral-bordered panel)**:
  - Stacked `Amount` (`0..150%`) and `Gate` (`-72..-6 dB`) dials, plus the **`Rev Gate`** (`Reverse Gate`, `live.text`) toggle at the bottom (inverts the gate so loud hits above `Gate` are muted and only quiet signals below `Gate` pass through).
- **Stage 3 — `3. OUTPUT` (`x = 360..442`, Amber-bordered panel)**:
  - `Output Mode` selection box (`live.menu`: `Soft Clip`, `Hard Clip`, `Limiter`) placed directly above the `Ceiling` dial (`-18..0 dB`), followed by the `Mix` dial (`0..100%`).

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/reciprocator_core.genexpr` — Lookahead delay (`0..10 ms`, shared by wet VCA and dry path for phase alignment), detector HP/LP conditioning, blendable Peak/RMS envelope follower (`0.05..250 ms` attack, `0.5..1500 ms` release), normal & reverse smooth cubic noise gate around `Gate` (`-72..-6 dB`, `revgate`), reciprocal gain (`pow(env, -amount)` up to `150%` inverted dynamics), and `Ceiling` (`-18..0 dB`) with selectable `Soft Clip` (`tanh`), `Hard Clip`, or fast peak `Limiter` (`outmode` `0`, `1`, `2`).
- `Buffer scope("recipscope")` writes peak-held input, envelope, gate open state, normalized dB gain, and output peaks every 64 samples. Bound on `loadbang` via `scope #0-recipscope`.

### Known rough edges
- **`Soft Clip` (`outmode 0`) is a real `tanh` now.** Before the nested-return fix every output mode fell through to the Limiter line, so old audio-test expectations and existing Live Sets sound different: Soft/Hard now clip instead of limit. The `audiorate` case (Soft Clip, Ceiling -1 dB) peaks near 0.68, not at the ceiling.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_reciprocator.py && node tests/test_reciprocator.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Reciprocator.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render): `python3 scripts/run_reciprocator_audio_tests.py`

## 9. Architecture of `devices/Percipitator.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`408 x 169` px — Horizontal Value-Bar Stack)
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..218`, `w = 212`)**:
  - Top bar (`y = 7..22`): Title (`Percipitator`).
  - Visualization Canvas (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 26, w = 204, h = 134`):
    - **Full-Width Partials Viewer (`y = 20..104`)**: 16-band Spectral Precipitation Waterfall with peak-hold caps, balanced volume-independent falling droplets rendered on top of the bars, an adjustable `G -42dB` Gate readout pill, and a vertically draggable `Gate` threshold line.
    - **Real-Time Parameter Visual Feedback**:
      - `Pitch`, `Shift`, `Strch`: Displace the active colored partial bars horizontally while leaving faint slate ghost bars at the unshifted input band positions.
      - `Sieve`: Shades muted band lanes dark in the background grid with a bottom coral tick.
      - `Decay`, `Blur`, `Atk`, `Vol`: `Decay` controls falling droplet comet-tail length and peak-cap fall speed; `Blur` renders a soft horizontal bloom halo around active bars; `Atk` rounds bar top corners; `Vol` scales bar height (while keeping droplets visible even at `-36 dB`).
    - **Top-Right Wave Pill (`x = 130..198, y = 2..18`)**: Live single-cycle preview of the morphed/warped `Sine -> Triangle -> Saw -> Square` wave (drag vertically for `Wave`, horizontally for `Warp`).
    - **Bottom Strip (`y = 108..130`)**: Interactive 12-Note Scale Quantizer & Sidechain MIDI keyboard (`C..B`). Saved in Live Sets via `ScaleMaskData` (`getvalueof` v2: `[2, curScaleMask, curGateDb]`, backward-compatible with v1 `[1, curScaleMask]`).
- **Right Panel (`x = 222..402`, `w = 180`) — Two Columns of Horizontal Value Bars (Zero Rotary Dials)**:
  - **Column 1 (`PITCH / ENV`, `x = 230..308`)**: `Pitch` (`-24..+24 st`, bipolar `appearance: 3`), `Shift` (`-2000..+2000 Hz`, bipolar `appearance: 3`), `Strch` (`50..200%`), `Snap` (`0..100%`), `Blur` (`0..100%`), `Decay` (`1..1500 ms`), and `Atk` (`0.2..100 ms`).
  - **Column 2 (`WAVE / TONE`, `x = 314..392`, with `10 px` right margin to panel edge at `402`)**: `Wave` (`WT Pos` `0..100%`, default `0%` Sine), scrollable `Mode` (`Bend`, `Asym`, `Sync`, `Fold`, `unitstyle: 9`), `Warp` (`0..100%`, default `50%`, bipolar `appearance: 3`), scrollable `Sieve` (`All`, `Odd`, `Even`, `Sprs`, `Rise`, `unitstyle: 9`), `Tilt` (`-100..+100%`, $\pm 36\text{ dB}$, bipolar `appearance: 3`), `Vol` (`Partials Vol` `-36..+18 dB`), and `Mix` (`0..100%`).
- **Sidechain MIDI Input (`midiin` -> `midiparse` -> `prepend midi_in` -> `obj-viz`)**: Incoming MIDI note-on/note-off messages dynamically build a 12-bit pitch-class mask (`computeMidiChordState`) from held notes, latching the last played chord and highlighting held notes in emerald on the bottom 12-note strip.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/percipitator_core.genexpr` — 16-band log-spaced Chamberlin SVF bandpass + zero-crossing pitch tracker + soft-knee precipitation gate (`gate`, `decay`, `attack`, `blur`, and `freeze` spectral hold) + Partial Sieve (`sieve`) + Spectral Tilt (`tilt`, `pow(2.0, t * pos * 6.0)` = $\pm 36\text{ dB}$) → 12-note scale quantizer (`scalemask`, `snap`, `pitch`, `stretch`, `fshift` Hz offset) → 16 4-frame `Sine -> Triangle -> Saw -> Square` wavetable oscillators (`buffer~ #0-percipwt`, `8192` samples) with `Bend`, `Asym`, `Sync`, and `Fold` phase warp modifiers → resynthesized partials volume (`resynthgain`) & equal-power dry/wet mix.
- Loadbang binds `wt #0-percipwt, scope #0-percipscope` and pokes the 4-frame `Sine -> Triangle -> Saw -> Square` wavetable into `buffer~ #0-percipwt`.

### Known rough edges
- **Freeze / silence sleep** (`band_track`) are verified by `run_percipitator_audio_tests.py` cases `freeze_hold` / `freeze_off` / `sleep_silent` / `sleep_wake` (`stepped` mode, tone cut by an event on `dev-gain`). The cases cannot observe the sleep branch directly; they only show the output stays quiet and the bands wake again.
- **`sleep_silent` is not exactly silent**: after the input stops, a slow low-band (40 Hz SVF) ring-down leaves a peak of about 0.014 (active is about 0.46) even at `Decay 1 ms`. The check allows `< 0.03`. Do not tighten it to near zero without changing the DSP.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_percipitator.py && node tests/test_percipitator.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Percipitator.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render): `python3 scripts/run_percipitator_audio_tests.py`

## 10. Architecture of `devices/Degrade.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`480 x 169` px — Compact Matrix Rack)
- **Left Panel (`x = 6..204`)**:
  - Top bar (`y = 7..22`): Title (`Degrade`) and `Output` gain numbox (`0.0 dB`).
  - Interactive Chain & Dual-Mode Visualizer (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 10, y = 26, w = 190, h = 136`):
    - **Top Reorderable 5-Stage Pill Bar (`y = 3..23`)**: Displays `[BIT] > [SR] > [COD] > [TELE] > [FFT]`. Drag any pill horizontally to reorder the DSP signal chain (`order_code`), or click its left power LED dot to toggle section enable (`0` CPU when disabled).
    - **Viewport (`y = 26..114`)**: Switches between **Technical Mode** (input silhouette vs. quantized staircase waveform + 12-band `SPEC` spectral hole analyzer) and **Abstract Mode** (fractured phase-space CRT glitch field).
    - **Bottom Bar (`y = 117..133`)**: Single geometric vector icon button to toggle Technical / Abstract mode alongside the live chain order readout (`TECH BIT>SR>COD>TEL>FFT`).
- **Right Panel (`x = 208..474`)**:
  - **Top 5-Column Dark LCD Micro-Matrix (`y = 6..94`)**:
    - `1.BIT` (`Wrap` `%`, `Dith` `%`), `2.SR` (`Jitter` `%`, `Mode` `S&H`/`Filt`), `3.COD` (`Echo` `%`, `Bird` `%`), `4.TEL` (`Codec` text slider: `uLaw`, `ALaw`, `ADPC`, `GSM`), and `5.FFT` (`Frame` `ms`, `Mode` text slider: `Den`, `Scr`, `Spl`, `Cmb`).
  - **Bottom 6 Hero Dials (`y = 104..150`)**: `Bits` (`1..16`, default `16`), `Rate` (`100..44100 Hz`, default `44100 Hz`), `Damage` (`0..100%`), `Line` (`0..100%`), `Glitch` (`0..100%`), and `Mix` (`0..100%`, default `50%`). All parameters default to transparent settings.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/degrade_core.genexpr` — Dynamic 5-slot router (`order_code`) executing only enabled sections (`if ((stId == k) * (<stage>_on > 0.5))`):
  - **Stage 0 (`BIT`)**: Mid-tread bit quantizer (`1..16` bits), signal-following envelope grit floor, integer overflow wrap (`wrap_amt`), and high-passed TPDF dither (`dither`).
  - **Stage 1 (`SR`)**: Sample-rate decimation (`100..44100 Hz`), chaotic clock jitter (`jitter`), and `S&H` vs reconstructed `Filt` mode.
  - **Stage 2 (`COD`)**: SpecOps `MP3ify` / `MAIM` style 12-band zero-crossing pitch-tracked sinusoidal MDCT resynthesizer with lateral psychoacoustic spectral holes, $x^{3/4}$ quantization, `72 ms` multi-tap lookahead block smear (`preecho`), staggered MDCT chirp windows + wide FFT-bin-hopping birdies (`birdies`), and extreme packet starvation at `70..100%` `Damage` (nearly silent at `100%` except for isolated sinusoidal birdie glitches).
  - **Stage 3 (`TEL`)**: Telephone bandpass constriction + voice sample clock + `uLaw`, `ALaw`, `ADPC` (IMA ADPCM slope overload), and `GSM` (RPE-LTP formant buzz + frame dropouts).
  - **Stage 4 (`FFT`)**: 6-band resonant SVF spectral glitch engine with 4 modes: `Den` (musical-noise spectral denoiser), `Scr` (click-free 6-band SSB quadrature matrix scramble, smooth even at `5 ms`), `Spl` (dual Hann-windowed dispersive phase splat), and `Cmb` (power-law spectral contrast sieve + pitch comb).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_degrade.py && node tests/test_degrade.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Degrade.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render): `python3 scripts/run_degrade_audio_tests.py`

## 11. Architecture of `devices/Multiband Split.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`268 x 169` px — Ableton-native look: see section 22 "Ableton-native look")
- **Zero Duplicate Title**: no device title (Live's header names it). Body panel `live_surface_bg`; two flat titled sections on `live_desktop` (`sc.make_section`: `Bands` `x = 4..264, y = 4..124`, `Crossover` `x = 4..264, y = 128..165`); the two section panels then the body panel are the last boxes. The face script is `mbsplit_config.js`, `spectral_theme.js`, the injected `GEN_DSP_TEXT` / `GEN_DSP_HASH`, `mbsplit_dsp.js` (deferred gen~) and `mbsplit_ui.js` (all listed in `tsconfig.mbsplit.json`); `scripts/build_mbsplit.py` uses the `spectral_common` theme builders. No hard-coded colours in the face: everything is `sptColor("live_...")` (band colours = muted cyan / pink / salmon / grey-orange from the theme, handles and active controls the one orange accent).
- **Canvas (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 8, y = 20, w = 252, h = 102`)**, drawn on the section colour:
  - **Top strip (`y = 1..14`)**: flat `Auto` pill (`x = 0..30`), `1` to `4` band pills (`1:Full` for 1 instance; `1:Low, 2:High`; `1:Low, 2:Mid, 3:High`; `Low`, `Lo-M`, `Hi-M`, `High` for 4), created automatically per detected linked instance (`computeDetectedBandCount()`), and the volume slider (`-inf` to `+18.0 dB`, right 66 px, `0 dB` tick, value in the LCD value colour, drag / double-click reset, synced with hidden `obj-ctrl-gain`). Active pills are orange with dark text (`live.text` look), off pills `live_control_bg` with light text, a 1 px muted band-colour meter at the bottom edge. Regular click solos a band; Shift-click (or the right edge of a pill) toggles bands into a multi-band pass mask (`BandMode = 5`, `CustomMask`).
  - **LCD (`y = 16..102`, `PLOT_X = 0, PLOT_Y = 16`)**: near-black `live_lcd_bg` with a faint log-frequency / dB grid, small grey axis labels (`100`, `1k`, `10k`), the 8192-point Hann FFT spectrogram (`MBSPLIT_FFT_SIZE = 8192`, 4096 bins, 248 columns): faint muted waterfall, grey input trace, per active band a faint fill and 1 px crest line, thin peak-hold; 1 px Linkwitz-Riley curves (active bright, inactive dim, white composite when several bands pass); crossover lines (1 px grey) with small hollow orange numbered handles `1` / `2` / `3` (draggable, double-click reset) and plain frequency labels in the value colour; the link badge (`getLinkBadgeRect()`, bottom right, text only with an orange dot when linked, click toggles `Link ...` / `Link`). Until the deferred gen~ runs the same badge reads `DSP loading...` (orange), or `DSP failed: <reason>` (alert colour), see "Work sharing and deferred DSP".
- **Crossover section controls (`y = 145..160`, native `live.*` bound to theme colours)**: `Slope` `live.tab` (`12` / `24` / `48`, `x = 8..58`), `Phase Mode` `live.text` toggle (`MIN` / `DYN`, `x = 61..87`), then `F1`, `F2`, `F3` label + borderless LCD value (`live.numbox` appearance 4, `x = 105 / 161 / 217`, `w = 41`). **Missing splits are greyed out.** Crossover `Fk` exists only with more than `k` bands (1 band: none; 2: `F1`; 3: `F1`, `F2`; 4: all). The v8ui sends only `ui_st_f<k> active 0|1` (through `route` straight into each numbox, only when the state changes: `emitCrossoverActive()`, re-sent after `loadbang`); Live dims inactive controls in its theme.

### Nested Rack ("Chain within a Chain") Scoping, Same-Chain (`Cmd+D`) Isolation & Cross-Instance Sync
- `parseRackContextFromPath(lomPath)` in `src/mbsplit_config.js` inspects `LiveAPI("this_device").unquotedpath` and extracts the **immediate enclosing `RackDevice` LOM path** (`parentRackPath`), **immediate enclosing `chains <N>` index** (`chainIndex`), and **device index within that chain** (`devices <D>` -> `deviceIndex`).
- **Nested Rack Scoping**: In `Auto` link mode, instances inside an outer Audio Effect Rack link exclusively with siblings in that outer rack and auto-create `1..4` bands matching the detected instances (`Chain 1..N -> Band 1..N`), while instances inside a nested sub-rack ("a chain within a chain") link exclusively with siblings inside that nested sub-rack.
- **Same-Chain (`Cmd+D`) Left-Most vs. Right-Hand Isolation**:
  - Within any chain `(parentRackPath, chainIndex)`, `isLeftMostInOwnChain()` and `getActiveGroupKey()` in `src/mbsplit_ui.js` compare alive splitters by `(deviceIndex, order, id)`.
  - Only the **left-most** splitter in each chain (`isLeftMostInOwnChain() === true`, e.g. `chains 0 devices 0`) joins `"rack_group::" + parentRackPath`.
  - Any splitter to the **right** in the same chain (`isLeftMostInOwnChain() === false`, such as a `Cmd+D` duplicate at `chains 0 devices 1` before it is grouped into a sub-rack) receives `"rack_group::isolated::" + INSTANCE_ID`, keeping it in its own empty `1`-band chain (`1:FULL`) with independent crossovers.
  - Reordering a right-hand splitter to `devices 0` or deleting the left-most splitter automatically promotes the new left-most splitter into `"rack_group::" + parentRackPath` and syncs the rack's `F1` / `F2` / `F3` / `Slope`.
- **Cross-Instance `LiveAPI("this_device")` Thread Safety & Allocation Reuse**:
  - In Max for Live (`v8ui`), calling `new LiveAPI(..., "this_device")` inside a cross-instance `Global` callback resolves `"this_device"` to the *caller's* patcher rather than the callee's patcher. Each instance therefore caches a single `selfLiveApi` bound on its own thread (`isOwnThread !== false`) and reuses `selfLiveApi.unquotedpath` during liveness and topology checks, preventing cross-instance path clobbering and main-thread `LiveAPI` allocation floods.
- Cross-instance synchronization uses `Global("mbsplit_shared_bus")` to link `F1` / `F2` / `F3`, `Slope`, `Phase Mode`, and auto-detected `NumBands` while keeping `BandMode`, `CustomMask`, and `Gain` (`-inf..+18 dB`) independent per chain.
- `SplitStateData` blob is version 3: `[3, f1, f2, f3, numBands, slope, bandMode, customMask, linkGroup, gainDb, showLinkStatus, phaseMode]` (v1 / v2 still load; they leave the phase mode alone).

### Work sharing and deferred DSP (2026-10-22; numbers measured in the VM, real Max NonRealTime, and Live 12.4.6)
Every instance of a rack sits first in its chain and sees the same input, outputs only its own band, and used to run the whole tree, the 16-band analyzer and its own 8192-point FFT. There is no STFT hop in this device (the crossover is sample synchronous), so the hop-clock problem of the Spectral Split suite does not apply here.
- **Gen prunes to the instance's bands** (`Param prune`, sent by the face: 1 when the link group has 2 or more alive instances, 0 for a lone instance, which keeps every band so the band meters show them all). `bandmask` decides which of `b1..b4` are computed; stage 1 serves both phase modes (`needLP1` = band 1, `needHP1` = bands 2..4), stages 2 / 3 only the all-IIR mode, with their LP / HP cascades and allpasses separately (`needLP2`, `needHP2`, `needAP2a`, `needLP3`, `needHP3`, `needAP3a`, `needAP3b`). The allpass phase compensation of a computed band is the same as in the full tree, so the rack still sums flat: `prune_*` audio cases null each pruned band against an unpruned twin to float exactness (the residual is exactly 0 for IIR LR2 / LR4 / LR8 with 4, 3, 2 bands and Dynamic Phase with 4 and 3 bands). In Dynamic Phase mode stages 2 / 3 are skipped altogether (only the FIR is used; the IIR warms up during the 4 ms fade back to IIR) and the FIR convolves only the kernels the bands need (`needF2` = bands 2, 3; `needF3` = bands 3, 4; band 1 needs none, band 4 one), kernels are redesigned only when needed. Filter coefficients (`tan`, `g / (1 + g)` and the two SVF damping sets of each stage) are cached and recomputed only when a smoothed crossover moves or the slope / rate changes. A band that is not needed holds stale filter state: a mask or `numbands` change cold-starts the new filters (an ordinary transient, the output switches band at that moment anyway).
- **Silence sleep**: input below 1e-5 for 16384 samples skips all filter and FIR work (output is exact zeros; ring pokes keep running); a wake-up is immediate (the quiet counter is a local, so the first loud sample is processed).
- **Trimmed telemetry**: the 16-band analyzer (scope slots `0..15`, 16 SVFs and 16 `tan` per sample) and slot `22` were never read by the face and are gone. Slots `28` (bands computed bits 0..3 + 16 when pruned), `29` (asleep) and `30` (ring on) are new; the face peeks 32 floats.
- **One FFT per link group**: the first alive instance in chain order (leader) runs the 8192-point FFT (`computeRawSpecColumns`) and publishes the 248 raw dB columns in `Global("mbsplit_shared_bus").spec[groupKey]` (`{owner, t, seq, cols, silent}`); the others copy them (`applyRawSpecColumns` keeps each face's own smoothing, peak hold and waterfall). A follower switches the gen's `ringon` off (the gen then writes no 8192-sample ring) and computes itself, with a 200 ms ring refill, whenever the shared frame is older than 400 ms (leader deleted, DSP not running: a leader without a running gen publishes no heartbeat). All instances are assumed to see the same input (first device of every chain); an instance behind other devices shows the leader's spectrum. Band meters: each gen only measures the bands it computes, the face shows the max over the group's `getBandPeaks()`.
- **Face polling**: the scope is read every 40 ms, the LOM / topology work (own and sibling path refresh, alive checks, peer lists) every `TOPO_INTERVAL_MS = 250` ms (was every 40 ms in every instance, O(K^2) LiveAPI reads), redraws only when a displayed value moved (spectrogram row, meter step, DSP state); a silent input stops redrawing after the waterfall has decayed (120 ticks).
- **Deferred DSP** (AGENTS 1d, `src/mbsplit_dsp.js`, copied from Reverb+): the device ships without its gen~; `live.thisdevice` sends `init_dsp`, the face writes the embedded `.gendsp` (`/tmp/mbsplit_core_<hash>.gendsp`), creates `gen~ @gen <file>` one Task tick later, wires `mb_genin` (`gate 1 1`, every gen-bound message goes into its data inlet) / `mb_plugin` / `mb_plugout`, and re-sends the buffer binding and all parameters (`emitDspState`) every 150 ms until the gen's beat counter (scope slot 23) moves. Instances create one at a time (`Global("mbsplit_dspq")`), a gen~ that never starts is rebuilt (first attempt 3.5 s, then 6 s, 2 retries). **Until it runs the device outputs silence** (no `plugin~` -> `plugout~` cord): a dry pass-through would make a rack of K chains K times as loud. Load trace: `Global("mbsplit_trace").text`.
- **Buffer isolation fix**: the scope buffer was `buffer~ #0-splitscope`; `#0` is not expanded in a top-level device in Live (AGENTS 15), so every instance shared one ring and header. It is `---mbsplitscope` now (`scope ---mbsplitscope` / `setbuffer ---mbsplitscope`); the harness keeps its own `#0-splitscope`.
- **Measured** (`scripts/measure_mbsplit.py`, VM; per instance in wall seconds per second of audio on the NonRealTime driver, 16 instances = 4 racks of 4 chains, harness floor about 0.001; before = the previous core, after = pruned rack instances):

  | case | before | after |
  |---|---|---|
  | LR4, noise input | 0.0032 | 0.0010 |
  | LR8, noise input | 0.0042 | 0.0019 |
  | Dynamic Phase, noise input | 0.0190 | 0.0125 |
  | LR4 / LR8 / Dynamic Phase, silent input | 0.0029 / 0.0046 / 0.0199 | 0.0010 / 0.0012 / 0.0010 |

  Gen compile did not change (0.92..1.07 s before, 0.95..1.06 s after; the noise of the method is about 0.1 s), so it is hidden instead: in Live (4 chains) the face is up at once, the first gen~ is running 1093 ms after creation, the other three 157..163 ms (cached compile), all four 2.4 s after the script loaded. Face scripts in Node (mock, 4 instances, per 40 ms round, without painting): 11.9 -> 3.2 ms and 16000 -> 2304 `LiveAPI` constructions per 500 rounds; silent input: 2000 -> 480 redraws per 500 rounds. In Live the leader ran 39 FFTs in 1.5 s and the three followers none (they copied 39 frames).
- **Not done / open**: Dynamic Phase band 3 still convolves two kernels (a combined `k3 - k2` kernel would halve it); a lone instance, or a rack whose instances are not the first device of their chain, runs the full tree / its own spectrogram; whether a hidden (unselected) face paints could not be detected, so every face still draws when its data changes.

### Dynamic Phase (`Phase Mode` = `DYN`, gen `dynphase 1`)
- **F1** stays the minimum-phase Linkwitz-Riley split (selected `Slope`). **F2 / F3** become complementary linear-phase FIR splits of the F1 high band: Kaiser (`beta 6`) windowed-sinc lowpass, symmetric half-length `D` taps, DC-normalized; `band2 = LP2 * hi`, `band3 = LP3 * hi - LP2 * hi`, `band4 = delay_D(hi) - LP3 * hi`, `band1 = delay_D(lo)` (`delay_D(x)` with 1 band). The sum is exactly the F1 allpass delayed by `D`, whatever the kernels are, so kernel redesigns (every 32 samples while F2 / F3 move) never break the flat sum.
- `D = 128 * round(sr / 48000)` clamped `128..512` (`128` at 44.1 / 48 kHz, about 2.7 ms; `256` at 96 kHz). F2 / F3 transition: about `sr / D` (375 Hz at 48 kHz) either side of the crossover, from -0.7 dB to below -60 dB, so slopes get gentle below about 300 Hz. That is why F1 stays IIR.
- Switching modes fades out for 4 ms, swaps, then fades in for 4 ms. The rings always run, so the new mode starts with valid history.
- **Latency reporting**: gen publishes `D` at `scope[26]` and the active mode at `scope[27]`. The v8ui calls `this.patcher.setattr("latency", phaseMode ? D : 0)` (`updateDeviceLatency()`). Live re-reads this at runtime: verified through the MCP bridge, where `latency_in_samples` followed the attribute immediately. Every linked instance carries the same phase mode, so all Rack chains report the same latency.
- JS mirror in `src/mbsplit_config.js`: `firLatencyForSampleRate`, `designFirHalfKernel`, `evalFirHalfKernel`, `computeFirLowpassMag` (raised-cosine display approximation, within 0.1 of the real kernel), and `createReferenceSplitterDsp(sr).processSampleDynamic`.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/mbsplit_core.genexpr` — Phase-compensated stereo Linkwitz-Riley crossover tree (`12 dB/oct` LR2, `24 dB/oct` LR4, `48 dB/oct` LR8) supporting `1..4` bands with matching allpass phase-compensation stages (`ap2_b1`, `ap3_b1`, `ap3_b2`) so parallel chains sum to a flat `0.000 dB` allpass unity response across all crossover frequencies, plus `-70..+18 dB` (`-inf..+18 dB`) output volume.
- `Buffer scope("splitscope")` (`8224` samples) writes telemetry every 64 samples (`16..19` band peaks, `20` / `21` input / output peak, `23` beat counter, `24` ring write position, `25` sample rate, `26` FIR latency, `27` dynamic phase active, `28..30` work-sharing state, see above) and, while `ringon` is 1, streams continuous mono input audio into an 8192-sample circular ring buffer (`32..8223`) for the 8192-block FFT spectrogram. Bound on `loadbang` via `scope ---mbsplitscope`. Params: `f1 f2 f3 numbands slope bandmask gain phase dynphase prune ringon`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_mbsplit.py && node tests/test_mbsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Multiband Split.amxd"
```
- `node tests/test_mbsplit.js` also checks the deferred-DSP structure (no gen in the file, `gate 1 1` hub with data-inlet cords only, `mb_plugin` / `mb_plugout`, `---` buffer names, embedded `.gendsp` = the core) and the sharing logic with four mock instances (prune flags, one leader FFT and three copies, stale-frame takeover with ring refill, lone instance, band-meter merge); `tests/test_mbsplit_paint_bounds.js` paints the DSP pill states too. It runs both the offline VM unit tests and `runLiveMcpRegressionSuite()`. On the host, set `MBSPLIT_SKIP_LIVE_MCP=1` to skip the live suite: Live runs only in the VM (section 1a). The live suite (which automatically drives `scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js` against an active Ableton Live MCP session when online to verify `Cmd+D` same-chain right-hand isolation, same-chain reordering, nested sub-rack isolation, cross-chain rank compaction, and device deletion + `Undo` restoration).
- Audio checks (real Max render, shared NonRealTime harness, inside the VM per section 1a): `/usr/bin/python3 scripts/run_mbsplit_audio_tests.py`. Pure Python; the offline reference allpass is ported from `src/mbsplit_config.js` because the VM has no `node`. Includes `dyn_null_vs_delayed_allpass`: one gen in Dynamic mode records its 4-band sum (L) and the dry input (R). The probe dumps both channels next to its result file, and the runner rebuilds the F1 allpass of R with the JS reference, delays it by 128 samples, and requires a null below -120 dB (measured -149 dB). Also `dyn_band_isolation`: 2.4 kHz passes band 3 within 0.1 dB, and 300 Hz is rejected by more than 55 dB. New: `prune_nb4_lr4 / lr8 / lr2`, `prune_nb3_lr4`, `prune_nb2_lr4`, `prune_nb4_dyn`, `prune_nb3_dyn` (pruned instances against unpruned twins, weighted so errors cannot cancel; residual exactly 0), `prune_telemetry` (bands computed = band 3 + prune bit, ring off, only that band meters) and `sleep_wake` (exact zeros while asleep, tone wakes in the same sample, steady state within 0.05 dB, no click). `scripts/vm_mbsplit.sh` syncs to its own `~/ableton-mbsplit` in the VM (`audio`, `run <script>`, `live`, `test`, `livetest`); `scripts/measure_mbsplit.py` produces the numbers above (`scripts/vm_mbsplit.sh run measure_mbsplit.py <core> [big] [silent] "--, prune 1, ringon 0"`).
- **In Live** (VM, under the Live lock): `scripts/vm_mbsplit.sh livetest` writes `mbsplit_test.als` (`scripts/make_mbsplit_set.py`: a 4-chain rack of the device and an empty Reference track, MCP Bridge on Main) and runs `scripts/test_mbsplit_live.js` through an ssh tunnel: deferred load and its trace, all instances prune and compute only their band, exactly one FFT leader and three copying followers, the rack track's output meter equals the Reference track's for sines at 70 / 400 / 2000 / 9000 Hz (rack sum flat in real Live, also after a linked F1 edit), an F1 edit on chain 1 reaches the other chains, muting chain 4 removes the 9 kHz band (Live's output meter is not linear in amplitude, so that check judges the meter ratio: -5.0 dB at the meter for a -24 dB band). All checks pass in Live 12.4.6: rack meter 0.842 = reference at all four tones, gens ready 1075 / 159 / 159 / 157 ms after creation, ring on `[1,0,0,0]`, bands computed `[17,18,20,24]`.

## 12. Architecture of `devices/Cepstra.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`256 x 169` px — Controls on Top, Full-Width Cepstrum Display Below)
- **Normal, visible Ableton-native `live.*` controls on top (`y = 6..72`, panel `x = 4..252, y = 5..73`)** and the dot-matrix cepstrum display under them across the full width (`obj-viz-disp`, **the only `v8ui`**, `x = 6, y = 76, w = 244, h = 88`, over a black rounded panel `x = 4..252, y = 75..165`). No `live.*` widget overlaps the `v8ui` (all end at `y <= 72`), nothing uses `ignoreclick`, there is no title label or text label (Live shows the device name), and the two background `panel`s are the last boxes (`background: 1`). Orange = formant-related controls (Res, Mid, Side, Auto / MIDI), blue = harmonic ones (Gate, Gate Mode), Mix neutral, Lock green, as in the display.
  - Top row (`y = 6..22`, `h = 16`), icon controls using Live's built-in SVGs (`usepicture 1`, `remapsvgcolors 1`; the preview renderer rasterizes them via Quick Look in `builtin_svg_mask()`): `Auto` (`hz.svg`, `x = 6, w = 22`) and `MIDI` (`piano_keys.svg`, `x = 30, w = 22`) `live.text` toggles (exclusive, both off = Manual, mapped onto the `Cutoff Mode` parameter and gen `resmode`) over Res, `Lock M/S` toggle (`unlock.svg` / `lock.svg`, `x = 93, w = 20`, green) centred over the Mid+Side pair, `Gate Mode` `live.tab` (`Spec` = `Auto_Mute.svg`, `Ceps` = `release_velocity.svg`, `x = 150, w = 50`) over Gate, `Quality` `live.tab` (`1k` / `2k` / `4k` text, `x = 204, w = 46`) flush right over Mix.
  - Dials (`y = 26..72`, `46 x 46`, `showname 1`, `shownumber 1`): `Res` (`x = 6`), `Mid Shift` (`56`) and `Side Shift` (`104`, adjacent to Mid), `Gate` (`154`), `Mix` (`204`, right-most, flush right).
  - The `Formant` / `Harmonic` level parameters (and the `LEVELS` bars) were removed. Remaining parameter names, ranges, defaults, types and **order** are unchanged (`Res 0..100 = 57`, `Mid Shift` / `Side Shift` `-36..+36 = 0`, `Mix 0..100 = 100`, `Gate 0..100 = 0`, `Quality 1k/2k/4k = 2k`, `Gate Mode Spec/Ceps = Spec`, `Lock Mid-Side Free/Lock`, `Cutoff Auto`, `Cutoff MIDI`, `Cutoff Mode Man/Auto/MIDI`); all `parameter_enable: 1`. The `Cutoff Mode` tab stays off the face (the AUTO / MIDI toggles map onto it), so existing sets and automation of it still load.
- **Mid / Side Lock and AUTO / MIDI exclusivity live in the display script** (`set_mid` / `set_side` + `applyLock()`, `set_auto` / `set_midi` / `set_mode`). They work for the dial itself, automation and macros because every change arrives as `set_<x>`; the follower (`mid` / `side`) and the other toggle (`auto` / `midi`) are moved by the same delta / state with a silent `prepend set` (+ gen) and never re-enter the script. `Res` follows the same path for a cutoff-bar drag.
- **Display (`x = 6..250, y = 76..164`; everything derives from the v8ui size)** — the **signed real Mid cepstrum `c[n]` against LINEAR quefrency** (`n = 0` at the left edge, `20 ms` at the right; not `|c|`, not log; no axis tick labels):
  - Compact split bar (`y = 3..16`): orange `FORMANTS` label with an orange bracket from the left edge to the cutoff, blue `HARMONICS` label with a blue bracket from the cutoff to the right edge. It uses the same linear axis as the cutoff bar.
  - Dot-matrix plot (`y = 18..83` in the v8ui, `5 px` pitch, `67` columns, `13` rows; the `192` telemetry columns are resampled onto the dot columns by `columnRange()` / `poolSigned()`): the **zero line sits at mid-height** and is always drawn as a dotted line, orange left of the cutoff and blue right of it (with silence or no input the display settles into just that flat dotted line). With signal each column is a vertical **stem of dots from the centre line up (positive) or down (negative) to the column's value**, end dot brightest, snapped to the grid; a column spanning several bins keeps the value with the largest `|c|` and its sign so pitch peaks survive. `c[0]` / `c[1]` are excluded from the normalization and simply clip at the top / bottom edge; columns at or beyond `N / 2` (the `1k` quality) are dim / empty. Columns with `n < L` are orange, `n >= L` blue.
  - Scaling (`cepstrumDisplayHeight()` in `src/cepstra_config.js`): symmetric `sign * asinh(20 |c| / ref) / asinh(20) * 0.85` (`|c| = ref` lands at 85% of the half-height, small stems stay visible, big values clip) with an auto-gain `ref` = 95th percentile of `|c|` over `n >= 2` (`adaptRef`: fast attack `0.6`, slow release `0.03`) and a light `0.5` one-pole smoothing per frame.
  - **Vertical cutoff bar** through the plot and the split bar marks the lifter cutoff `L` on the linear axis (`x = L_ms / 20 ms`). Manual: solid white bar with a grip, follows `Res`; Auto / MIDI: dashed light-blue bar with a diamond that follows the tracked `L`. Clicking or dragging anywhere in the display sets `Res` (`axis01ToRes()`: screen x -> linear ms -> lag -> Res through the unchanged `0.3..15 ms` logarithmic Res mapping; in Auto / MIDI it sets the `k = L / period` scale); double-click resets it. A small green diamond with a `120Hz` / note label marks the pitch-period column in Auto / MIDI. A small `ms / Hz` readout shows while dragging and for about 1 s after a change. Idle redraws are skipped when silent.
  - **Ceps gate overlay**: with Gate Mode = Ceps and Gate > 0 the threshold (`scope[263]`, in `|c|` units) is drawn as faint `+/-` dotted horizontal lines across the harmonic (blue) region only, and gated-out stems (|c| below the threshold, right of the cutoff) are drawn dimmed. Spec mode shows no threshold lines.
- **Sync (no feedback loops)**: every live control reports to the display script with `prepend set_<x>` (`res`, `mid`, `side`, `gate`, `gmode`, `quality`, `lock`, `auto`, `midi`, mode) and to gen (`* 0.01` / `prepend <gen msg>`). Changes that originate in the script (cutoff-bar drag -> `res`, Lock follower -> `mid` / `side`, AUTO / MIDI -> `auto` / `midi` / `resmode`) go `route res mid side resmode auto midi` -> `prepend set` into the live object (silent: `set` never re-outputs) and the gen path. `Mix` drives the gen `wet` Param (a Param named `mix` would shadow the GenExpr `mix()` builtin).

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/cepstra_core.genexpr` — true cepstral formant shifter: STFT overlap-add with Hann analysis and Hann synthesis windows at 75% overlap (hop `N/4`, normalized by `1 / (N * 1.5)`), FFT size `N = 1024 * 2^quality` (`1024` / `2048` / `4096`), own radix-2 FFT written as GenExpr helper functions over `Data` arrays sized for `4096`, `float64` precision.
  - **Mid/Side packing**: `M = (L + R) / 2`, `S = (L - R) / 2`. `M + jS` share one complex FFT. Because both log-spectra and both cepstra are real and symmetric, the same packing carries them through all four FFTs per hop (forward FFT, cepstrum IFFT, envelope FFT, synthesis IFFT).
  - **Per-frame pipeline** (`job_*` functions): `logmag = log(|X| + 1e-9)` -> cepstrum `c = IFFT(logmag)` -> lifter (`|n| < L`, half-Hann taper over `clip(L / 4, 2, 6)` bins) -> envelope `E = FFT(formant cepstrum)` -> fine structure `H = logmag - E` -> shifted envelope `E'[k] = E[k / 2^(st/12)]` (linear interpolation, `midshift` for Mid, `sideshift` for Side) -> harmonic gate (`gate_h`, Spec mode: relative to the **local partial peak**, not the envelope. `job_spec` part 0 builds the peak envelope `P[k] = max_j (H[j] - d |k - j|)` of the fine structure in two O(N) passes (`d` = 1 neper per half harmonic spacing implied by the lifter, `W = N / 2L`; stored in `hre` / `him`, which only the Ceps path otherwise uses), then bins more than `8 + 40 * (1 - Gate)^3` dB below `P` are cut with a soft knee (up to 60 dB). So partials keep their level while the noise between them drops: on the 48 kHz test vowel + noise about 4 dB at 40%, 10 dB at 60%, 14 dB at 100%, f0 within 1.5 dB. An envelope-relative threshold (the old design) sat above most partial peaks, because `E` is a log *mean*, and attenuated partials and noise alike) -> output magnitude `exp(clip(E' + H_gated, -21, 12))` with the original phase (same for Side) -> IFFT -> synthesis window -> overlap-add. Neutral settings (`shift 0`, gate `0`) are an exact magnitude identity.
  - **Gate Mode** (`gatemode`: `0` Spec, `1` Ceps; Gate amount unchanged). `Spec` is the peak-relative spectral gate above (`gate_h` on `H[k]`; bypassed in Ceps mode). `Ceps` is a **voicing-keyed gate** (`job_cgate`, one job per frame, O(N/2)): pitchedness `v` = largest Mid cepstral peak in `max(L, 1 ms) .. 1/60 s` over the RMS of that region (about 3–4.5 for noise at any level, 5–11 for noisy voicing, ~25 for a clean vowel). Frames with `v` below `Tv = 3.5 * 7^Gate` (3.5 .. 24.5, soft knee ±1) are attenuated by up to 60 dB on both channels (stereo-linked log gain `stat[9]`, added in `job_spec`), smoothed across frames (open 0.7, close 0.25 per frame). So low settings gate unpitched content (breaths, hiss, noise) and higher settings let only cleaner pitched content through. `stat[8]` / `scope[263]` = `Tv * RMS` (in `|c|` units): the display's ± threshold lines are the level the pitch peak must cross for the gate to open. `stat[5]` stays 0, so the old harmonic-FFT slots in the schedule are idle (`jobCount` unchanged). At Gate 0 or in Spec mode the gain is 0 (exact identity, null test unchanged). The earlier coefficient-threshold cepstral gate was dropped: with only 2–5 rahmonics per 2048 frame, removing cepstral detail smooths the comb and *raised* the noise between partials at low settings. JS reference: `cepstralVoicing()` / `voicingThreshold()` / `voicingGateStep()` in `src/cepstra_config.js`.
  - **Cutoff modes** (`resmode`): `0` Manual (`res` -> `0.3 ms .. 15 ms`, logarithmic, clamped `< N/2`); `1` Auto (largest local maximum of the Mid cepstrum in `1/1000 s .. 1/60 s` with parabolic refinement and a `0.06` voicing threshold; `L = k * period`, `k = 0.3 + 0.7 * res`, held when unvoiced); `2` MIDI (`midinote` Param, `period = sr / mtof(note)`, same `k`, last value kept with no note). `L` is smoothed between frames (`0.4` per frame).
  - **Time-sliced work**: each frame is `5 * log2(N) + 11` jobs (one FFT pass or one slice of an O(N) loop each), spread across the first `N/8` samples of the hop, so no single sample runs more than one FFT pass. Latency is therefore `N + N/8` samples (`2304` at `2k`); the dry path is delayed by the same amount (`Data` ring) and Mix is equal-power.
  - **Quality switch**: changing `quality` aborts the in-flight frame, clears the overlap-add rings and mutes the output for `N + N/8 + 3 * hop + 256` samples with a 256-sample fade-in (brief mute, no crash).
  - **Silence sleep**: input below `-100 dB` (`0.00001`) for a whole frame (`quietCount >= N`) skips all FFT work (lossless: such a frame's output is zero); outputs go through `fixdenorm` / `fixnan`.
- `Buffer scope("cepscope")` (`272` samples), bound on `loadbang` via `scope #0-cepscope`: `0..191` the **signed** Mid cepstrum `c[n]` on 192 **linear** quefrency columns (`n = 0 .. 20 ms`, `qmax = 0.02 * samplerate`; each column keeps the bin with the largest `|c|` and its sign; `9999` = column at or beyond `N/2`), `256` current `L` in samples, `257` f0 estimate (Hz), `258` input level, `259` frame counter, `260` `N`, `261` voiced flag, `262` `resmode`, `263` Ceps-gate threshold for Mid in `|c|` units (`0` when inactive). Written once per frame.
- MIDI: `midiin` -> `midiparse` -> `unpack 0 0` -> `> 0` (velocity) opens a `gate` so only note-ons pass -> `prepend midinote` -> gen (and `prepend midi_note` -> `v8ui`). Note-offs close the gate, so the last note is kept.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_cepstra.py && node tests/test_cepstra.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Cepstra.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max render, shared `scripts/max_harness.py`, **inside the tart VM only**, section 1a): `python3 scripts/run_cepstra_audio_tests.py` — every case holds the harness's machine-wide Max lock (section 1b) and runs at a forced `48 kHz` on Max's NonRealTime driver in its own temp dir (scheduler `delay`/`metro` timing is not used). `qswitch` uses `mode="stepped"` with an `events` entry so the `quality 2` message lands on time, and `perf` uses `mode="audio_device"` (real CoreAudio, machine-wide lock) because CPU load cannot be measured offline. Stimulus is a `120 Hz` impulse train through `700 / 1220 / 2600 Hz` resonators. The probe also checks that the largest `c[n]` over `2..20 ms` in the telemetry lands at the `1/f0` column (`8.33 ms`) with positive sign (at `4k` the 2nd rahmonic, `16.67 ms`, may out-rank it, so either is accepted there). Cases: neutral null (`1k` / `2k` / `4k`, and `neutral_ceps`: Gate Mode = Ceps at Gate 0), `ceps_off` / `gate_ceps` (`vowelgaps` stimulus: vowel on 0.25 s of every 0.5 s with constant noise; Ceps 20%: noise-only stretches drop >= 40 dB while vowel stretches stay within 1.5 dB), `Mix 0` equals delayed dry, `Mid +12 st` raises the spectral centroid while f0 stays, `gate_off` / `gate` (Spec 60% on vowel + noise: between-partials vs partials drops >= 6 dB while the f0 component stays within 1.5 dB), Auto / MIDI cutoff telemetry, Quality switch mid-run, silence sleep, `4k` CPU (`adstatus cpu` max below 70%, real-time driver). The probe takes `resultpath` / `analyze <dur_ms>` from the harness and gets a `rec` buffer holding exactly the measured window, so there is no sample-rate retry workaround.

## 13. Architecture of `devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd` & Live Debugging (`ableton_mcp_bridge.als`)

### Overview & Pre-Configured Live Project (`/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als`)
- **`/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als`** is a ready-to-launch Ableton Live Set containing `devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd` loaded on the Master track (`live_set master_track devices 0`).
- Because `ableton_mcp_bridge.als` references `devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd` directly on disk (`../devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd`), rebuilding `devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd` via `python3 scripts/build_ableton_mcp.py` automatically takes effect the next time `ableton_mcp_bridge.als` is opened in Ableton Live.
- **Quick Launch & Verification**:
  ```bash
  # Launch Ableton Live with the MCP Bridge project
  open -a "Ableton Live 12 Suite" "/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als"

  # Wait until the bridge HTTP listener is online (default port 9877) and verify LOM access
  node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --list-sessions
  node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --call ableton_get_session_info '{}'
  node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --call ableton_get_tracks_tree '{"includeParams":true}'
  ```

### Presentation Layout (`260 x 169` px — `devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd`)
- **Pass-Through Audio (`plugin~` -> `plugout~`)**: Zero-latency stereo pass-through so the device can sit transparently on the Master track or any track/chain without altering audio.
- **Live Telemetry & RPC Monitor (`obj-ui`, `v8ui`, `x = 6, y = 6, w = 248, h = 157`)**:
  - **Top Header (`y = 0..22`)**: Status LED, `MCP BRIDGE` title, active port badge (`PORT 9877`, or `PORT 9878+` in multi-session mode), and `[PING]` self-test button.
  - **Info Strip (`y = 26..44`)**: Live endpoint readout (`127.0.0.1:<port> | RPCs: <count>`) and `[CLEAR]` log button.
  - **RPC Activity Log (`y = 48..153`)**: Real-time scrolling log of the most recent LOM RPC commands, latency (`ms`), and status (`OK` / `ERR`).

### Two-Tier Architecture (`node.codebox` + `v8ui` LOM Executor + Stdio MCP Server)
1. **`src/ableton_mcp_node.js` (`node.codebox` `@autostart 1`)**:
   - Hosts a zero-dependency local HTTP JSON-RPC bridge on `127.0.0.1:<port>` (`GET /health`, `POST /rpc`, `POST /shutdown`).
   - Forwards incoming `/rpc` requests (`{"action": "...", "params": {...}}`) into Max via `maxApi.outlet("lom_req", JSON.stringify(payload))` and awaits `lom_res` from `v8ui`.
2. **`src/ableton_mcp_lom.js` (`v8ui` on Max's Main/LOM Thread)**:
   - Executes `LiveAPI` (Live Object Model) operations safely on Max's main thread and inspects shared `Global(...)` buses inside the Max JS runtime.
   - Supported actions / MCP tools (registered in `~/.cursor/mcp.json` under `"ableton-m4l"` via `scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js`):
     - `ableton_list_sessions`: Lists all active Ableton + MCP Bridge instances running on this machine (`ports 9877..9884` + `/tmp/ableton_mcp_sessions.json`).
     - `ableton_ping`: Health check + basic `live_set` tempo/playing state.
     - `ableton_get_session_info`: Tempo, time signature, transport state, track count, return count.
     - `ableton_get_tracks_tree`: Full hierarchical dump of all tracks, return tracks, master track, nested `RackDevice` chains, devices, and parameters.
     - `ableton_lom_get` / `ableton_lom_set` / `ableton_lom_call`: Generic `LiveAPI` property reader, property writer, and function invoker on any LOM path (e.g., `live_set tracks 0 devices 0 chains 1`).
     - `ableton_get_device_parameters` / `ableton_set_device_parameter`: Reads or sets device parameters by index or name (`str_for_value` included).
     - `ableton_delete_device`: Deletes a device at `trackPath` (`delete_device <deviceIndex>`) to test live device deletion and Undo behavior.
     - `ableton_transport`: Controls `play`, `stop`, `continue`, `undo`, `redo`, and `tempo`.
     - `ableton_inspect_mbsplit`: Inspects `Global("mbsplit_shared_bus")` (`peers`, `groups`, `selfLomAlive`, `scopeBeat`, `detectedBands`) alongside every live `Multiband Split` device in the Set.
     - `ableton_eval_lom_js`: Evaluates arbitrary diagnostic JavaScript on Max's `v8ui` LOM thread (`LiveAPI`, `Global`, `Max` available in scope) and returns the result + `post()` logs.

### Starting & Controlling Multiple Unique Ableton + MCP Sessions on This Machine
Agents can run and control **multiple isolated Ableton Live + MCP Bridge sessions simultaneously** on this machine:

1. **Automatic Port Allocation & PID Isolation (`9877..9892`)**:
   - When `src/ableton_mcp_node.js` starts, it tries `ABLETON_MCP_PORT` (default `9877`).
   - If a port returns `EADDRINUSE`, it queries `GET http://127.0.0.1:<port>/health`:
     - **Same Ableton/Max Instance (`existing.max_ppid === process.ppid`)**: When a device is reloaded inside the *same* Live session, the new `node.codebox` sends `POST /shutdown` to the stale listener and re-binds the same port (`9877`).
     - **Different Ableton/Max Instance (`existing.max_ppid !== process.ppid`)**: When a *second* Ableton Live process is running concurrently, the bridge leaves port `9877` untouched and automatically binds the next available port (`9878`, `9879`, etc.), updating its UI badge (`PORT 9878`) and registering in `/tmp/ableton_mcp_sessions.json`.

2. **Recommended Dual-Session Launch (Zero Modal Dialogs — Suite + Beta)**:
   - This machine has both **`/Applications/Ableton Live 12 Suite.app`** (`Live 12.4.6`) and **`/Applications/Ableton Live 12 Beta.app`** (`Live 12.4.15b2`) installed with separate preference directories (`~/Library/Preferences/Ableton/Live 12.4.6/` and `Live 12.4.15b2/`).
   - Because they are separate app bundles/versions, macOS and Ableton Live allow both to open `.als` projects simultaneously with **zero single-instance modal warnings**:
     ```bash
     # Session 1 -> binds 127.0.0.1:9877
     open -a "Ableton Live 12 Suite" "/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als"

     # Session 2 -> automatically detects 9877 is in use by Suite and binds 127.0.0.1:9878
     open -a "Ableton Live 12 Beta" "/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als"

     # Discover all active sessions and their ports/PIDs
     node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --list-sessions
     ```

3. **Launching Additional Cloned Sessions of the Same App Binary (`open -n`)**:
   - If you need a 3rd+ session using `open -n -a "Ableton Live 12 Suite" --args "/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als"`, note two important details:
     1. Each Ableton Live instance spawns its own embedded `Max.app` server. If a second instance of the *same* Live build launches, Ableton shows a startup modal (`"Another instance of the same Live version is running. Live will not be able to recover Sets after crashes."`) before loading the `.als` file—dismiss it via Return key (`osascript -e 'tell application "System Events" to key code 36'`) so Live finishes loading the Set and starts the bridge on port `9879+`.
     2. You can also pre-assign a dedicated port and tag via environment variables when launching:
        ```bash
        ABLETON_MCP_PORT=9879 ABLETON_MCP_SESSION_TAG="agent-b" open -a "Ableton Live 12 Beta" "/Users/mrwilson/software/ableton/ableton_mcp_bridge.als"
        ```

4. **Targeting a Specific Session in MCP Tools or CLI**:
   - Every MCP tool accepts an optional `"port"` integer parameter (`9877`, `9878`, etc.), and the CLI accepts `--port <port>`:
     ```bash
     # Query Session 1 (Port 9877)
     node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --port 9877 --call ableton_get_session_info '{}'

     # Query Session 2 (Port 9878)
     node scripts/ableton_mcp_server.js --port 9878 --call ableton_get_session_info '{}'
     ```
   - **Process Safety Rule**: Never run a broad `pkill -f "Max.app/Contents/MacOS/Max"` while an Ableton Live session is open, because Ableton Live hosts Max for Live inside a child `Max.app` process (`max_ppid` reported by `ableton_list_sessions`).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_ableton_mcp.py && node tests/test_ableton_mcp.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Ableton MCP Bridge.amxd"
```

## 12. Architecture of `devices/Envelope Trigger.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`228 x 169` px collapsed, `342 x 169` px with MIDI To open)
- **No title bar**: Live's own device header names it.
- **Main (`x = 4..204`)**:
  - Graph (`obj-viz`, `v8ui`, `x = 8, y = 8, w = 192, h = 104`) has two views, picked with a segmented `[envelope | scope]` vector-icon toggle in the HUD's top-left (`x = 3..31`):
    - **Envelope:** the scrolling fast envelope (dB scale, about 3.6 s) and the slow baseline.
    - **Scope:** an oscilloscope of the mono input (linear, about 186 ms), drawn as one min/max polygon per pixel column, with the threshold mirrored as ± lines.
  - The selected view emits `view 0|1` → `route thr view` → the hidden `Graph View` `live.text` (Toggle mode, `parameter_invisible: 1`) → `prepend set_view`. `live.thisdevice` re-applies it through an `i` store.
  - Both views draw:
    - a dashed threshold you can drag (emits `thr` → gen + `prepend set` on the dial)
    - thin (1 px) yellow fired-note ticks, always full height
    - a flashing velocity LED on the right
    - a HUD with `> <MIDI To destination>` (coral when it is `No Output`) and the note plus last velocity
  - Detection dials under the graph (`y = 116..162`): `Threshold`, `Sens`, `Hold`.
- **Collapsible strip (`obj-strip`, `v8ui`, `x = 208..224`)**:
  - Modelled on Envelope Follower's "Audio Routing" strip: a round arrow button and a vertical `MIDI TO` label drawn with `save` / `translate` / `rotate`.
  - Clicking it sends 0/1 to the hidden `MIDI To Panel` `live.text` (`parameter_invisible: 1`, stored, not in presentation).
  - That `live.text` must use `mode: 1` (Toggle). In Button mode (`mode: 0`) it never latches, so the panel won't open.
  - The toggle feeds `t i`, plus an `i` store that `live.thisdevice` bangs on load to re-apply the saved state. Never bang the toggle itself: a bang flips a Toggle `live.text`.
  - The `t i` drives:
    - `set_open` back to the strip
    - `expr 228 + $i1 * 114` → `prepend setwidth` → `live.thisdevice`
    - `== 0` → `prepend hidden` → every panel widget, plus the `obj-panel-midi` background
  - **Test hook:** `receive envtrig-test` → `obj-strip`.
    - `test_click` behaves like a click.
    - `test_report` writes `{open, toggle, width, hidden{<scripting name>: 0/1}}` to `Global("envtrig_test")` as `report_<LOM device id>` and `last`.
    - Every panel widget has a `varname` so the strip can `getnamed()` it.
    - `PANEL_NAMES` is prepended to the strip script at build time.
- **MIDI To panel (`x = 228..338`, starts `hidden: 1`)**: `MIDI Output Type` and `MIDI Output Channel` menus, then `Note` (unitstyle 8), `MIDI Ch`, `Length`, `Vel Lo`, `Vel Hi`.
- `scripts/render_preview.py` renders the collapsed state. Its v8ui mock supports `save` / `restore` / `translate` / `rotate`; paths and text follow the matrix, while rects and arcs follow only its translation.

### MIDI out from an audio effect (MidiRouter technique)
- **How it routes:** an Audio Effect sends MIDI to any track through a plain `midiout`, which goes to the device's LOM DeviceIO `this_device midi_outputs 0`. Setting that DeviceIO's `routing_type` / `routing_channel` gives the same "MIDI To" choice as External Instrument, and Live saves it with the set. Requires Live 11.0.5+.
- **Initialisation:** `live.thisdevice` → `init_routing` → `obj-viz` builds four `LiveAPI` observers (`available_routing_types`, `available_routing_channels`, `routing_type`, `routing_channel`). Never init from `loadbang`, because the LOM isn't ready yet.
- **Reading values in v8:** `get()` returns `["{json}"]`. Observer callbacks pass `[property, <Max Dict>]`, where the Dict is a handle like `{name: "u0950..."}` that must be read with `.stringify()`. `lomValue()` in `src/envtrig_config.js` handles every shape. Treating the Dict as data left the menus empty in Live.
- **Menus:** both are hidden parameters (`parameter_invisible: 2`, no initial value) that only mirror the LOM. `obj-viz` outlets 1 and 2 fill them with `_parameter_range` (undocumented) and `setsymbol`; their output comes back as `settype $1` / `setchannel $1`.
- **Destination track:** it must be monitoring (`In`, or `Auto` while armed) for its instrument to play.
- **Note path:** gen gate → `edge~` → `t b b`. The right outlet samples the held velocity with `snapshot~` into `makenote`'s velocity inlet; the left outlet sends `i <note>` to `makenote`'s pitch inlet. Then `pack 0 0` → `midiformat` (channel inlet 6) → `midiout`.
- **Timing:** notes are scheduler-timed (one audio vector of jitter), plus the 2.5 ms velocity capture.

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden, 4 outlets)
- `src/envtrig_core.genexpr` — single band on the mono sum. Audio passes through untouched (`out1`/`out2`).
- **Envelopes:** a fast envelope (instant attack, 15 ms release) and a slow 60 ms baseline.
- **Onset:** fires when fast > `thr` and fast > slow × `(1 − sens) × 12 dB`, while armed and outside the `holdms` lockout.
- **Velocity:** a 2.5 ms capture of the peak, mapped from threshold..0 dBFS to `velmin..velmax`.
- **Gate:** high for `holdms / 2`, so `edge~` sees it at any vector size.
- **Re-arm:** below 70 % of the threshold, or back near the baseline.
- **Outlets:** `out3` gate, `out4` held velocity.
- **Naming:** never name a Param `hold`; with that name the lockout and gate outputs silently stopped working.
- **Telemetry:** `Buffer scope("trigscope")` (`buffer~ #0-trigscope @samps 800`, bound via `scope #0-trigscope`).
  - Written every 64 samples:
    - `0` fast env
    - `1` slow env
    - `2` fire counter
    - `3` roll
    - `4` input peak
    - `5` last velocity
  - Oscilloscope ring, one block per 32 samples:
    - `6` write head (`0..255`)
    - `32 + 3 × block` holds mono min, max, and an onset flag
    - The onset flag is set at the onset, not at the end of the velocity capture, so ticks line up with attacks.
  - Pokes stop once the device has been silent for a few windows.
  - The layout constants (`SCOPE_BLOCKS`, `SCOPE_RING_OFFSET`, `SCOPE_BUFFER_SAMPS`) live in `src/envtrig_config.js`. `tests/test_envtrig.js` checks that the build and the gen core match them.

### Performance
- **Gen:**
  - The `pow`/`exp` coefficients (threshold, onset ratio, envelope coefficients) are cached in `History`. They're recomputed only when `thr`, `sens` or the sample rate changes, not every sample.
  - The `log10` velocity math runs only when a note fires.
- **UI:**
  - The graph polls at 30 fps and stops redrawing once its history has scrolled flat in silence. A moving ring head, a new fire or a decaying LED wakes it again.
  - The envelope view reads only the 7-sample header. The 768-sample oscilloscope ring is copied only while the scope view is showing, and only when its head has moved.

### Build / Test / Preview
Host (no Max):
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_envtrig.py && node tests/test_envtrig.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Envelope Trigger.amxd"
```
Max and Live run only in the tart VM (section 1a), through `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh`, which rsyncs the repo to `~/ableton` there:
- **Audio:** `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh audio` runs `scripts/run_envtrig_audio_tests.py` on the shared NonRealTime harness (section 1b).
  - Test signals come from an audio-rate gen source; nothing is timed with `metro` or `delay`.
  - The probe records a single `rec` buffer: ch1 = gate × velocity, ch2 = audio out.
  - In the `scope` case, bursts every 100 ms check that the ring holds about 0.8 min/max peaks and onset flags. The source keeps playing until the result is read, so the burst period must stay shorter than the 186 ms ring.
- **Collapse/expand:** `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh ui` runs `scripts/run_envtrig_ui_tests.py`.
  - It runs the device's own patcher inside the harness scaffold.
  - Harness `events` send `hook test_report` / `hook test_click` to a `v8` probe (`tests/envtrig_ui_probe.js`). The probe forwards them to the `envtrig-test` hook and checks the toggle, `setwidth` and the 13 panel widgets' visibility.
  - The probe must be `v8`: classic `js` and `v8` `Global`s clash on the same name.
  - Don't name a probe handler `step`: messages sent to it never reached the probe.
  - The scheduler can lag audio time even in `stepped` mode, so the probe writes its result only once it has seen both `analyze` and all seven hooks.
- **In Live:**
  - Recommended: `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh run` (live + test under the Live lock, section 1a). `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh live` rebuilds in the VM and writes `envtrig_test.als` (MCP Bridge on Main, two Envelope Trigger instances in one rack chain, generated by `scripts/make_delayplus_sets.py` with VM paths). It then restarts the VM's Live with that set.
  - Check that no other session is using Live in the VM before you do this: the restart ends any Live session there.
  - Killing Live leaves `CrashRecoveryInfo.cfg` / `CrashDetection.cfg` in `~/Library/Preferences/Ableton/Live <version>/`. Their "Live unexpectedly quit… recover your work?" dialog blocks the next launch, so the bridge never comes up. The script deletes both before reopening Live.
  - `scripts/vm_envtrig.sh test` then runs `scripts/test_envtrig_live.js` on the host through an ssh tunnel (local 19878 → VM bridge 9877). It clicks every instance's strip through the hook and checks the same state, then leaves each panel as it found it.

## 14. Architecture of `devices/Better Sampler.amxd`

### Presentation Layout (`600 x 169` px, `760` px with the inspector, Bitwig Sampler look)
- **Three `v8ui` canvases** run `src/sampler_face.js` (after `src/sampler_config.js` + `src/sampler_ui.js`), chosen by `FACE_REGION`:
  - `obj-viz` (`"main"`, `0,0,528,169`): engine list, sample panel and bottom panels. It holds the `SamplerStateData` blob.
  - `obj-viz-side` (`"side"`, `531,3,66,163`): Inspector button, `Poly` / `Mono`, `Width` (L R), `Glide`, `Vel`, `Out`.
  - `obj-viz-insp` (`"inspector"`, `600,0,160,169`): the inspector panel.
- `show_inspector` makes the face emit `setwidth_cmd 760` (or `600`) → `prepend setwidth` → `live.thisdevice`. `live.thisdevice`'s ready bang sends `device_ready` so the saved width is re-applied.
- Every parameter is a real, automatable `live.*` object (`obj-ctrl-<key>`) kept off the face (`presentation 0`).
- The face sends `["param", key, value]` → `route param load_sample browse drop_set setwidth_cmd` → `route <every key>` → `obj-ctrl-<key>`. The objects echo through `prepend set_<key>` → all three v8uis → `storeParam()`.
- **Late echoes**: Live can echo a gesture's values late. `expectEcho()` / `isStaleEcho()` drop an echo that is older than the newest value the face sent, so a dragged marker or knob never snaps back. Values the face didn't send (automation) always apply.
- **Look**: flat panels (`0.165`) on grey (`0.255`) with a near-black sample panel, blue values, yellow start / end, green loop, and orange knob arcs. Knobs are grey discs with a white pointer, and the "hero" knob of a panel is a white disc. Colours live in `COL`.
- **Left column**: `Single` / `Sliced` (param `sliced`) and the five engines in Bitwig order, Repitch, Spectral, Cycles, Textures, Fragments (`ENGINE_ORDER`). `mode` indices are 0 Repitch, 1 Cycles, 2 Textures, 3 Spectral, 4 Fragments.
- **Sample panel**:
  - Header: file name (click to browse), active / max voices, `ROOT` note + `ct`, and L/R meters.
  - Waveform (Single):
    - Start / end flags.
    - Bitwig loop overlay: a green body, a crossfade gradient before the loop start (drag it: zone `xfade`), handles at the bottom, and a middle handle that moves the whole loop (`loop_move`).
    - Footer: `PLAY` (reverse, start, end) and `LOOP` (mode dropdown, start, end, xfade %).
  - Waveform (Sliced): yellow slice flags with key names, faint unmapped markers, and blue start / end handles. Footer: `Use: keys from <slice_from>`, `Slice at: <Divisions | Beats | Onsets | Pitches | Manual>` with its amount (count, note value menu, or sensitivity %), and `Playback` (`slice_play`).
- **Dropdowns**: there is one generic menu (`openMenu`, `toggleMenu`, `menuItems`, zones `menu` / `menu_pick`). It is drawn last so it sits over everything, and a click elsewhere closes it.
- **Bottom row**:
  - Engine panel (mode-specific):
    - Repitch: `◀ Clean | Analog | Digital ▶`, with Crunch / Tape or Bits / Color, plus the Digital `rp_imaging` / `rp_vsr` toggles.
    - Spectral: `Pure` (Formant, Shift %) or `Reharm` (Bend, Quantize menu, %), plus `sp_onsets` / `sp_formant_on` toggles.
    - Cycles: `Pulse` (Formant, Width), `Partials` (Formant, Split, Fund.) or `Phase` (Formant, Mod, Harm.), plus `cy_xfade`.
    - Textures: Size, Spray.
    - Fragments: grain envelope glyph (`fr_env`), `Rpt` (`fr_repeats`), Size / Density / Motion, and `fr_sync` / `fr_use_density` toggles.
  - Play panel: Freeze, Pitch, Speed (dimmed in Repitch) and Pos (dimmed unless frozen).
  - Filter panel.
  - Envelope panel: `AMP` / `MOD` / `LFO` / `UNI` pages.
- **Inspector**: Note Glide (`glide_ms` + `L` legato = `mono_legato`), then `<Mode> mode` settings.
  - Spectral: Phase Dispersion, Quality / Cost, Live Performance, Relative Formants, Local Key, Local Scale.
  - Fragments: Direction, Latch Initial Rate, Repeat at Initial Pos, Grains Survive End, Max Grains / Voice.
- **Interaction**:
  - Dragging a knob vertically covers its full range in 120 px, shift-drag is 8x finer, and double-click resets it.
  - A knob click captures the mouse until release: the cursor is hidden and hover is frozen.
  - Hovering or dragging a knob shows its value.
  - The LOM cannot give a knob Live's parameter focus: `live_set view selected_parameter` is read-only.
- **Loading a sample**: drop it on the waveform (`obj-drop-wave`), or click the file name (`browse` → `opendialog sound`). Both reach the face (`prepend dropfile` / `prepend browsed`), which emits `load_sample` → `prepend replace` → `buffer~ #0-bsamp`. The buffer's done bang → `prepend sample_loaded` → face.
  - A path split by spaces is rejoined. An empty `live.drop` sends `none` on load; it is ignored.
  - **`live.drop` only outputs in parameter mode** (`parameter_enable 1`, `parameter_type 4`, `parameter_invisible 1`, `Sample File`). Live then replays the saved path on Set load; `dropfile` ignores a replay of the path being restored. A dialog pick sends `drop_set` → `prepend set` → the drop zone. Keep exactly one `live.drop`.
  - `obj-drop-wave` is the one allowed `live.*` / `v8ui` overlap: invisible, `ignoreclick 1`, after `obj-viz` in `boxes`.
  - `buffer~` reports a finished load as `sample_loaded bang`, which must not rename the sample.
  - A mono file leaves a 1-channel buffer: the face reads `channelcount()`, and the gen peeks with `channelmode="clamp"`.
  - A failed refresh sets `lastLoadError` and is `post()`ed.
  - Load trace: `Global("better_sampler_trace").text`, readable through the bridge with `return new Global("better_sampler_trace").text;`. `#0` is not substituted in a top-level device, so `Buffer("#0-bsamp")` is readable too.
- **Sliced keymap**:
  - `activeSliceStarts(sliceMarkers, start, end)`: the first slice starts at the start point. Slice `k` plays on `slice_from + k` (`voiceAlloc.setSliceBaseNote`), and the last slice stops at the end.
  - **Live bounds**: start / end changes re-resolve held notes (`updateSliceBounds`) and re-poke `#0-bsvoices` without retriggering.
  - **Slice at** (`recomputeSlices()`, slicers in `src/sampler_config.js`): Divisions and Beats (tempo from the file name via `bpmFromName`, else Live's tempo) are laid out from the start point and move with it. Onsets / Pitches run on a mono copy of the sample (`sliceMono`). The copy is built lazily, only when one of them is selected, and in 256k-frame chunks on a `Task` (`requestSliceMono`), so loading a sample reads only waveform-sized chunks. The footer shows `analyzing…` meanwhile, and a new load cancels it and starts over. In Manual, double-click adds or removes a marker, and dragging a flag moves it (that also switches to Manual).
- **Analyzed Root Key** (`root_dyn`, Bitwig's "Dynamic" root): clicking the ROOT icon or label in the header toggles it. When it's on, the header shows `Dynamic` instead of the note / `ct` values.
  - The face tracks the sample's pitch once per sample with `trackPitchCurve()` (`src/sampler_config.js`: YIN on audio decimated to about 8 kHz, about 40 Hz to 2 kHz, a 25 ms hop, octave-error folding and a median; it holds the last pitch through unvoiced parts and uses the first voiced pitch, or ROOT, before it). It runs on the lazy mono copy, so it starts only when the feature is on.
  - The 4096-point MIDI curve is written to `buffer~ #0-bspitch` (bound in the gen as `pitch #0-bspitch`), which the gen reads as the root at each voice's read position.
  - The curve is drawn in orange over the waveform, scaled to its own range plus 2 st, bright where voiced and dim where held. A new sample re-analyses; toggling reuses the cached curve. Tests: `node tests/test_sampler_pitch.js`.
- **Sliced keymap bounds**: only keys `slice_from .. slice_from + slices - 1` play (`sliceKeyMapped`). Keys below the first slice key or past the last slice play nothing (no voice is allocated). They used to clamp or wrap onto a slice.
- **Grain display (Textures / Fragments)**: the face reads telemetry slots 64..95 (up to 32 grains' read positions, -1 when a slot is unused) and 96..127 (their window levels) from `#0-bsscope` (`@samps 128`). It draws a thin line per audible grain, brighter when louder, plus a faint spray band. Every overlay is clipped to the waveform (`fillClipped`).
- **Pos (`play_pos`) only applies while Freeze is on**.
- With no sample loaded, MIDI still runs the voices, but no scan lines are drawn.

### Deferred DSP (GUI first)
- **The device ships without its gen~.** All gen-bound messages (`prepend <param>`, the buffer-binding message, `prepend tempo`) go into a pass-through hub, `obj-gen-in` (`gate 1 1`, left open, scripting name `bs_genin`; a `t a` hub silently refused the scripted connect). `plugin~` / `plugout~` have the scripting names `bs_plugin` / `bs_plugout`.
- The main face's script embeds the gen patch as `.gendsp` JSON (`GEN_DSP_TEXT`, built by `gendsp_json()` in `scripts/build_sampler.py`) and its hash (`GEN_DSP_HASH`). The side and inspector faces don't.
- On `device_ready` the face waits one Task tick (30 ms) so the GUI paints. `loadDsp()` then:
  - writes `/tmp/better_sampler_core_<hash>.gendsp` (only if missing or a different size);
  - creates `gen~ @gen <file>` (named `obj-gen`) with `patcher.newdefault`;
  - wires `bs_genin → gen`, `bs_plugin → gen` (both channels) and `gen → bs_plugout`.
- The gen keeps compiling after creation and drops messages until it runs. So `dspConfirm()` re-sends `dsp_ready` every 150 ms until the gen's telemetry frame counter (`#0-bsscope` slot 39) moves, with a 10 s timeout.
  - `dsp_ready` goes `route` → `obj-dsp-resend` (`t b b`): it re-bangs the buffer-binding message and every gen-bound `obj-ctrl-<key>`, so their current values reach the gen.
  - It is sent once more on ready and again 300 ms later, because the last resend before the counter moved can still be dropped.
- `writeVoiceToBuffer` pokes a voice's slice bounds (slots 4/5) before the whole slot. One `poke` writes slot by slot, so a new trigger counter could otherwise start the note on the previous slice.
- The header shows an orange `DSP loading…` pill in the voice-count slot until then, or a red `DSP failed` pill (cannot write the file, no gen~ created, or timeout). Notes played before that are silent.
- Measured in the VM: creating the gen blocks Max's main thread for about 0.37 s; the rest of the compile runs after the GUI is up.
- **Things that don't work**:
  - Passing the code as `@code` (or `setattr("code")`) to a new `gen.codebox~`: the text is re-tokenised, so newlines and comments are lost, and the core never ran.
  - The shared harness's offline driver never adds objects created mid-run to its DSP chain (not even a plain `cycle~`), so the deferred path can only be verified in Live. The audio tests build the gen directly.

### In Live (tart VM)
The deferred path only runs inside Live, so it has an automated test (AGENTS.md 1a: the VM only):
```bash
python3 scripts/build_sampler.py && node tests/test_sampler.js
scripts/vm_sampler.sh run     # recommended: live + test under one Live-lock hold (section 1a)
scripts/vm_sampler.sh live    # sync, rebuild in the VM, write sampler_test.als (scripts/make_sampler_set.py), relaunch Live, wait for the bridge
scripts/vm_sampler.sh test    # scripts/test_sampler_live.js through an ssh tunnel; scripts/vm_sampler.sh audio = the audio suite
```
`sampler_test.als` = one MIDI track with the device (`MxDeviceInstrument`) + the MCP Bridge on Main. The test waits up to 20 s for `dsp ready` in `Global("better_sampler_trace")` (each line is stamped `+<ms>` since the script loaded; fails with the trace on `dsp-error`), runs `dsp_diag`, checks the telemetry counter (slot 39) advances, drops a generated 220 Hz WAV and plays `midi_note 60 110`, expecting the track's `output_meter_left` above 0.05. Debug messages go through `messnamed("#0-bsdebug", ...)` (`#0` is not substituted in a top-level device, so the bridge can address it). Measured: gen~ creation starts about 0.5 s after the script loads and `dsp ready` follows about 1.6 s later (about 2.1 s total).

### Gen core (`gen.codebox~`, hidden)
- `src/sampler_core.genexpr`: 16 voices, each with up to 8 stereo unison sub-voices.
- **Engines** (`mode`):
  - `0` Repitch, with characters (`rp_char`):
    - Clean.
    - Analog: saturator with pre/post EQ (`rp_dist`), and a tape stage (`rp_color`: + brighter, hiss, wow/flutter, azimuth; − low-mid, softer).
    - Digital: 13 bits at Dist 0 down to 2, sample-rate reduction for Color, plus `rp_vsr` and `rp_imaging`.
  - `1` Cycles, with `formant` and characters (`cy_char`):
    - Pulse: phase-distortion width.
    - Partials: odd/even `cy_split` plus a `cy_fund` sine.
    - Phase: dirty-sine PM, `cy_mod` / stepped `cy_harm`.
    - `cy_xfade` crossfades two windows.
  - `2` Textures: granular cloud.
  - `3` Spectral: phase vocoder at 75 % overlap, FFT 1024 / 2048 / 4096 by `sp_quality`, time-sliced like Cepstra.
    - Pitch is bin remapping. A smoothed-envelope formant stage gives Pure `formant` / `sp_shift`.
    - Reharm `sp_bend` and `sp_quant` (Octaves / Key with `sp_key` / `sp_scale`, `sp_quant_amt`) act on harmonics above 1.5 times the root fundamental, so they need a correct `root_note`.
    - Also `sp_onsets`, `sp_dispersion`, `sp_live`, `sp_rel_formant`.
    - **Only voices 0..3 run Spectral**: the face caps polyphony at 4 in this mode (`voiceCap()`).
  - `4` Fragments: grain pool capped at **32 per voice** (`fr_max_grains` is clamped), round-robin.
    - Each spawn starts `fr_repeats` grains one grain length apart.
    - Controls: `fr_density` / `fr_use_density`, `fr_env` shape, `fr_motion` (pan and playhead randomisation), `fr_direction`, `fr_latch`, `fr_repeat_init`, `fr_survive` (timer).
    - `fr_sync` snaps the grain size to beat multiples of `tempo`. The face observes `live_set tempo` and sends `tempo <bpm>` → `prepend tempo` → gen.
- **`sliced`** is orthogonal to the engine. Every engine reads the slice bounds from `#0-bsvoices` live (slots 4/5) and honours `slice_play` (0 slice end, 1 sample end, 2 one-way loop, 3 ping-pong) and reverse. Pitch ignores the note when sliced.
