- **N = 5 generic controls** (the max over the types), assigned per type by `AUTO_KEYS` (`src/megamod_config.js`); Depth is always the third: LFO `rate, syncDiv, depth, phase, -`; Envelope `attack, release, depth, gain, -`; Random `rate, syncDiv, depth, smooth, seed`; Steps `rate, syncDiv, depth, steps, glide`; MSEG `rate, syncDiv, depth, loopStart, loopEnd`. Rate **and** Div are both there (the one `sync` selects is audible). **Switches** (`AUTO_SW_KEYS` = `shape, sync, bipolar, retrig, oneshot, loopOn`, `AUTO_SW_USED[type]` says which a type shows) have a FIXED meaning for every type, so they get real Live names: `M3 Shape`, `M3 Sync`, `M3 Bipolar`, `M3 Retrig`, `M3 1Shot`, `M3 Loop` (`autoSwitchName`); the value is the model value itself (`0/1`, shape `0..4`), not a percent. N stays 5: no type has more than 5 numeric controls (Random, Steps and MSEG have exactly 5 with Rate and Div both counted). Not automatable (not parameters): step values, MSEG points, and the buttons (type chip, delete, collapse, count chip).
- **Value curve** (`autoNorm` / `autoValue`): parameter = `norm * 100`, mapped with the same curve as the UI knob (log for `rate` / `attack` / `release`, linear otherwise, integer controls round); a test compares every `ubKnobs` spec with it. **Display**: Live shows percent of the control's travel (`str_for_value`: `50.0 %`), not Hz / ms / dB, because one generic parameter has a different range per type and a saved object's range cannot depend on the type.
- **Names are fixed: `M3 A` .. `M3 E`, `M3 On`, `M3 Shape` / `Sync` / `Bipolar` / `Retrig` / `1Shot` / `Loop` (slot 3) and `Depth 5` (pool slot 5)** (`autoSlotNames`, `autoSwitchName`, `autoDepthName`). **Runtime renaming does not work in Live 12.4.6** (verified in the VM through the bridge, `scripts/probe_megamod_rename_live.js`): `setattr("_parameter_longname", n)` (also with `_parameter_shortname`, `annotation_name`, without the underscore, as messages, followed by toggling `_parameter_invisible` / `_parameter_enable` / `parameter_mappable`) changes the Max object (`getattr` reads the new name back) but `DeviceParameter.name` / `original_name` stay what the file says, after 0.3 s, 3.3 s, `select_device`, device off / on and collapse / expand. So the meaning of a letter depends on the modulator type (`autoControlLabel(type, j)`, e.g. Envelope: A attack, B release, C depth, D gain) and the UI says it in the tooltips: the strip knobs and enable button end with ` | Live: M3 A`, the Depth pill with ` | Live: Depth 5` (or "no Live parameter (pool slot 65+)"). Renaming at device load (from the blob) is not done either: it would not follow a type change. `test_rename` (modes 0..11) and the probe script stay for re-checking a newer Live.
- **Sync rules (no feedback loops)**:
  - UI -> Live: `MM.setParam` / `MM.updateMapping({depth})` **message** the value into the object (`o.message("float", v)`, never `set`), so it outputs and Live records automation when armed (a type change or an undo pushes every control of the slot, switches included; the chips push their switch through `autoPushKey`). The echo (the object outputs it again into `autop` / `autod`) is recognised by value (`autoSent`) and ignored.
  - Live -> model (automation playback, macro, MIDI map, the parameter list, a click on a face): `autop(i, v)` / `autot(i, v)` / `autod(k, v)` -> `MM._autoParamIn` / `_autoSwitchIn` / `_autoDepthIn` write the model (a switch the type does not show, e.g. Envelope `Sync`, is ignored), the engine cfg (or the throttled mapping table) and fire a `param` / `mapping` change for the redraw. They **never push back, never `markDirty` / `notifyclients` (automation must not dirty the Set), never touch the undo stack**; a value that maps to the model's current value is a no-op (integer controls round, so a quantised value does not re-trigger anything).
  - Deleting a modulator leaves its parameter values; undo re-applies them from the snapshot. A released mapping slot's depth parameter goes back to 100 %.
- **Load order** (`autoInit`, when names + LOM are ready): nothing is pushed before `ready`; objects that output while Live restores them are only cached. At `ready`, **if the marker (`ap_marker`, saved with the Set) reads 1 or 2, Live's stored values win** (the switches only at marker 2: a Set saved before the switches existed has them at their initial 0, so its marker 1 keeps the blob's switch values, pushes them and upgrades the marker to 2): every parameter's value (from the restore output, else `getvalueof`) is adopted into the model and the engine (automation or a macro may have moved it; the blob can be stale), a missing value falls back to pushing the blob's. **If the marker is 0** (a new device, or a Set saved before this feature, which has no stored parameter values) **the blob's values are pushed** into the parameters once and the marker is set to 2. A `setvalueof` after ready (preset / Live undo) means **the blob wins** and the parameters follow (`autoPushAll(true)`). Verified in Live (`scripts/test_megamod_reload_live.js`, `duplicate_track` clones a device through its stored state like a Set load: with the model frozen at 70 % while the parameter was set to 30 %, the clone loaded 30 % and `init = {stored: true, marker: 1, cached: 71}`; a fresh device reported `stored: false`). Saving a Set through the UI could not be automated (the File > Save click wrote nothing), so a real save / reopen was not run. Mapping depth parameters belong to the pool slot, and each mapping keeps its slot across deletes and reloads (`mapping.slot`), so a lane on `Depth <k>` stays with its mapping; a new mapping may reuse a freed slot and inherit that slot's (now unbound) lane.
- **Limits and cost**: mappings in pool slots >= 64 keep a UI-editable depth but have no Live parameter. The pool (with the 96 switch parameters) is 1,575 boxes and 1,710 cords: `Mega Modulator.amxd` 961,259 bytes without the pool -> 1,199,351 (161 parameters) -> 1,601,610 bytes (257 parameters). Load in Live (VM, `scripts/measure_megamod_load.py`, 5 runs, set opened -> bridge sees the device's parameters -> script ready): no pool 8.7 / 8.7 s, 161 parameters 8.7 / 8.7 s, 257 parameters 8.6 / 8.6 s median (params visible / ready; bridge up 8.2 s): the difference is below the 0.25 s polling step, the load is dominated by Live and the gen compile.

### Learn
- **Observer**: `init_lom` (from `live.thisdevice`, never `loadbang`) observes `live_set view selected_parameter`. LiveAPI observers pass **one array** (`["selected_parameter", "id", N]` or `["id", N]`); flatten it before parsing.
- **Deferral**: the selection is handled on the next scheduler tick (`Task.schedule(0)`). Never read the LOM or touch objects inside a Live notification.
- **Lookup**: `describeParameter` uses a fresh `new LiveAPI(cb, "id N")`. Reusing a `goto` on a shared API returned nothing.
- **Rejected selections**: duplicates (same modulator + same target) and the device's own parameters that are not valid self-mod targets (`Device On`, `MegaModState`, the marker, anything of the source modulator itself) are ignored. A valid own parameter (a pool parameter of ANOTHER modulator, or any `Depth`) creates a self-mapping of the Options selector's kind (see "Self-modulation").
- **Persistence**: targets are stored by LOM path plus track/device/parameter names. On load they are resolved by path first, then by name among top-level devices. Unresolved mappings are kept and marked `missing`, and a 1.5 s poll revives them or flags them.
- **Target deleted (auto-delete)**: when a mapped device / parameter / track is deleted in Live, its mappings are deleted (`validateTargets` -> `autoDeleteMappings`): both pool boxes get `id 0`, the slot is freed, its depth routes go with it (the cascade), and the whole poll's batch is ONE undo entry (`kind: "tdel"`, label `... (target deleted)`).
  - **Detection**: the 1.5 s poll, no observers. A mapping is deleted only when (1) its target resolved earlier in THIS session (`seenLive`), (2) it was unresolvable on `AUTO_DELETE_POLLS` = 2 consecutive polls (`missPolls`, so about 3 s; a revive resets the count), and (3) the LOM is healthy (`lomHealthy`: `live_set master_track` and this device both resolve; while a Set loads nothing resolves and nothing is deleted). Internal / VIA LIVE self-mappings and depth routes are never auto-deleted by the poll (depth routes only through the cascade).
  - **Moved vs deleted**: a MOVED device keeps its Live id (`move_device` verified in the VM), so the poll follows the id to its new path and refreshes `target.path` / `trackName` / `deviceName` (also onto a track with another name). A target whose id died is DELETED even when a same-named parameter now sits at the old path (the next identical device slid into the deleted one's place; with the old path-first rule the mapping silently jumped onto it): a path match is ignored for a mapping that lost its id (`how === "path"`), a NAME match elsewhere still counts as moved. The lost id is probed again while the mapping is missing (`lostPid`), so a temporary glitch that brings the same object back revives it.
  - **Undo**: `MM.undo()` of a `tdel` entry restores the mappings (their modulator must still exist), then `reresolveRestored` resolves path-then-name again: resolved -> rebound (`seenLive`), unresolved -> kept but marked missing, and NOT auto-deleted again until it has resolved once (so Cmd-Z after Live's own Undo rebinds to the restored device; Cmd-Z while it is still gone keeps red rows). Redo deletes the batch again.
  - **Mappings missing at load (decided)**: kept and marked missing, however long they stay so (the device may have been removed while Mega Modulator was not running and the user may be about to restore it); they revive by path / name when the device returns. Only a deletion OBSERVED during the session (the mapping was resolved once, `seenLive`) auto-deletes.
  - **Learn after a deletion (bug, fixed)**: root cause: `addMapping`'s duplicate check compared `targetKey` = the LOM path only. Deleting device A leaves its mapping's path, and the device after it slides into A's index, so the new learn target has the same path as the dead mapping and was silently refused as a duplicate (`addMapping -> NULL (duplicate/self/no mod)` in the `MM_DEBUG` trace; with an identical-looking neighbour the dead mapping even jumped onto it). SECOND cause, seen in Live with real clicks (trace): after a probe of a dead id (`goto("id N")` on the shared `scratchApi()`), the poll's later `goto("id N")` probes on that shared object returned id 0, so EVERY mapping learned afterwards was flagged missing 1.5 s after it was created (`syncMapping ... active false pid 0`) and auto-deleted / shown red: it looked like the mapping "no longer works". The poll now probes ids with a FRESH `new LiveAPI(fn, "id N")` (like `describeParameter`). The duplicate check now ignores stale mappings (missing, or a different live id for the same path). The learn handler (`selectionChangedBody`) is guarded: an exception disarms a single-shot learn (never half armed), a multi-learn stays armed, an undescribable id leaves it armed; `bindSlot` records an id only when the write succeeded and the poll re-binds any live target whose slot is not bound (self-heal). `validateTargets` handles each mapping in its own try / catch.

### Self-modulation (schema 9): a modulator modulating this device's own controls
One modulator can modulate any automatable parameter of the device itself (LFO 1 -> LFO 2's Rate, an Envelope -> a mapping's Depth, ...), in two interchangeable ways chosen by the Options selector `Self-mod: INTERNAL | VIA LIVE` (`settings.selfMod`, default `internal`; the selector only decides what NEW self-mappings are, see "Switching the selector"). Feedback between modulators (LFO 1 <-> LFO 2) is allowed.
- **Targets = every parameter of the pool**, each with a dest code 0..255 (`selfDestFor` / `selfDestInfo` / `selfDestName` / `selfDestFromName` in `src/megamod_config.js`; the code IS the Live parameter, so a learn only needs the parameter's name): `0..95` = `slot * 6 + j` (`j` 0..4 the generic controls `M<k> A..E`, `j` 5 = `M<k> On`; the same number as the `ap_<i>` object), `96..191` = `96 + slot * 6 + t` (`M<k> Shape / Sync / Bipolar / Retrig / 1Shot / Loop`, the `at_<i>` objects), `192..255` = `192 + k` (`Depth <k+1>`, the Depth of the mapping in pool slot `k`, the `ad_<k>` objects; since schema 10 an INTERNAL learn on one of these makes a depth route, see "Depth-modulation routes", and engine destinations `192..447` address the depth of any of the 256 pool slots). Not targets: the marker, `Device On`, `MegaModState`, step values and MSEG points (not parameters).
- **Learn gesture** (both kinds): arm LRN, then touch the knob (a click / drag on a drawn control's transparent face selects that Live parameter, so the `selected_parameter` observer sees the device's own parameter id). `_selectionChanged` no longer drops own parameters: `classifySelfParam(name, srcMod, state)` accepts a pool parameter and rejects the others, a **self-loop** (any control, switch or On of the SOURCE modulator itself: a modulator that switches itself off stops producing the signal that would switch it on again, and a control driven by its own output is a degenerate loop) and an empty modulator slot. Another modulator's controls, its `On`, every switch and any `Depth` are fine. Duplicates (same source + same dest code, in either kind) are ignored (`targetKey` = `self:<code>`). The mapping is made by `MM.addSelfMapping(mod, dest[, kind, info])` (kind default `settings.selfMod`); `addMapping` still refuses an own parameter (an internal target always goes through `addSelfMapping`).
- **INTERNAL (default)**: `target = {internal: true, selfDest, kind, slot, ctl, path: "", paramName: "M2 A", ...}` (`makeInternalTarget(code)`), Mod only (`sanitizeMapping` forces `mode: "mod"`; the REM tab / the list's M/R chip are inert and say "Mod only"), the usual polarity / range / depth / MSEG shape / enable. It uses **no pool slot, no live.* object, no Live id**: it lives in the engine's internal matrix (route `mapping.slot` 0..63, `MM.internalCapacity()`; the 65th fires change kind `"full"` with `id -1`, the UI pill reads "Internal routes full (64)", and no pool slot is consumed). Status: `ok` / `disabled` / `missing` (never `pending`).
  - **Buffers** (`megamodint` 512 floats, `megamodicurve` 64 x 256; `INT_*` in config, static-checked by `tests/test_megamod.js` against the genexpr): route `r` at `4r` = `[source + 1 (0 = off), dest code, 0, 0]`, `[256]` = highest used route + 1 (0 = the engine skips the whole matrix), `[264 + (type * 5 + j) * 5 + {0..4}]` = control descriptor `[CF field + 1, lo, hi, log, int]` per type / generic control (from `AUTO_KEYS` and `MOD_PARAM_RANGE`, so the engine has no second copy of the ranges), `[414 + t]` / `[420 + t]` = switch `t`'s CF field + 1 and max (shape 4, toggles 1). `megamodicurve[r * 256 ..]` is the route's transfer table, the same `mappingTable()` and `(x+1)/2 * 256` convention as the router, depth baked in (an internal mapping has no Depth parameter). `MM.flushInternal` writes everything on load / names / ready; a disabled mapping, a disabled source or a missing destination writes source 0 (the route is off, so a table read of a silent source cannot leave a stale offset).
  - **Engine** (`src/megamod_engine.genexpr`, step 3c, before the slots are evaluated): per sample, every active route reads its source's **previous-sample** output (`outv` still holds last sample's value when the matrix runs, so loops are stable with one sample of delay), maps it through its table (`o` in -1..1 = the Mod offset, i.e. `range * (...)` x depth) and the offsets of all routes into one destination are **summed** (`dsum` / `dcl`, one destination is applied once); a DEPTH destination (`192+`) instead **multiplies** the routes' baked factors (`dprd`, see "Depth-modulation routes"). The slots read `cm`, a modulated copy of the config cache `cf` (`cm` is refreshed together with `cf`, every 16 samples per slot, so a route that goes away restores the stored value within 16 samples); the matrix only overwrites the modulated fields, so the CPU cost with no routes is one `peek` of the route count per sample.
  - **Scaling per control (normalized knob space)**: a numeric control A..E is offset in its **normalized 0..1 knob space**, using the UI knob's own curve (`autoNorm` / `autoValue`: log for `rate` / `attack` / `release`, linear otherwise): `n = curve(stored)`, `n' = clip(n + o, 0, 1)`, `value = curve^-1(n')` (integer controls: Steps count, Div, Seed are rounded) and the result is used directly (clamped to the control's range by construction). One octave of an LFO's Rate is therefore `ln 2 / ln 4000` = 0.0836 of the knob (log 0.01..40 Hz) = a mapping range of 8.4 %: `range 0.0836` on LFO 2's Rate makes it swing between 1/2 x and 2 x. The rate multiplier (`ratemul`) is applied after, to the modulated rate; when the LFO is **synced** the Rate control (A) is not what runs it (the Div control B is: a mapping on B modulates the division index, on A it has no audible effect, and the UI draws the Rate knob's face as B). `selfCtlValue(type, j, base, off)` is the JS reference; the tests compare it with the descriptor-driven formula for every type, control, offset and base.
  - **Toggles, On and the Shape enum**: modulated by thresholding the modulated normalized value, per sample: a toggle / `On` is on while `clip(stored + o, 0, 1) > 0.5` (so a stored-off toggle with `+-` range 1 turns on while the source is above 0.5; a stored-on one turns off while it is below -0.5); `Shape` steps through its 5 values: `round(clip(shape / 4 + o, 0, 1) * 4)`. A mid-run `Sync` flip re-anchors the phase (no special handling), a `Retrig` / `1Shot` / `Loop` flip takes effect at the next cycle event.
  - **Depth targets** (extended to all 256 pool slots in schema 10, see "Depth-modulation routes"): every pool slot applies its Depth **in the router per sample** (all 8 routers, `router_gen_code`): the table is baked at depth 1 and the router computes Mod `y * d`, Remote `c + (y - c) * d` from `route[2k+1]` (the effective depth, written by the engine: base from `route[512 + k]` (mapper, = the mapping's depth) TIMES the product of the depth routes' factors, clipped -1..1; one slot per sample is also copied base -> effective, so a base change arrives within 256 samples = 5 ms) and `route[768 + k]` (Remote centre `c = (min + max) / 2`, or -1 for Mod). The `route` buffer stays 1024 floats: `[0..511]` source / effective depth of 256 slots, `[512..767]` base depth, `[768..1023]` Remote centre (the constant keeps the name `ROUTE_DEPTH_MIN`). The router sees the engine's value at the end of the audio vector (no per-sample alignment between two `gen~` objects), so a depth modulation is block-rate stepped (about 1.3 ms at 64 samples: fine for LFO rates, audible as zipper only for audio-rate depth modulation). Writing the depth is two floats (`writeDepthCells`), never a table.
  - **Telemetry** (`megamodscope`, 128 -> 512 -> 640 floats): `128 + 12 * slot + [0..4]` = the effective normalized A..E, `+ 5` the effective On, `+ 6..11` the effective switches (shape 0..4, toggles 0/1); `320 + k` = the effective Depth of pool slot `k` (`k` 0..255 since schema 10) (the engine's `cm` after modulation; `MM.readTelemetry()` -> `tel.eff[slot] = {ctl, on, sw}`, `tel.depth[k]`).
- **VIA LIVE**: a normal pool mapping (`mode` Mod or Remote, a pool slot, `live.modulate~` / `live.remote~`, `Depth <k>` lane and all) whose target is the device's own Live parameter, `target.selfDest = code` marks it (`ownTarget()` finds the parameter by name on `this_device`, `bindOwnPid`). Measured in Live 12.4.6 (VM, `scripts/test_megamod_selfmod_live.js`, evidence below): **modulating the device's own parameter works**: the pool's `live.modulate~` is bound to the device's own parameter id, the LOM `DeviceParameter.value` keeps the STORED value while the parameter's `live.dial` **outputs the modulated value** into `autop` / `autot` / `autod`, and the engine follows through that path. The rule that makes this safe (`viaDests`, `viaIn`, `rebuildViaDests` in the mapper): for a destination with an active **Mod** via-Live self-mapping, an incoming value goes to the engine's cfg float (or the router's base-depth cell) **only** - never to `mod.p` / `mapping.depth`, the undo stack, the dirty state or `autoCache`; the stored value is re-read from the LOM (`value`, 4 Hz `Task`, `viaReadStored`) so a real change of it (face drag, automation, macro) still reaches the model; a cfg rewrite of the slot re-applies the modulated value (`reapplyVia`); when the modulation stops (mapping deleted / disabled / converted / source off / type change) `restoreDest` writes the model's value back. A **Remote** via-Live mapping takes the parameter over (its value really moves, like automation), so it uses the normal path and the model follows it. VIA LIVE modulation updates at Live's control rate (not per sample) and costs a pool slot + two live objects; it is also visible in Live's own modulation UI and can be mapped / automated by Live, which INTERNAL cannot.
- **Rows**: INTERNAL rows carry a green loop glyph, VIA LIVE rows an orange `L` badge (list, maps popover, remap editor header: `internal route` / `via Live: M2 B`); the target is named by meaning, `LFO 2 Rate` / `Follow On` / `Wobble Depth` (for a Depth: the mapped parameter's name, `Depth 5` when the slot is empty; `MM.selfLabel(m)`, `selfDestLabel`), via Live with the parameter in brackets (`LFO 2 Rate (M2 A)`); in a narrow column the control alone is shown (`ubSelfFit`: `Rate`, `Shape`, `On`) and the hover text / footer spells everything out. **Rings**: a knob whose control is the destination of an ACTIVE self-mapping (`ubModKinds`, rebuilt each paint) draws a faint ring (`r + 3`) in the mapping's colour (internal green, via Live orange), a bright arc from the stored value to the live (modulated) value and a white dot at the latter; a modulated switch chip is outlined while its live state is on (with a corner dot), the `On` glyph and the remap editor's Depth pill (a tick at the live depth) get the same markers. Nothing is drawn for a disabled mapping / source or a missing control.
- **Switching the selector** (`MM.setSelfMod(kind[, convert = true])`, Options row 4): applies to NEW self-mappings, and **converts every existing self-mapping of the other kind** (the answer to "offer to convert": it always converts, as ONE undo step `{kind: "conv"}`, announced by a note pill "Converted N self-mappings (undo with Cmd-Z)"; Cmd-Z puts every mapping back exactly (targets, ranges, shapes, pool slots, Live ids) and Shift-Cmd-Z converts again). The selector itself is a setting, not part of the undo stack. Id, source, polarity, range, depth, shape and enable are kept. INTERNAL -> VIA LIVE: Mod stays Mod, the mapping takes a free pool slot and binds the own parameter. VIA LIVE -> INTERNAL: a **Remote** mapping becomes the closest Mod: an absolute window `min..max` becomes an offset of the same span in the same direction (`+` with range `max - min`, or `-` with range `min - max` for an inverted window; the offset is applied around the stored value, so it is the nearest equivalent, not an identical value curve). A conversion that finds no room (internal matrix full / pool full) leaves that mapping as it was and fires `"full"`.
- **Edge cases (decided)**: *Deleting a modulator* (UI X / Delete key / `removeModulator`) deletes the mappings it SOURCES and the self-mappings that TARGET it (`mappingsTouching`), as one undo entry; undo restores all of them in their old list order with their routes / pool slots, and if the old slot was taken meanwhile the modulator returns to the next free slot and the restored self-mappings that targeted it are re-aimed at it (`retargetSelf`). *Changing a slot's type* keeps its self-mappings and matches them **by position** (A..E, switch index): a mapping whose control exists in the new type keeps modulating whatever sits at that position (A is Rate for an LFO, Attack for an Envelope), the others become **missing** (red row, route off, no Live id bound) and revive automatically when the type changes back (`selfDestExists`; the Envelope has no E, Sync, Shape...). *Rate multiplier and sync* still apply (above). *Saving / loading* round-trips both kinds (`settings.selfMod`; the internal target is its dest code, the via-Live one a normal target plus `selfDest`; internal routes get their saved `mapping.slot` back, via-Live mappings their pool slot). *Caps*: 64 internal routes (the `"full"` event with `id -1`), 256 pool mappings. A modulator cannot target itself (see Learn), but it can target other modulators' Depth, On and switches.
- **GenExpr gotcha (Max 9)**: two helper functions (`normOf(raw, lo, hi, lg)` called with `peek(...)` expressions as arguments, `valOf(n, lo, hi, lg, it)` with five plain arguments) made the whole `gen.codebox~` silently produce zeros (every output 0, no error in the harness; the same `normOf` with plain variables was fine, so the trigger is not understood). The matrix therefore inlines its math; `tests/test_megamod.js` forbids those two function names. To bisect such a failure, the audio runner takes `MEGAMOD_GEN=<file>` (another engine file) and `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh audio [case ...]` runs it in the VM.

### Depth-modulation routes (schema 10): a modulator modulating the Depth of another mapping
Depth used to be modulatable only through a self-mapping onto the `Depth k` parameter (pool slots `0..63`, addressed by slot). Since schema 10 it is a first-class feature.
- **Model**: a depth route is an INTERNAL mapping whose target is `{internal: true, kind: "depth", mapId}` (`makeDepthTarget`, `isDepthRoute`, `depthRoutesOf` in `src/megamod_config.js`). It follows the target mapping **by id** across deletes, reloads and slot changes. Source = any modulator. It has the usual polarity / range / shape / depth / enabled, is Mod only (`sanitizeMapping` forces it, the REM tab and M/R chip are inert), and uses no pool slot, no `live.*` object and no Live id. It is **not** a self-mapping (`isSelfMapping` is false), so the INTERNAL / VIA LIVE selector never converts it and `classifySelfParam` never sees it. `MM.addDepthRoute(i, mapId)`, `MM.depthRoutesFor(mapId)`, `MM.depthTargetOf(m)`, `MM.depthRouteLabel(m)` ("Frequency Depth"), `MM.effectiveDepth(mapId)` (the engine telemetry of a modulated mapping, else its own depth).
- **Decisions**: (1) **Self-loop rule: allowed.** A modulator may modulate the depth of its OWN mapping (tremolo of self): depth only scales the router output, which never feeds the engine, so there is no feedback loop (unlike a control modulated by its own output, which `classifySelfParam` still refuses). (2) **Depth routes are MULTIPLICATIVE (VCA-like), several routes onto one mapping multiply**; the effective depth is `clamp(mapping.depth * prod m_k, 0, 1)` (`effectiveDepth(base, factors)` is the JS reference), with the factor of route k `m = range * dep * y` (UNIPOLAR, 0..range, no polarity; no `(1 - range)` offset; formulas below). The same source twice on one mapping is a duplicate (`targetKey` = `depth:<mapId>`) and is refused. (3) The target must be a **pool mapping** (a normal or a VIA LIVE one): an INTERNAL route has no router stage (its depth is baked in its table) and a route has no depth of its own to modulate. (4) **Cap**: the 64 internal routes stay shared with the self-mod routes (`"full"` with id -1, pill "Internal routes full (64)"); raising it would shift the packed `megamodint` / `megamodicurve` layout (4 floats x 64 routes then the count at 256 and the descriptors at 264), which was not worth it. (5) Deleting the target mapping deletes its routes (`deleteMapping`), deleting the source modulator deletes its routes (`mappingsTouching`), deleting the modulator that SOURCES the target mapping deletes the routes of every other modulator onto it; each is ONE undo step and undo restores everything in the old list order (a mapping that comes back in another pool slot gets its routes re-aimed). A VIA LIVE `Depth k` mapping stays what it was (a pool mapping on the Live parameter); an INTERNAL learn on a `Depth k` parameter (or converting such a VIA LIVE mapping to INTERNAL) now creates a depth route on the mapping that owns slot `k` (refused when the slot is empty).
- **Schema 10 migration** (`migrateDepthRoutes`, run by `normalizeState` on every load): an INTERNAL self-mapping with `selfDest 192 + k` becomes a depth route on the pool mapping whose `mapping.slot` is `k` (source, polarity, range, depth, shape, enabled and its matrix route kept); one whose slot has no mapping is dropped (it modulated nothing); orphan routes (unknown `mapId`, or a target that is not a pool mapping) and duplicates are dropped on load too. Tested: schema 9 -> 10, VIA LIVE `Depth k` untouched, round trip.
- **Engine / router**: the engine's internal matrix now has 448 destination cells (`dsum` / `dcl` 512, `INT_DEST_COUNT`): `192 + k` for `k` 0..255 is a depth destination, written by the mapper as `depthRouteDest(slotOf[mapId])` whenever the target's slot changes (`syncDepthRoutesFor`, also at the end of `rebuildAllSlots` and `releaseMappingSlot`, where the route goes off). For a depth destination the matrix does not sum: the mapper bakes each depth route's FACTOR table (`depthFactorTable`, `m` per table position) into `megamodicurve`, the engine multiplies them (`dprd` 512, reset to 1 with `dsum` / `dcl`) and writes `clip(route[512 + k] * product, 0, 1)` per sample to `route[2k+1]` (`step 3c`); step 3b copies base -> effective for one of 256 slots per sample; telemetry writes four depth cells per sample (`320 + ts + 64 q`). The matrix is still skipped when no route exists (one `peek`). All 8 routers read `peek(route, 2k+1)` and `peek(route, 768+k)` for each of their 32 slots (a fixed number of peeks, inlined; no helper function, see the GenExpr gotcha). `ROUTE_DEPTH_MIN` moved 576 -> 768, `SCOPE_SAMPS` 512 -> 640.
- **UI: learn onto a mapping row + sub-rows** (`ubDmodAssign(src, mapping)` -> `MM.addDepthRoute`, `ubDmodArmedAssign`, `ubSubClick` in `src/megamod_ui.js`). **Assign**: arm LRN (Shift = multi) with modulator X, then click a mapping row (the name area) in the LST list or in the maps popover: X modulates that mapping's depth (a single-shot disarms; the source is the modulator LRN was armed **from**, `ubDmodSrc`, so a multi-learn that retargets with the selection still assigns from X; a duplicate or an INTERNAL row is refused with a note pill). Armed hover outlines the row in violet (`UB_DEPTH_COL`). **Show**: under each mapping row with routes an indented sub-row `elbow arrow, power toggle, source dot + name, small amount bar (drag = range), value, x` (`ubSubParts`, 13 px `UB_ROW_SUB`, hit kinds `ds_tog` / `ds_bar` / `ds_del` / `ds_row`); in the popover the same on 19 px rows (`ubPopSubParts`, `mp_ds_*`). The x deletes the route (undoable). A route is also a row of its SOURCE modulator's list and popover (violet `D` marker, name `<parameter> Depth`, inert M chip), edited in the remap editor like any internal route (the header line `< depth route` is a back link to the mapping it modulates, hit id `dback`; `ubPbSelMapping()` accepts a route whose TARGET mapping belongs to the selected modulator). The Depth pill is unchanged (value only). Parameter faces stay hidden while a menu or the popover is open (`ubFacesWanted`). Preview flags: `UB_PREVIEW_DMOD = 1` adds routes with LRN armed on Dice (`UB_PREVIEW_DMOD_B = true`: the maps popover with sub-rows); the committed default is 0 (the LST inspector, no routes).
- **Remote + depth route (regression, user report "Remote 0..1 with a +-15 % depth route sits at min")**: not reproducible in the engine, the router, the mapper or real Live; the checks pin it. Audio `remote_depth` (`python3 scripts/run_megamod_audio_tests.py remote_depth`): Remote 0..1 (and 0.2..0.9) and Mod mappings in slots 5 / 70 / 96 with and without a +-15 % route, base depth 1 / 0.8 / 0.5; the recovered per-sample depth (`[2k+1]`) matches `clip(base + route)` within 0.01 and a Remote 0..1 output spans 0..1. `tests/test_megamod.js` ("REMOTE mapping + depth route") checks the router cells `[2k]` / `[2k+1]` / `[512+k]` / `[768+k]` for slots < 64 and >= 64, before / after adding the route, after a base / min change and after a reload. `scripts/test_megamod_remote_depth_live.js` (under the Live lock, `FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live` then `run`): Remote onto a real Live Delay `L Time` (stopped / playing / 3 earlier mappings, a `duplicate_track` Set-load clone) with and without the route; `L Time` normalized sweeps 0..1. Notes: Delay `L Time` is an exponential 1 ms..5 s parameter (normalized 0.5 is about 70 ms, not 2.5 s); a learn ignores a selection equal to the previous one (`lastSelectedId`), so select another parameter first in scripted runs.
- **Depth semantics (revised after schema 10, no schema bump)**. A is a modulator with a mapping, B modulates that mapping's depth.
  1. **Multiplicative (VCA-like), not additive; the route's range is the depth span B controls (final spec).** **A depth route is always UNIPOLAR and has no polarity choice** (changed: it used to take `+` / `±` / `−`, which let the depth go negative and invert A). The factor is **`m_k = range * dep * y`** (`y` = the route's shape at B's normalized output `u = (B + 1) / 2`, 0..1; `dep` = the route's own Depth, 1 by default), so `m` is in **0..range**: B at its minimum gives 0, B at its maximum gives `range` (`depthFactor(route, x)` / `depthFactorTable`, baked by `syncInternal` for depth routes instead of `mappingTable`; `depthFactor` forces polarity `+` itself, and `mappingOutput` of a depth route does the same; there is **no `(1 - range)` offset**). The effective depth is **`eff = clamp(base * prod m_k, 0, 1)`** (`effectiveDepth(base, factors)`, also floors a stray negative factor at 0; base depth is already 0..1); with no route `eff = base`. `eff` is never negative, so a depth route never inverts the mapping (Mod `o * eff`; Remote c + (lookup - c) * eff stays inside its bounds).
     - **Definition (the user's three cases; A = a Remote mapping, B modulates A's depth through a route; "B is range X" = the route's range, the sub-row bar):**
       1. A 0..100 %, route range 0 -> A always outputs **50 %** (`m = 0`, depth 0, Remote sits at the centre).
       2. A 25..100 %, route range 0 -> A always outputs **62.5 %**.
       3. A 0..100 %, route range 50 %, B sweeping its full range -> A stays within **25..75 %** (`m = 0.5 y`, `0 <= eff <= 0.5`, `v = 0.5 + (lookup - 0.5) * eff`: at B's peak `eff = 0.5` and A reaches both 25 and 75 when its own lookup is at an end; at B's minimum `eff = 0` and A is centred at 50 %). For range 0 the Remote sits at `c` = 50 % (62.5 % for bounds 25..100 %): `m = 0`, `eff = 0`, `v = c`.
     - A route's own Depth 0 or Range 0 gives `m = 0` (A's depth 0). Several routes multiply (one factor of 0 zeroes the depth, so range 0 on any route holds A at its centre / 0; the product of 0..range factors stays in 0..1, never negative). Full range (`range = 1`) lets B control the whole depth: `m = (B + 1) / 2` for a linear shape.
     - The sub-row amount bar edits `range` (same widgets and fields as before, no layout change): a FULL bar lets B control the whole depth (`m` = `(B + 1) / 2`, 0..1), a bar at 0 shuts A's depth, a bar at 50 % limits the depth to 0..0.5.
  2. **Remote output is bipolar around the CENTRE of its bounds.** `lookup = min + (max - min) * y` (the table baked at depth 1), `c = (min + max) / 2` (`remoteCentre(m)`), **`v = c + (lookup - c) * eff`** (base and modulated depth alike). Depth 0 with bounds 0..100 % gives 0.5, bounds 25..100 % give 0.625, depth 0.5 on 0..1 spans 0.25..0.75; depth 1 is unchanged. In the router `route[768 + k]` stores the centre (-1 for Mod, the sentinel still works because a centre is >= 0); Mod stays `out = lookup * eff`. `mappingOutput(m, x, depOverride)` (the third argument is the effective depth, 0..1, used by the UI), `mappingTable` and `writeDepthCells` follow.
  3. **Schema decision: no bump (stays 10).** Nothing new is stored. **Behaviour change**: a saved Remote mapping with base depth < 1 now scales around the centre instead of toward min (intended, no compatibility shim), and an old saved depth route (`range` r) now means `m = r * y` (multiplicative and unipolar, the final spec; earlier revisions had `m = r * B` with a polarity, `m = (1 - r) + r * B`, and before that `+- r` added to the base depth). Mod mappings' base depth (`o *= depth`) is unchanged.
  4. **UI**: the Remote output bar's fill runs from the centre to the live value (`ubPaintAbsMeter`) and both output bars (Mod centred bar, Remote bar) use the effective, modulated depth (`MM.effectiveDepth`). The Depth pill's live tick uses the clamped `eff` (0..1, `MM.effectiveDepth`); all of these read the engine's effective depth, so they follow `m = range * dep * y` with no UI formula of their own. The range readout (`20-80%`) and the popover bar show the bounds, which are unchanged.
  5. **Unipolar depth routes: migration and UI (no schema bump, stays 10).** **Migration choice**: nothing is rewritten. An old depth route saved with polarity `±` / `−` keeps its stored `polarity` field (never deleted) but it is IGNORED for depth routes (`mappingOutput`, `depthFactor`, the UI helper `ubPol(m)` = 2 for a depth route); on load it behaves as `+`: `m = range * y`. The old `±` meaning (`m = range * (2y - 1)`, negative for B below the centre) is gone on purpose. **UI**: a depth route shows NO `[+][±][−]` control: the remap editor's tab row omits the `pol` cells (`ubDetailRects`, no hit / paint, a forced polarity click is ignored), the maps popover row skips `ubPopPolButton` (the `pol` column stays empty so columns never move; `mp_pol` is not hit and its click handler ignores routes), and the sub-rows never had one. Readouts have no sign: `ubValueText` gives `40%` (not `+40%` / `±40%` / `−40%`) in the list row, sub-row, popover and editor readout. Bars are 0..100: the sub-row / popover bar fills from the LEFT edge to `range` (`ubHBarModValue(r, 2, x)`), the editor's vertical bar fills from the BOTTOM to `range` (`ubRangeValue(rect, 2, y)`, one marker, live fill = `depthFactor`, `ubPaintOutMeter`). Tests: `tests/test_megamod.js` (formula for several ranges and B values, the cases, a 600-configuration fuzz with up to 4 routes and any stored polarity: `eff` always in 0..1 and Remote inside its bounds, old `±` / `−` routes load unipolar), `tests/test_megamod_ui.js` (no polarity cells in the editor, sub-row and popover, unsigned readouts); audio `remote_depth` / `depthmod_hi` / `depthmod_sum` / `selfmod_depth` check the measured depth stays in 0..1 and match `clip(base * prod range * (B + 1) / 2, 0, 1)`; Live `scripts/test_megamod_dmod_live.js` / `scripts/test_megamod_remote_depth_live.js` check the cases and that the effective depth never goes below 0 on a real Delay `L Time` mapping.
- **Tests**: `tests/test_megamod.js` (20,779 checks in total): depth-route model and engine matrix (dest `192 + slot`, slots >= 64), refusals, the factor `m = o` / effective depth `eff` for every polarity and range (range 0 -> m 0, `±` range 0.5 -> 0.5 B, `+` 0..range, `-` -range..0, several routes multiply, clamp, inversion), the three cases as JS reference (Remote 0..1 and 0.25..1, route range 0 / 0.5, B at 0 / +-1 / 0.5) and their Mod-mode equivalents, Remote centring (0 -> 0.5 for 0..1, 0.625 for 0.25..1, 0.5 spans 0.25..0.75), the router cells (`[768+k]` = centre) for slots < 64 and >= 64 and after a reload, delete / undo / redo cascades (target mapping, source modulator, the modulator that owns the target mapping), a slot change (undo into another slot) and reload (also a blob that moved the slot), the schema 9 -> 10 migration, learn / selector interplay, the static contract (all 8 routers read `route[2k+1]` / `route[768+k]` for their 32 slots, `dsum` / `dcl` 512, the 256-slot refresh and telemetry). `tests/test_megamod_ui.js` (12,210 checks in total; incl. the Remote output bar's centre fill at depth 1 / 0.5 / 0): the LRN gesture creates the route (single-shot and multi, LST list and popover), sub-rows paint and edit (bar, toggle, delete + undo), the route's remap editor edits the route, hit areas do not overlap and everything is inside the device and 161 px (rows, sub-rows, popover rows, the editor rects), faces hidden while the popover is open. Real Max (VM, `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh audio depthmod_hi depthmod_sum`): `depthmod_hi` = LFO 1 (1 Hz, `±` range 1, m = sin) modulates the depth of a mapping in pool slot 96 (>= 64; its source is an 8 Hz sine, base depth 0.8): the per-cycle |peak| of the router output follows `0.8 m` (-0.8..0.8, inverting); `depthmod_sum` = two `±` range-1 routes (1 Hz and 1.7 Hz, base 1) MULTIPLY (the product of the two sines); `selfmod_depth` = `±` range 0.5, eff = 0.5 sin; `remote_depth` = 20 runs (Remote / Mod mappings in slots 5 / 70 / 96): the full-range VCA scenario (A = 3 Hz sine Remote 0..1, B = 1 Hz sine through a full-range `±` route: the output is 0.5 wherever B ~ 0), `+` route, two routes, base depth 0 (holds the centre), 0.5 (spans 0.25..0.75), and the three cases verbatim (`case1_A0_100_range0` holds 0.5, `case2_A25_100_range0` holds 0.625, `case3_A0_100_range50` spans 0.25..0.75); `mapping_depth` Remote is now `c + 0.5 (full - c)`. All 22 audio cases pass. Live (VM, under the Live lock): `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-dmod -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh run scripts/test_megamod_dmod_live.js'` (`scripts/test_megamod_dmod_live.js`, new hooks `test_dmodgeom` / `test_dmodroute`; the `test_report` mappings carry `depthRoute`, `nRoutes`, `effDepth`) drives the learn gesture with real OS clicks (LST rail button, LFO 1's tab, LRN, LFO 2's tab, the mapping's row) -> ONE depth route (internal, mapId = the mapping, learn disarmed, a sub-row), the telemetry's effective depth moves -0.5..0.5 with LFO 1 (a 1 Hz sine, base 1, route `±` range 0.5: m = 0.5 sin), delete / undo through the hooks, then the three cases on a real Delay `L Time` (normalized LOM value; B a slow +-1 square): case 1 holds ~0.5, case 2 ~0.625, case 3 stays within ~0.25..0.75 and reaches near both ends; `scripts/test_megamod_remote_depth_live.js` runs the same three cases as well (both variants). Results are in the task report.

### State
- The `MegaModState` blob (`parameter_type: 3`, v8ui `getvalueof` / `setvalueof`) holds the versioned JSON state, which covers the modulators, the mappings and an opaque `ui` object (selection, expanded strip, list open, scroll, selected mapping, strip order). Older versions still parse.
- **Schema 10** (`MEGAMOD_VERSION`): adds depth-modulation routes (target `{internal: true, kind: "depth", mapId}`, see "Depth-modulation routes"). **Migration**: internal self-mappings that targeted `Depth k` become routes on the mapping in slot `k` (dropped when the slot is empty), orphans / duplicates are dropped; every older state loads unchanged.
- **Schema 9** (`MEGAMOD_VERSION` was 9 until schema 10): adds `settings.selfMod` (`"internal"` | `"live"`, default `"internal"`) and self-mappings (`target.internal`, `target.selfDest` 0..255, derived `kind` / `slot` / `ctl`; an internal mapping is always `mode: "mod"` and its `slot` is its internal route 0..63). **Migration**: a state of any older schema has no `selfMod` (`sanitizeSettings` -> `internal`) and no self-mappings, so every old set loads unchanged; `tests/test_megamod.js` checks schema 8 -> 9. Everything below is unchanged.
- **Schema 8**: adds `mapping.depth` (0..1, default 1 = 100 %, sanitized by `sanitizeMapping`). **Migration**: a mapping without `depth` (any older schema) loads at 1, so every old table and output is unchanged. `mappingOutput` applies it: **Mod** `o = depth * range * (...)` (so depth 0.5 halves the modulation, 0 is none); **Remote** `v = clamp(min + (max - min) * y * depth, 0, 1)` as first shipped (the span shrank toward `min`); **superseded: it now scales around the centre, `v = c + (min + (max - min) * y - c) * depth` with `c = (min + max) / 2`** (see "Depth semantics (revised after schema 10)"; depth 0 parks the target at `c`, 1:1 at depth 1). Tables are rewritten when depth changes (`writeMappingTable` through `syncMapping`; Live-driven changes are throttled to one rewrite per 33 ms with a trailing edge, `scheduleDepthTable`). Superseded in part by schema 9's router: for pool slots below 64 the table is baked at depth 1 and the Depth is two floats in `megamodroute` (`ROUTE_DEPTH_BASE` 512 + k, `ROUTE_DEPTH_MIN` 576 + k) applied per sample in the router (`writeDepthCells`; no table rewrite, so the 33 ms throttle now only guards the cells); slots 64+ keep the baked table. Audio-verified (`mapping_depth`, router 3 = slots 96+, unchanged; `selfmod_depth`, router 0).
- **Schema 7**: adds the device-global `settings` object `{defaultMode: "mod"|"remote", smoothMs: 0..500, rateMul: 0.25..4}` (defaults `mod`, 0, 1; `sanitizeSettings` clamps). **Migration**: a state of any older schema has no `settings`, so `normalizeState` applies the defaults and keeps everything else. `MM.getSettings()` / `MM.setSettings(patch)` (clamp, fire `"settings"`, mark dirty, and send `smooth` / `gen ratemul` only when a value changed); `MM.sendSettings()` re-sends both on load, on names-ready and on ready. Settings are not part of the undo stack.
- **Schema 6**: a mapping is `{id, mod, enabled, mode, polarity, range, min, max, shape, target}`; `amount`, `invert` and `bipolar` are gone. `min` / `max` (0..1, defaults 0 / 1) are the independent absolute limits used **only in Remote**; `polarity` and `range` are used only in Mod and are ignored (but kept, so switching mode restores them) in Remote. `polarity` is `1` (`+`), `0` (`±`) or `−1` (`−`); `range` is 0..1 (the limit marker's distance from the output bar's centre, the whole Mod depth). Defaults: `±`, range 0.5 (the same depth as the old amount 0.5 × range 1). With `y = evalMseg(shape, (x+1)/2)`:
  - signed modulation `o = range * (polarity === 0 ? 2y-1 : polarity * y)`, in −1..1.
  - **Mod**: `mappingOutput = o` (the offset `live.modulate~` gets).
  - **Remote** (`live.remote~ @normalized`, absolute 0..1): `mappingOutput = clamp(min + (max − min) * y, 0, 1)`; polarity / range are ignored.
  - **Migration schema < 6 (amount folded into range)**, Mod and Remote mappings alike: `range' = clamp(|amount| * range, 0, 1)`. If `amount < 0`, `+` and `−` swap polarity; for `±` the shape is flipped vertically (`y → 1 − y` on every point; `curve` / `sCurve` stay as they are, because the segment easing is a fraction of the y span, so `evalMseg` of the flipped points is exactly `1 − y`). Tables match the old `amount * range * (...)` within 1e-9 (measured 2.2e-16 over every polarity, amount sign 1 / 0.5 / 0.2 / −0.2 / −0.5 / −1 and range). Remote tables are unchanged (the folded polarity / range keep a later switch back to Mod identical to the old Mod output). Older schemas run through the chain below first. A stray `amount` key in a schema 6 object is ignored.
  - **Migration schema 4 → 5** (remote mappings only, `a` = old amount, `r` = old range, each result clamped to 0..1; derived before the schema 6 fold): `+` min 0, max `a·r`; `−` min 1, max `1 − a·r`; `±` min `0.5 − a·r/2`, max `0.5 + a·r/2`. Exact (tables within 1e-9) unless a clamp bites, which needs a negative amount on `+` / `−` (the old output was clamped per sample, min / max clamp the endpoints, so e.g. `+` with amount < 0 becomes a constant 0, the same response). Mod mappings just gain min 0 / max 1. Schema < 4 goes through the migration below first (it yields `+`, range 1, amount 1, i.e. min 0 / max 1 with the old limits baked into the shape).
  - **Migration (schema < 4)**: the old affine parts are baked into the shape's point y values (an affine map of the y values affinely maps `evalMseg` for every bend and S-curve, since the segment easing does not depend on y). Mod: `y' = lo + (hi-lo) * (invert ? 1-y : y)` with `lo/hi = min/max` (schema 3) or `0/1` (schema < 3, where Mod ignored them), `polarity = bipolar ? 0 : 1`, range 1, amount unchanged. Remote: `y' = min + (max-min) * (invert ? 1-y : y)`, `polarity 1`, range 1, amount 1, so `v = y'`, the old value. Schema 1 curve fields go through `curveToShape` first. `tests/test_megamod.js` checks schema 1 / 2 / 3 × Mod / Remote × invert × bipolar × min/max (max table error about 2e-16). Raw objects without `polarity` handed straight to `mappingOutput` / `mappingTable` still use the legacy formula.
- **Schema 5 (superseded)**: Mod used `o = amount * range * (...)` with `amount` −1..1 (default 0.5, range default 1).
- **Schema 4 (superseded)**: Remote used polarity / range / amount (see the migration above).
- **Schema 3 (superseded)**: `min` / `max` were range markers in both modes (Schema < 3 Mod mappings, whose min/max were ignored, loaded the full range).
- **Schema 2**: each mapping carries `shape` (MSEG points `{x,y,curve,sCurve}`) instead of `curve` / `curveAmt` / `curveSteps`. **Migration**: a mapping without `shape` is converted by `curveToShape(curve, curveAmt, curveSteps)` in `sanitizeMapping`. Exp / Log / S with power up to `2^3.6` become one segment (exact, same power law), steeper ones 32 fitted segments, Stepped becomes hard steps (two points per step, `0.0001` apart at the edges). `tests/test_megamod.js` checks every old curve type against the legacy `shapeCurve` formula (max table error below 0.02). `shapeCurve` is kept only as that reference.

### Debugging
- **Debug trace**: `dlog()` in the mapper is silenced by `MM_DEBUG = false`. Set it to `true` (and optionally `MM_DEBUG_LOG` to an absolute file path) to trace learn, slot binding and target resolution, or switch it on at runtime in Live with the hook `test_debug <absolute path>` (`off` stops it).
- **Parameter faces (Live's native right-click menu; a scoped extension of rule 4)**: the v8ui draws every control, so Live shows no parameter menu on it. Every pool object except the marker is therefore also a **transparent face**: `presentation 1`, `hidden 1`, parked 1x1 at `[0, 0, 1, 1]` (outside the v8ui, so the saved file passes the static overlap check), every colour alpha 0, in `boxes` after the v8ui and before the background panels. Generic parameters are `live.dial` (`showname 0`, `shownumber 0`), the Depth parameters `live.numbox`, the On and boolean switches `live.text` (no text, Toggle mode) and the LFO shape a `live.tab` (5 values). **No `live.slider`**: its automation dot was seen to move with the value. The v8ui is interactive elsewhere; a face covers only its own control. Rule 4's overlap ban therefore holds for every other control: a face never overlaps another face or another drawn control (`tests/test_megamod_ui.js` checks the strips' count chip, chips, glyph, name, type and delete buttons).
  - **Layout** (`ubFacesWanted` / `ubStripFaces` / `ubFacesUpdate` in `src/megamod_ui.js`, `MM.faceApply` in the mapper): wanted set = for every strip, collapsed or expanded: the power face (`R.pwrFace` = `[x, 0, 17, 17]`, `M<k> On`), a 27 x 28 `live.dial` centred on every knob (the parameter the knob currently shows, so a synced LFO's Rate knob is `M<k> B`; **every** knob of every type, none is dropped), and in the expanded editor a `live.text` exactly on every chip (`SYNC` / `BIP` / `1SH` / `LOOP` / `RTG`) and one 5-cell `live.tab` over the five LFO shape chips (92 x 12; chips sit at a 19 px pitch, so the 18.4 px cells split in the gaps); plus the LST view's Depth pill (a 15 px `live.numbox` face, `[x+w-depthW, y0+21, depthW, 15]`, around the 13 px pill; `depthW` has 7 px padding each side). Options has no parameters. **Geometry never depends on a value** (nothing is moved while dragging; `tests/test_megamod_ui.js` compares the rects at every value minimum and maximum). Strip layout that makes room for the 28 px dial box: collapsed knobs at `cx = x + 14.5 + 27 k`, `cy = 102` (box `88..116`), value text at `y = 124`, label `133`; expanded: viz `18..80`, chips `y = 82` (12 px), knobs `cy = 109` (box `95..123`), value `131`, label `140`, count chip `143`. Faces are placed after every layout change: at the end of `paint()`, in `uiChanged` (every event but learn / full, so it also runs when nothing is painted) and at ready. All faces are hidden while the type popup or maps popover is open, before the pool is live (`autoOn`), and for anything that would leave the device width or the 169 px height. `faceApply` caches `x,y,w,h` per object and only calls `setattr("presentation_rect", ...)` / `setattr("hidden", ...)` for objects that changed; an unchanged repaint makes no call. Positions add the v8ui's presentation origin (read once, reset on a width change).
  - **Automation dot (a red dot Live draws on a face widget when its parameter is automated)**: it cannot be seen from the VM (no screenshots), so the layout rests on the widgets' geometry: a face is a widget whose size and drawing do not change with the value, and the whole face box plus a margin around the drawn control is kept free of drawn text. The `live.slider` faces used earlier made the dot move with the thumb (user report) and land on the value text, hence the change. A `live.dial` is forced to 27 x 28, so the dial is centred on the 16 px knob (5.5 px margin each side) and the value text / label start below its box (8 px of text at `y = 124` under a box ending at 116): whichever corner Live puts the dot in, it falls on the face's margin, never on the value. The `live.text` chips and the shape tab are as wide as their chips (labels are centred, >= 6 px from the edges); the numbox face of the Depth pill has 7 px of padding in the pill. How to verify by eye: in Live, right-click a face > Show Automation (or draw an automation lane), then watch the dot while dragging the knob and while the value text changes (e.g. toggle SYNC, which changes the Rate knob's text): it must stay put and not cover text. **Measured in Live 12.4.6 (VM)**: Live snaps displayed widgets: `live.dial` 27x28, `live.numbox` height 15, `live.slider` at least 18 x 50; `live.text` (17x17, 30x12, 28x12) and `live.tab` (92x12) keep the requested size. Hidden-and-moved faces do not change the parameter list (257 parameters). **Z-order (the cause of the first failed drag)**: Live loads the patcher with `obj-viz` LAST in the object chain (`test_zorder`: v8ui index 1382 of 1383, the faces around 300), so every saved face sat behind the v8ui and the drag reached the v8ui (`ui.sel` changed). Colour alpha is irrelevant (0, 0.01 and 0.5 all behaved the same). `faceApply` therefore calls `patcher.bringtofront(face)` each time it unhides a face; after that the face takes the click (`ui.sel` stays), also with alpha 0. Real left drag, -30 px on the Rate face: `55.52 -> 84.15` (the slider is more sensitive than the drawn knob's +23); double-click on the face resets to the saved default 0. **Real right click** on a face opens a new native window (layer 25, 183 x 136, via `mm_os_input.py cgwindows`), i.e. Live's parameter context menu; the item names are not readable, so which items it holds is not machine-verified. The type popup hides every face and they return when it closes (maps popover: Node only).
  - **Caveats**: the v8ui paints no tooltips; Live's Info View names a face's parameter. Drag feel is Live's slider feel (relative, vertical), not the drawn knob's exactly; the Depth pill drags vertically on its face, not horizontally. Clicking a face does not select the strip (only the v8ui does). A face's double-click default is the saved one (0, depth 100), not type specific. The menu's items cannot be read (System Events sees nothing), so only its appearance is checked.
  - **Live test**: `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-faces -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh faces'` (`scripts/test_megamod_faces_live.js`; the device only paints after the master track and then its track are selected, which the script does). It also expands an LFO strip and checks, with real mouse events: the Phase face (`M1 D`, a 27 x 28 dial centred on the knob) takes a drag and changes the LOM parameter and the model; a click on the SYNC face flips `M1 Sync` and the model (and the Rate knob's face becomes `M1 B` at the same rect), a click on a shape cell sets `M1 Shape`, a right click on the Phase face opens the native menu window; all 14 faces of that layout are at their rects. Hooks: `test_faces`, `test_selui`, `test_expand <slot>`, `test_cls`, `test_poolrect`; `test_report` carries `ui` and `hover` so the script finds the v8ui on screen without screenshots.
- **Width bug (fixed)**: `resizeFace()` looked the v8ui up with `getnamed("obj-viz")`, but the builder renames the main v8ui's scripting name to `MegaModState`, so the lookup was `null` in Live: the background panels grew with the device and the v8ui stayed at its saved 580 px (`mgraphics.size` `[580,161]` with a 732 px device), cutting the strips off. It now uses `MM_UI_VARNAME` (`MegaModState`, falling back to the script's own `box`); `tests/test_megamod.js` guards the varname and the sizes. Verified by hand in Live on the host: the right side draws and clicks work at full width. In the VM, scripted clicks past local x ~500 still fail (`scripts/test_megamod_width_live.js` FAIL lines) while hover works, so treat those lines as a VM / OS-input limitation (1024 px screen) rather than a device bug.
- **Width check (Live)**: hook `test_geom` writes `geom_<device id>` (requested width, `ubLayoutWidth()`, `obj-viz` / `obj-panel-*` presentation rects, the v8ui's `box.rect` / `mgraphics.size`, `devicewidth` / `openrect`, each strip's x / w and SYNC chip rect). `scripts/test_megamod_width_live.js` (4 LFOs, 3 expanded, then the maximum; real click on the right-most strip's SYNC chip) runs via `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-width -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh run scripts/test_megamod_width_live.js'`. `tests/test_megamod.js` checks that `resizeFace` gets the exact `setwidth` value (obj-viz = width - 2 x margin, panel = width) for every request.
- **Live test (target deleted)**: `scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-delete -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh run scripts/test_megamod_delete_target_live.js'` (`scripts/mm_scan_select.py` finds, by real clicks and `selected_parameter`, the screen point of a stock Utility dial, since the VM has no screenshots). Covers delete -> mapping gone within 4.5 s with its slot unbound and one undo entry, no jump onto an identical neighbour, real LRN click + real dial click -> working mapping, Mega undo after a (re)inserted device rebinding, and `move_device` to another track keeping the mapping. Live's own Undo does not restore a device deleted through the LOM, so the test re-inserts a Utility. Node: `tests/test_megamod.js` section 7z (learn after delete, throwing LiveAPI, two-poll confirmation, move, unhealthy LOM, load-missing kept, batch + undo / redo, self mappings untouched).
- **Test hook**: `receive megamod-test` → `obj-viz` supports `test_learn <mod> <param id>`, `test_report` (writes the slots, routes, ids, the bound pool ids `bound`, `undoInfo`, modulator params `modP`, mapping `depth` and `auto` ready flag to `Global("megamod_test")` as `report_<device id>` and `last`), `test_poke <mapping id> <v>`, `test_add <type>`, `test_settype <slot> <type>`, `test_remove <slot>`, `test_undo`, `test_setparam <slot> <key> <v>` (the UI path), `test_names` (cached names / values as `names_<device id>`) and `test_rename <varname> <name> <mode>` (renaming experiments). Drive it with `messnamed("megamod-test", ...)` from the MCP bridge's `ableton_eval_lom_js`, inside the VM (section 1a).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
python3 scripts/build_megamod.py && node tests/test_megamod.js && node tests/test_megamod_ui.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Mega Modulator.amxd"
```
Audio checks (real Max, inside the VM, through `scripts/max_harness.py`): `python3 scripts/run_megamod_audio_tests.py`. They cover LFO period and shapes, the Options settings (`rate_mul`: `ratemul` 2 / 0.5 on a 2 Hz LFO measures 4 Hz / 1 Hz, free and synced, a mid-run change keeps the phase continuous within one normal step, the follower ignores it; `smoothing`: a square through a router slot with `smooth 50` rises with a 50 ms one-pole time constant, `smooth 0` is unchanged), unipolar/bipolar ranges, random, steps, MSEG, the envelope follower, tempo sync, retrigger, telemetry, slot isolation, and router slot output against `mappingOutput()`.
Self-modulation Node tests (`tests/test_megamod.js` section 8, 20,290 checks in total; `tests/test_megamod_ui.js`, 12,203 in total, the depth-modulation block is last): dest codes <-> Live names for all 256 targets, existence / naming by meaning / the learn rejection rules, the descriptor-driven engine math against `selfCtlValue` for every type, control, offset and base, the static contract between the buffers / offsets and `src/megamod_engine.genexpr` (and the router code for slots below / above 64), schema 8 -> 9 and the round trip of both kinds, learn on the device's own parameters in a mock LOM (INTERNAL: route, table, descriptors, no pool slot; rejections; duplicates), the 64-route cap (`"full"`, id -1), VIA LIVE (bound to the own parameter id, modulated output reaches the engine only, the stored value read back, depth cell, restore on delete), conversion in both directions with undo / redo and the Remote -> Mod rule, deleting / undoing a modulator with self-mappings (also into another slot), type changes (missing / revive), save / load, telemetry parsing; UI: the Options row (rects, hits, tooltips, paint, state, conversion + note pill), list / popover / remap-editor rows and markers, inert M/R chip and REM tab for internal routes, ring / switch / Depth-tick paint, the two `full` pills.
Self-modulation audio cases (`selfmod_internal`, `selfmod_depth`, `selfmod_toggle`; `python3 scripts/run_megamod_audio_tests.py selfmod_internal selfmod_depth selfmod_toggle`, or `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh audio [case ...]` from the host: sync + run in the VM): `selfmod_internal/rate` = LFO 1 (0.5 Hz sine) modulates LFO 2's Rate (4 Hz) by +-1 octave through the matrix: the recorded zero crossings of LFO 2 match the integrated `f2 * 2^(oct * sin(2 pi f1 t))` reference within a millisecond, f 2.16..7.93 Hz, fastest cycle at LFO 1's positive peak; `selfmod_internal/feedback` = LFO 1 <-> LFO 2 each modulating the other's Rate (range 0.3): finite, in -1..1, still running; `selfmod_depth` = LFO 1 modulates the Depth of a mapping in router 0 slot 0 (source LFO 2 at 8 Hz, base 0.5, +-0.5): `out / LFO 2` follows `clip(0.5 + 0.5 sin(2 pi t), 0, 1)` within 0.01 (amplitude modulation 0.09..0.99 per cycle); `selfmod_toggle` = LFO 1 (2 Hz) toggles the Bipolar switch of a 40 Hz LFO (bipolar while LFO 1 > 0.5): 0 mismatches over 79 windows. All 21 cases (the 19 older ones included; `selfmod_depth` still passes unchanged, its route is dest `192 + 0` = depth of pool slot 0) pass.
Self-modulation in real Live (VM, under the Live lock; real clicks on the LRN button and on LFO 2's Rate face through `scripts/mm_os_input.py`):
```bash
scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-selfmod -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh run scripts/test_megamod_selfmod_live.js'
```
`scripts/test_megamod_selfmod_live.js` (new hooks in the mapper: `test_selflearn`, `test_selfmod`, `test_mapupd`, `test_delmap`, `test_measure <slot> <ms>` (busy-polls the engine scope phase and counts an LFO's cycles) and `test_cfg`) runs LFO 1 as a very slow square (a constant +1 / -1) and LFO 2 at 4 Hz: baseline 4.00 Hz; selector INTERNAL, real LRN + Depth-face + Rate-face clicks -> one internal mapping `LFO 2 Rate` (no pool slot), range 1 octave -> 8.00 Hz (LFO 1 = +1) with the engine cfg still 4 and the telemetry ring value above the stored one; selector VIA LIVE, the same gesture -> a pool mapping whose `live.modulate~` is bound to the device's own `M2 A` id (7), LFO 2 at 2.00 Hz (LFO 1 = -1), LOM `M2 A` value 72.238 % = the stored value, the engine cfg 2 (the modulated value), the model's stored rate still 4; the conversion VIA LIVE -> INTERNAL keeps modulating and Cmd-Z-style undo restores the pool mapping.
Hotkey checks (real OS keys and clicks, inside the VM only):
- Standalone Max: `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh max` from the host (syncs, rebuilds, runs `scripts/run_megamod_key_tests.py` in the VM). Seeds state through `pattr @bindto MegaModState` from `tests/megamod_key_seed.json` (regenerate with `node scripts/megamod_key_seed.js` on the host; the VM has no node). Keys come from System Events, clicks from CGEvent (`scripts/mm_os_input.py`). Verified: no-focus keys ignored, click + Delete deletes the selected modulator and its mappings, Cmd-Z / Ctrl-Z undo, Shift-Cmd-Z / Ctrl-Y redo, clicking outside clears focus.
Automatable parameters + depth (the acceptance test, real Live in the VM, under the Live lock, section 1a):
```bash
scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-auto -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh auto'
scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-reload -- zsh -c 'FORCE=1 scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live && scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh reload'   # load order (duplicate_track)
scripts/vm_live_lock.sh megamod-load -- scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh measure [other-build.amxd]   # load time, RUNS=3 (scripts/measure_megamod_load.py)
```
`scripts/test_megamod_auto_live.js` (host, through the bridge tunnel; 24 checks) lists the device parameters (161, names, `%` display), adds / retypes a modulator with the `test_add` / `test_settype` hooks and checks that the names stay while the values of the right letters follow, sets a parameter through the LOM and checks the modulator changed (`test_report`), moves a control with `test_setparam` (the UI path) and checks the LOM value, maps a Live parameter with `test_learn` and checks that `Depth 1` moves the mapping depth, then `test_remove` / `test_undo`. `scripts/test_megamod_reload_live.js` checks the load order (clone via `duplicate_track`). The `vm_megamod_keys.sh` subcommands find the bridge on 9877..9884 (`tunnel()`): a Live process stuck exiting in the VM (`ps` shows `(Live)` in state `?E`, `open -a "Ableton Live 12 Suite"` then hangs forever for every session) keeps port 9877 and the new bridge takes 9878+; `live` / `measure` then start Live with `open -n -a`. Node: `tests/test_megamod.js` (builder pool, name tables, runtime pushes / Live input / echo / undo / 64-slot limit / load order in a mock) and `tests/test_megamod_ui.js` (knob curve equals the pool curve, strip drag -> parameter, Live-name tooltips, Depth pill hit / drag / wheel / double-click, no overlap; faces: every knob / chip / enable of every type expanded and collapsed has a face, faces never overlap each other or another control, the rects are identical at every value minimum and maximum, no setattr during a drag, chip click <-> switch parameter <-> model both ways, automation of a switch never pushes undo or dirties the Set). Audio: `mapping_depth` (Mod depth 0.5 halves the router output, Remote shrinks toward min, depth 0 is silent) and `auto_rate` (`autorate`: a mid-run Rate parameter value moves the LFO period 0.5 s -> 0.25 s without a phase jump) in `scripts/run_megamod_audio_tests.py`.
- Live: `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh live` then `scripts/vm_megamod_keys.sh test` (`scripts/test_megamod_keys_live.js`, prints `INTERCEPT` lines showing whether Live swallowed Delete / Cmd-Z). Not yet run in Live: needs a free Live in the VM and a freshly opened set.

## 22. Spectral Split suite — shared framework (`src/spectral_split_lib.genexpr`, `src/spectral_link.js`, `scripts/vm_spectral.sh`)

Four Bitwig-Spectral-Suite style splitters (Transient Split `transplit`, Loud Split `loudsplit`, Harmonic Split `harmsplit`, Freq Split `freqsplit`). A device is the FIRST device of EVERY chain of an Audio Effect Rack, one chain per output channel. Every instance runs the identical analysis on the identical input with linked parameters and outputs only its own channel (`Output` = Auto: the chain index). The per-bin masks of all channels sum to exactly 1, so the chains sum back to the input delayed by the constant latency 4096.

### `src/spectral_split_lib.genexpr` (stereo mask-STFT helpers on top of `cepstrum_lib.genexpr`)
Prepended by the build as `cepstrum_lib + spectral_split_lib + <slug>_core`. The header comment holds the Data the core must declare, the per-sample skeleton and the whole API; in short:
- Left and right share ONE complex FFT (`X = FFT(hann * (L + jR))`). A real mask with `m[k] = m[N-k]` multiplies `X` directly and `IFFT(mX) = mL + j mR`, so one forward and one inverse FFT process both channels and the stereo image is kept. The analysis runs on the Mid signal `(L+R)/2`, taken from the same `X` (`Xm[k] = ((1-j) X[k] + (1+j) conj X[N-k]) / 4`).
- `ss_in(ringL, ringR, stt, xl, xr, n)` push a stereo sample, returns 1 on a hop (n / 4); `ss_frame` packs the frame; `ss_mid(fx, ms, n)` Mid amplitude (a full-scale sine reads its amplitude) and phase for bins 0..n/2; `ss_apply(fx, mk, ch, n)` applies mask channel `ch` of a per-bin Data to bins k and n-k; `ss_ola(accL, accR, stt, fx, win, n)` and `ss_out(acc, stt, n, slot)` (slot 3 = L, 4 = R) finish the overlap-add; `ss_cols(...)` / `ss_tele(...)` pool bins into 128 log-frequency telemetry columns (`ss_db01` = 0..1 over -100..0 dB).
- Latency: the STFT path is `n - 1`; the core pads with `Delay padL(4096)` (`read(4096 - (n - 1))`) and the patcher has `"latency": 4096`. Silence sleep is three lines in the core (`quietCnt` over `1e-5`, `awake = quietCnt < n`).
- Per-bin analysis, the masks (sum to 1 at neutral) and the controls are device code; `tests/genexpr_functions.js` runs the plain functions in Node with bounds-checked Data (every `History` inside a function is a persistent global of that load: call `load()` again for a fresh run). `tests/test_transplit.js` shows the pattern (pre-process `poke(scope, ...)`, `log10`, `atan2`).

### `src/spectral_link.js` (rack context + cross-instance parameter linking)
Concatenated into the face script (device config, then this file, then the device ui). `splParseRackContext(path)` is a port of `parseRackContextFromPath`; `splCreateLink({kind, keys, channelCount, getLocal, apply, onInfo}[, env])` returns `{init, notify, info, autoChannel, dispose}`.
- Bus: `Global("spectral_split_bus")` with `groups[kind::parentRackPath] = {counter, params: {key: {v, ver, src}}}` and `peers`. Each instance polls its group on its own `Task` (80 ms; topology and heartbeat every 500 ms, peers stale after 3 s), applies entries whose `ver` is newer than the last it saw and whose `src` is not itself, and never re-publishes them (`notify` is ignored while `apply` runs). LiveAPI is only touched on the instance's own Task.
- `init()` runs from `live.thisdevice` (`init_link`, never `loadbang`): it adopts the group's values if a live peer exists, otherwise publishes its own and resets a group whose peers are all gone.
- Face wiring: the `set_<key>` handlers store the value and call `link.notify(key, v)`; `apply` emits `[key, value]` on a v8ui outlet -> `[route <linked keys>]` -> per key `[prepend set]` into the live.* object (silent) AND into the same gen path the control uses; `onInfo` re-resolves the output selection (`link.autoChannel()`: chain index clamped to the channel count, -1 outside a rack = all channels summed) and sends it to the gen. `Output` and display toggles are not linked keys.
- Tests: `node tests/spectral_link_tests.js` (mocked Global / LiveAPI / Task; also run by `tests/test_transplit.js`).

### `scripts/spectral_common.py`, `scripts/make_spectral_rack.py`
Patcher helpers (live.numbox / tab / menu / hidden state toggle, labels, panels, gen box, `.amxd` writer) and the `.adg` writer (`--amxd --name --chains "A,B,C" --out`: one chain per channel, each with the device, Output Auto; reuses the Live 12.4 rack template of `make_delayplus_sets.py`).

### `scripts/vm_spectral.sh` (all four devices)
`scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio <slug> [case ...]` rsyncs the repo into the VM `macos-1` (own checkout `~/ableton-spectral`, `SPECTRAL_VM_DIR` overrides; never share a VM checkout with another session, `rsync --delete`) and runs `scripts/run_<slug>_audio_tests.py` there (`ui <slug>`, `run <script.py>`, `sync`, `status`, `shot` also exist). Max launches go through `scripts/max_harness.py`, so several devices can queue on the machine-wide lock.
- **Live:** `scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest` (= `live` + `test`, under the VM Live lock) writes `spectral_test.als` (`scripts/make_spectral_set.py`: one track per device holding its `.adg` rack, MCP Bridge on Main), restarts the VM's Live on it and runs `scripts/test_spectral_live.js` on the host through an ssh tunnel: Auto resolves to each chain's index, linked parameters (also fractional ones) reach every other chain, `Output` stays per chain, no re-publish loop, gens running and the track meter moving with a drum loop.

### Chain colours (`CHAIN_COLORS` in `scripts/make_spectral_rack.py`)
Each rack chain carries the colour its device face uses for that channel, as an index into Live's 70-colour palette (palette RGB and the CIELAB-nearest choices are in the comment there): orange `1` (Transients, Mid, Harmonics A), cyan `21` (Tones, Loud, Harmonics B), grey `55` (Quiet, Nonharmonics; colours are keyed by chain name, so Loud Split's order 1 Loud, 2 Mid, 3 Quiet changes nothing here), Freq Split red `28`, blue `9`, yellow `59`, magenta `11`. In the `.adg` they are `<DocumentColorIndex>` + `<AutoColored false>` inside `<AudioEffectBranchPreset>`; `make_spectral_set.py` writes `<Color>` + `<AutoColored false>` in `<AudioEffectBranch>` (its Freq Split chains are now named Red / Blue / Yellow / Magenta like the preset). `scripts/test_spectral_live.js` checks `color_index` of every chain in the set. Verified in Live 12.4.6 for the set path only; loading an `.adg` file directly cannot be driven through the LOM, so the preset path (`DocumentColorIndex`, the element Live's own presets use) is unverified.

### Mapping of Bitwig's device sections onto Live
- Bitwig's per-channel Gain / Pan / On / Solo and channel FX chain = the rack's chain volume / pan / activator / solo and the chain's devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack. The devices do not duplicate them.
- Bitwig's inspector parameters are ordinary parameters on the face. Spectrum Display Pre / Post is a UI toggle (not linked).
- `Mix` (0..100 %, linked): the rack sums the chains, so each instance outputs `mix * wet_own + (1 - mix) * dry / K` (K = channel count; 1 for Output All or outside a rack), dry delayed by the same 4096. The rack sum is an ordinary dry/wet; caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices.

---


### Ableton-native look (shared theme helpers)
- Spectral Split faces follow Live's own device look (flat titled sections on `live_desktop` over a `live_surface_bg` body, near-black `live_lcd_bg` displays with a faint grid, 1 px lines, small hollow handles, Ableton Sans Medium 9.5 px, one orange accent, `live_lcd_control_fg_alt` values). Shared code: `src/spectral_theme.js` (v8ui: `sptColor` with Live-default-dark fallbacks measured in Live 12.4.6, `sptThemeWatch`, `sptApplyPanelTheme`, `sptLcd`, `sptCurve`, `sptHandle`, `sptText`, `sptLabelValue`, `sptButton`; concatenated after `spectral_link.js`, listed in the device's `tsconfig`) and `scripts/spectral_common.py` (`make_section`, `make_body_panel`, `make_theme_*`, `make_lcd_numbox`, `theme()`; section panels last, body panel the very last box). Never hard-code RGB in a face. `scripts/render_preview.py` draws `"Ableton Sans"` v8ui text and theme-bound widgets in a regular-weight font. Done for Harmonic Split (section 25); the other three devices still use the old Bitwig-style look until restyled.

- **Paint bounds (all five splitters)**: every value-to-pixel mapping is clamped / clipped into its plot at parameter extremes (`sptClamp`, `sptClipLine`, `sptClipPoly`, `sptCurveIn`, `sptFillPolyIn`, `sptTextIn` in `src/spectral_theme.js`), every path starts with a fresh `move_to`, labels never collide with axis labels, handles stay inside the canvas. `tests/paint_bounds_lib.js` (recording `mgraphics` mock with path state) + `tests/test_<slug>_paint_bounds.js` (required from `tests/test_<slug>.js`) paint each face over a grid of extreme parameter and telemetry values and fail on any shape or text outside its allowed regions, a `line_to` / `show_text` with no current point, or overlapping labels. dB parameters reaching below -70 dB use `parameter_unitstyle 9` + `%.1f dB` (unit style 4 shows `-inf`): Loud Split thresholds, Harmonic Split `Threshold`.

## 23. Architecture of `devices/Transient Split.amxd` + `devices/Transient Split.adg` (slug `transplit`)

Bitwig "Transient Split (Spectral)" look and parameters. Channel section (per-channel Gain / Pan / On / Solo / FX chain) = the Audio Effect Rack chain's own volume, pan, activator, solo and devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack. Inspector parameters are ordinary parameters on the face.

### Presentation Layout (`440 x 169` px, Ableton-native look: see section 22 "Ableton-native look")
- **Left section `Detection`** (`x = 4..292`) holds the one `v8ui` canvas (`obj-viz`, `x = 8, y = 20, w = 280, h = 141`, `src/transplit_ui.js`, hosts `spectral_link`, concatenated after `spectral_theme.js`):
  - LCD (`y = 0..92`): faint grid, Analysis Bias slider at the left edge (-100..+100 %, centre tick, hollow orange handle, double-click resets) and Transients / Tones Blend slider at the right edge (0..100 %, centre at 50 %). Plot (`x = 20..260, y = 4..88`): **Waveform** = scrolling amplitude view (80 telemetry frames, about 1.7 s), Transients above the zero line (orange `live_display_line_one`), Tones below (cyan `live_display_line_two`), thin muted outline over a faint fill, -60..0 dB; **Sonogram** = 60 x 22 history, cells muted cyan (tone) to orange (transient). `Post` button (top right of the LCD): Pre shows the analysis split, Post the channel output after Tilt / Blend (the channel that is not this instance's output is dimmed).
  - Legend row (`Transients`, `Tones`, output channel bright), the two Display Style icons (Waveform, Sonogram) and the chain chip (`Chain 1/2, linked`, `No rack`).
  - Value rows: `Tilt 0.00 %`, `Decay 8.00`, `Smoothing 1.50` (drag vertically, double-click resets) and the `Latency 5120 smp` footnote. A header pill in the LCD (`DSP loading...` / `DSP failed: <reason>`, `src/spectral_dsp.js`) shows while the deferred gen~ is not running. Style and Pre / Post are the hidden `live.text` parameters `Display Style` and `Display Post` (`parameter_invisible 1`, not linked).
- **Right sections** (`x = 296..436`, label x 302, control x 360, 70 wide): `Analysis` (`Type` menu, `Tilt Mode` menu, `Bias` and `Blend` LCD values) and `Output` (`Output` menu, `Size` tab, `Mix`). `Tilt`, `Decay`, `Smoothing` are real automatable parameters kept off the face (`presentation 0`), edited on the canvas.
- No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui; the three section panels then the body panel are the last boxes.

### Parameters (Live short names are unique)
| Name | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Tilt` (Tilt Amount) | -100..+100 % | 0 | `tiltamt` (`* 0.01`) |
