| `Decay` (Transients Decay) | 1.00..125.00 blocks, float, unit `%.2f` | 8.00 | `tdecay` |
| `Smoothing` (Tones Smoothing) | 1.00..64.00 blocks, float | 1.50 | `tsmooth` |
| `Bias` (Analysis Bias) | -100..+100 % | 0 | `anbias` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Blend` (Transients Tones Blend) | 0..100 % | 50 | `blendamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Mix` | 0..100 % | 100 | `mixamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Type` | Percussive / Noise | Percussive | `ttype` |
| `Tilt Mode` | Standard / Contour | Standard | `tmode` |
| `Size` | 512 / 1k / 2k / 4k | 1k | `fftn` (`512 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | Auto / Transients / Tones / All | Auto | face -> `outsel 0|1|2` |

Linked keys (shared by every instance in the rack): all except `Output` and the display toggles (`ttype bias tdecay tsmooth tilt tmode blend mix size`). Renamed from the previous version: `T Decay` -> `Decay`, `T Smooth` -> `Smoothing` (long names unchanged); `Blend` was -100..+100 cross-feed, now 0..100 Mix-style balance; `Decay` / `Smoothing` were Int 0..16 blocks, now float; `Mix` is new; `Display Style` values `Spectrum` -> `Waveform`. Do not name a Param or function argument `noise`, `rise`, `fall`, `bias`, `mix`... (GenExpr operators): the core uses `nzMode`, `rsK`, `flK`, `anbias`, `tiltamt`, `blendamt`, `mixamt`.

### DSP (`src/transplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `tsMasks()` in `src/transplit_config.js`)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R). Per hop and bin (`ts_masks`):
- `A[k]` = rms of the Mid amplitude over +-2 bins. **Percussive**: onset = `20 log10(A / H)` through a smoothstep between `d0 = 4.5 - 12 * Bias` and `d0 + 5.5` dB; `H` is a history of `A` (rise 14 ms, fall 40 ms). **Noise**: phase instability = `|phi(t) - 2 phi(t-1) + phi(t-2)| / pi`, (1, 2, 1) smoothed across bins, smoothstep between `0.10 - 0.12 Bias` and `0.30 - 0.27 Bias`.
- `Decay` (blocks = hops, fractional): `tx = max(ev, tx_prev * exp(-1 / Decay))`. `Smoothing` (fractional): `tone = max(1 - tx, qx_prev * exp(-1 / Smoothing) * A_prev / A)`. `mask(Tones) = tone`, `mask(Transients) = 1 - tone`: exact sum 1.
- **Live, rack machinery** (shared by every adopting device): `scripts/vm_spectral.sh probetest` (`Spectral Probe` x 4 + 3 chains: how Live processes the chains, `scripts/test_spectral_probe_live.js`), `sumtest [old]` (`spectral_sum_test.als` = [Sum Probe][Transient Split rack][Sum Probe] + a control track with two probes; `scripts/test_spectral_sum_live.js [--only baseline,size,size_last,param,restart]`, opt-in destructive `leader_deleted` / `add`; `old` builds the set from the device committed at HEAD to show the pre-rack behaviour). Probes: `scripts/build_spectral_probe.py` -> `tests/spectral_probe/*.amxd`. Several Live runs in a row: take the lock once (`scripts/vm_live_lock.sh <label> -- <command that waits>`) and call the `vm_spectral.sh` subcommands with `M4L_LIVE_LOCK_HELD=1` (release promptly: other sessions queue behind it).

### Rack machinery: one analysis per rack (leader / follower), shared sample clock, deferred DSP (reference device: Transient Split)

Files: `src/spectral_split_rack.genexpr` (helper functions), `src/spectral_split_rack_main.genexpr` (the per-sample template; its header comment is the design document), `src/spectral_link.js` (election, optional `cfg.rack`), `src/spectral_dsp.js` (deferred gen~), `scripts/spectral_common.py` (`rack_subs`, `expand_rack_core`, `gen_code_rack`, `gendsp_json`, `deferred_header`, `to_gen_hub`). Device-private cost: a function costs gen compile time even when uncalled, so the rack functions are a separate file that only adopting devices include (Loud / Harmonic / Freq Split still build exactly as before).

**What Live really does with the chains of a rack (measured, `scripts/test_spectral_probe_live.js`, Live 12.4.6 in the VM, probes in `tests/spectral_probe/`, a float32 ramp clip gives every sample its absolute index, see below).** Chains run in blocks of the host buffer (512 samples = 8 vectors of 64, `vectorsize` 64) and **concurrently on different threads, in no fixed order**: for every pair of chains the other instance is ahead of the reader in about as many vectors as it is behind (chain order does not decide it; the last chain tends to run after the others), about 9 % of reads see the other instance in the middle of its vector, and the tickets of a shared counter interleave. So one instance's gen can read another's writes only after the block (the join), and reads inside the other's current block can be torn: **nothing may rely on same-vector visibility**, data is exchanged with a lag larger than a block. A muted chain, a soloed-away chain and Device Off do NOT stop an M4L gen (its sample counter kept running in all three cases), so a leader does not disappear when its chain is muted. (Not run: a real insert of a chain at run time through the LOM; `Chain.duplicate_device` returned ok but the new device is placed in the same chain.)

**Design.** Every instance is the FIRST device of every chain and sees the bit-identical input. One instance (the **leader**) runs the whole pipeline (frame, FFT, analysis, per served channel: mask, inverse FFT, overlap-add) and writes the channel signals into its own **output ring** at INPUT time; the others (**followers**) never frame or analyse: they read their channel from the leader's ring. A lone instance outside a rack computes only its own channel (role 0, the pre-rack behaviour). Mix, the dry path, Output and the Mix share per chain stay per instance (the ring holds pure wet signals). This fixes the hop-phase bug structurally: there is one STFT per rack, so the channels cannot be framed differently, a late-started instance has no frame phase, a linked Size change only happens in the leader, and a follower ignores its own Size while following. Messages from the face (`role 0|1|2`, `serve <mask>`, `epoch <n>`, `ring <buffer>`, `lead <buffer>`, plus `scope`, `outsel`, the parameters) go through the `ss_genin` hub.
- **Latency 5120 = 4096 + 1024** (`RACK_LATENCY`): every instance outputs ring index `counter - 5120` (INPUT time), so the leader's data must be at least one host block old (the largest STFT latency n - 1 = 4095 leaves 1025 samples at Size 4k, enough for host blocks up to about 960 samples; larger blocks make followers underrun and fall back, fb 3). The dry path uses the same delay. Static patcher attribute and telemetry slot `TS_SC_LATENCY`.
- **Counters and shared sample clock.** Each instance counts its samples modulo 2^22 (exact in a float32 buffer). The offset D between the leader's counter and the own counter is found by CONTENT (`ss2_align`): both write a fingerprint `L + 0.618 R` of the input into their ring (field 0 of every slot), the follower searches a 64-sample window that ended 1500 samples ago in the leader's ring around the prior `leader hwm - own counter` (+-2048) and takes the first exact match (a periodic input matches one period away: the output is periodic too). `ss2_verify` re-checks 4 samples at every vector; two failures drop the alignment, 160 fruitless searches with content (about 0.2 s) mean the input differs (the device is not first in its chain) and the instance falls back to computing alone (fb 1). No other clock exists in a host that runs the chains concurrently (same-vector counter reads are ambiguous by one block, which the probe showed). A silent input has nothing to match and needs nothing: the output of the first content is due 5120 samples later, alignment needs about 2300 + 64.
- **Ring (`buffer~ ---ssring`, `16 + 8192 * (1 + 2 (K + 1))` floats).** Header 0 next output index, 1 fingerprint count, 2 `gapS`, 3 `gapE`, 4 computing flag, 5 served mask, 6 n, 7 layout version, 8 latency, 9 age. Slot of counter i: field 0 fingerprint, fields `1 + 2c` / `2 + 2c` output row c left / right (c = 0..K-1 the channels, c = K the "All" mask row, used by a lone instance with Output All). The leader writes the emitted sample at counter `t - (n - 1)` (input time, so no reader needs to know n). Leader restarts (first start, becoming leader, Size change) leave a **gap** `[gapS, gapE)` (zero-filled, bounds in the header); every reader fades by READ index (down over 256 samples before `gapS`, up over 512 after `gapE`), which is possible because the reader is more than 1000 samples ahead of the writer. A read older than the ring or newer than the leader's write is an underrun (fb 3).
- **Roles in the face (`splCreateLink` with `cfg.rack`).** Election on the bus (one JS thread, race free): the group record has `leader` and `epoch`; a leader stays while its peer record is fresh, its gen runs (the alive counter moves) and nobody reports it dead; then the ready instance with the lowest chain index leads and the epoch grows. A follower whose gen reports fb 2 (leader data stopped for 0.5 s) says so on the bus, so the election does not wait for the 3 s heartbeat. fb 1 instances are private (role 0, never leader). The leader serves every channel when the group has more than one instance, else only its own. The messages are resent every 2 s and by the DSP loader.
- **Fallback.** A follower that cannot use the leader (fb 1 input differs, 2 leader stale, 3 underrun) computes its own channel (SELF behaviour, a restart gap, then plays from its own ring) until the face sends a new epoch. No click: the output gain of every source change is slewed (250 samples) and gaps fade.
- **Telemetry.** The leader writes the arrays (columns, waveform ring); followers' faces draw the LEADER's scope (`link.rack.displayScope()`), so followers skip `ss_cols` / `ts_wave` / `ts_mean` (a face polls the 4-float header first and reads the 800 floats only for a new frame). Common scalars at `TS_SC_FRAME + 0..17` (cmp, follow, aligned, fb, offset, underrun, lag, serve, role, alive).
- **Deferred DSP (AGENTS 1d).** The device ships without gen~; `spectral_dsp.js` creates it one Task tick after `init_link`, file-backed (`/tmp/transplit_core_<hash>.gendsp`, so the instances of a rack share ONE compile), one instance at a time (`Global("spectral_split_dspq")`), resend until the alive counter (scope +17, moves in silence too) moves, retry after 3.5 s / 6 s (the first gen~ of a Live process can fail to join), pill `DSP loading...` / `DSP failed: <reason>`. Until it runs an instance inside a rack outputs SILENCE (K chains of dry would be K times too loud and jump when the delayed wet arrives), an instance outside a rack passes dry. Trace: `Global("spectral_split_trace").text`. Test hook `receive #0-sstest` -> `dsp_restart <chain>` restarts one instance's gen~ (a late start).

**Device-side contract (what Loud / Harmonic / Freq Split must add to adopt it).**
- **Core** (`src/<slug>_core.genexpr`): keep the device functions; replace the declarations / statements by `//@SS_DECL` (generic Buffer ring / lead, Data tab / win / fx / fy / rings / `accD` / stt / ms / cols, Params fftn / outsel / mixamt / role / serve / epoch, Delays, role / clock History), then the device's own `Buffer scope(...)`, Data, Param, History, then three hooks and `//@SS_MAIN`. Hooks: `//@hook ctl` (copy Params into History every 32 samples; `n`, `sr`, `selH`, `mixH`, `cmpH`, `nChg` available), `//@hook frame` (after `ss_mid`: compute the masks into the device's mask Data, row `MKB + c` for channel c, row `MKB + K` for the sum; `sAge` = frames since restart), `//@hook tele` (device columns of `scope`, computing instance only). The mix Param must be called `mixamt` (rename the device's `wetamt` or add a build substitution) and `outsel` is 0 = All, 1..K = channel. Device state that must not leak across restarts is cleared by the template through `sAge` (the Transient core resets its history when `sAge` is 0).
- **Build** (`scripts/build_<slug>.py`): `sc.gen_code_rack(core, sc.rack_subs(K, mask_data, mask_base, scope_base, zero_count))`; face script = `sc.deferred_header(prefix, gen)` + config + link + theme + `spectral_dsp.js` + ui; patcher: no gen~ box, `buffer~ ---ssring @samps sc.rack_ring_samps(K)`, hub `gate 1 1` (`ss_genin`, `ss_plugin`, `ss_plugout` scripting names), `sc.to_gen_hub(lines)` for every cord into the gen, `r #0-sstest`, `setnames <scope> <ring>` at loadbang, latency `sc.RACK_LATENCY`.
- **Face**: `setnames` handler, a link with `cfg.rack = {channels, ring, genState, send}` (send = the outlet that goes to the hub), `ssDevResend()` (scope / ring / parameters / outsel / `tsLink.rack.messages()`), `ssGenAlive()` (scope `+17`), `ssDevChanged()`, `dsp_restart`, `ssDspPill()` in the header, draw from `link.rack.displayScope()`; telemetry config constants `+8..+17` (leave `+8..+17` free in the scope layout: Loud / Harmonic Split use them for other scalars today and must move them).
- **Tests**: `tests/test_spectral_rack.js` (functions, shared), `tests/test_spectral_dsp.js` (loader, shared), `tests/spectral_link_tests.js` (election, shared), the device's own `rack_*` audio cases (`scripts/run_transplit_audio_tests.py`: leader + follower + SELF reference, null, equality with the SELF channel, input delay k = 37 / 300 / 1301 / 2500 samples (alignment finds offset -k), fallback on a different input, silence, impulse latency), the Live rack-sum check (`scripts/test_spectral_sum_live.js`, generic once `make_spectral_sum_set.py` lists the device), `rack` block in `scripts/test_spectral_live.js`.
- **Measured (VM, Live 12.4.6, shared harness; scripts `scripts/measure_spectral.py`, `scripts/measure_face_cost.js`, `scripts/test_spectral_sum_live.js`).**
  - *Hop-phase bug, verified and qualified.* The pre-rack core (every instance frames the signal on its own) breaks the rack sum as soon as two instances restart their framing at different samples: harness, old core, Size change to 2k / 4k delivered to the second instance 3 ms later: Transients + Tones null **-31.4 / -32.6 dB** (11 ms later -36.2 / -30.1 dB) against -149 dB when both change at the same sample (`scripts/demo_hopphase_bug.py`). In Live the OLD device passed the rack-sum check after every linked Size change (baseline -163.0 dB, 2k -156.2, 4k -161.5, 512 -154.0, 1k -163.1 dB, latency exactly the reported 4096): a gen restarts its framing at a host-block boundary (512) and Live delivered the linked change to both gens in the same block, so the framing phases coincided (hop 128..512 divide the block; Size 4k happened to coincide). The bug needs restarts in DIFFERENT blocks (a chain started or duplicated later, a deferred gen~ created at another time, a smaller host buffer) with an offset that is not a multiple of the hop.
  - *Rack sum with the new design* (`scripts/vm_spectral.sh sumtest`: `Spectral Sum Probe` before and after the rack, both frozen at the same sample counter, lag scan, null of the rack sum against the delayed input): baseline -162.9 dB, linked Size 2k / 4k / 512 / 1k -156.2 / -161.3 / -154.0 / -163.1 dB, Size set on the last chain -155.7, linked Bias -163.6, DSP restarted in chain 0 / chain 1 (a late start with a new sample counter, audio playing) -156.6 / -163.2 dB, all at lag exactly 5120 = the reported latency. Deleting the leader's device: the surviving instance takes over after 1.08 s (cmp 1, role 1, no fallback) and keeps playing. `livetest` (all four devices, 217 checks) passes: exactly one leader, the follower aligned with offset 0 and lag 0, no underrun, both gens reached "dsp ready". Not testable through the LOM: adding a chain device later (`insert_device` puts a native device of a similar name in); `dsp_restart` is the late-start stand-in.
  - *Load (Live trace)*: GUI first; first gen~ created 0.43 s after `init_link`, ready 0.92 s later (compile + DSP start), the second instance of the rack created right after and ready after 0.16 s (file-backed identical gen~ share one compile). Gen compile (harness, min of 3): Transient Split **0.47 s -> 0.81 s** (more code: K + 1 inverse transforms, align / verify), Loud 0.34, Harmonic 0.92, Freq 0.43 s; before, every instance compiled it at load (12 instances in one patch: 0.54-0.60 s each), blocking the GUI K x that.
  - *CPU per rack* (NonRealTime wall seconds per second of audio, 6 racks minus 1 over 5, % of one core, noise +-0.5 %): old (both chains compute) / shared (leader + follower): Size 512 **2.86 / 1.87**, 1k **3.89 / 1.65**, 2k **4.31 / 1.10**, 4k **4.68 / 2.33** (ratio 0.65 / 0.42 / 0.26 / 0.50). Single instance (old) 1.2-1.9 % of a core for Transient Split, 1.2-1.3 % Loud, 1.8-6.0 % Harmonic, 0.9-1.7 % Freq.
  - *Faces (Node, per call)*: paint 115 / 416 / 213 / 273 us (Transient / Loud / Harmonic / Freq); a poll that finds a new frame 117 / 147 / 49 / 76 us, without a new frame 1 us (Transient, header first) vs 7-9 us (the others still peek the whole buffer).
- **Limits worth knowing**: the output of a lone instance (outside a rack, SELF) after an Output change is silent for about 5120 samples (the new channel's ring has no history); the leader computes `K + 1` inverse FFTs per hop at most (K when it serves every channel), all in the sample that completes the hop (no time slicing; Harmonic Split's analysis spike is unchanged); followers still compile the whole core (a "follower-lite" gen hot-loaded with `setattr gen`, AGENTS 1d.5, would save their 0.5 s compile); the rack saves CPU, not wall time (the chains run concurrently, the critical path is now the leader).
- `Tilt` (explicit gain): Transients `* g`, Tones `/ g`, `g = 10^(12 dB * Tilt * w(f) / 20)`; `w` = -1 at 100 Hz .. +1 at 10 kHz (Standard) or **-1 around 1 kHz and +1 at the lows and highs (Contour: a positive amount raises highs and lows together)**.
- `Blend` b = 0..1: Transients mask `* min(1, 2 b)`, Tones mask `* min(1, 2 (1 - b))`. 50 % = both 1 = the original is reconstructed; 100 % only the transient portions (Tones silent, Transients unchanged); 0 % only the tones.
- **Mix**: `out = mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry * kShare`, `kShare = 0.5` when the instance outputs one channel of the rack (the 2 chains sum to `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`), `1` for Output All (outside a rack: plain dry/wet). Dry goes through its own 5120-sample delay (`Delay dryL`); `mix` is one-pole smoothed (no clicks). Mix, the dry path and Output are applied by every instance itself (the ring holds pure wet channels). Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices.
- The analysis state is reset (`sAge = 0`) when the pipeline restarts (Size change, first start, becoming leader) and when the device wakes from silence. Silence sleep: input below `1e-5` for a whole frame skips all FFT work. A pipeline restart leaves a gap of about `n + 128` samples in the output ring that every reader fades around (see the rack machinery section 22), instead of the old `4096 + N + 128` mute.
- **Constant latency 5120** (`RACK_LATENCY` = 4096 + 1024; patcher `"latency": 5120`): the outputs are read from the ring at input time `counter - 5120`. Before the rack machinery it was 4096 (a `padL / padR` Delay); only Size 4k forces the extra 1024 (n - 1 = 4095 leaves a single sample), the other Sizes would fit 4096 but a latency that changes with Size is not worth it.
- **Rack machinery (section 22, this device is the reference)**: one instance per rack computes (leader, serves both channels, `K + 1 = 3` inverse FFT passes at most), the other reads its channel from the leader's ring (`role 2`); a lone instance outside a rack computes only its own channel. Gen core `src/transplit_core.genexpr` holds the analysis functions and three hooks (`ctl`, `frame`, `tele`); `scripts/build_transplit.py` expands the template (`gen_code()`), the device ships WITHOUT gen~ (hub `ss_genin`, `ss_plugin`, `ss_plugout`, `buffer~ ---ssring`, test hook `r #0-sstest`), the face creates the gen~ after load (`face_code()` = deferred-DSP header + config + link + theme + `spectral_dsp.js` + ui). Gen messages added: `role`, `serve`, `epoch`, `ring`, `lead` (and `scope`, re-sent with the parameters). New instances start with `role -1` (silent until the face has told them).

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---transplitscope @samps 800`, bound by the face's `scope ---transplitscope` message; the face gets `setnames ---transplitscope ---ssring` at loadbang)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Transients post, `256..383` Tones post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `384..511` transient fraction of the column, `512` frame counter (wraps at 102400, a multiple of 64), `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` mean Transients mask, `518` latency, `519` output selection, `520..529` rack scalars (`TS_SC_CMP` computing, `FOL` following, `ALIGNED`, `FB` fallback 0 / 1 input differs / 2 leader stale / 3 underrun, `DOFF` counter offset to the leader, `UNDER`, `LAG`, `SERVE` mask, `ROLE`; written by EVERY instance every 1024 samples; the arrays, `517` and the waveform ring only by the computing instance) and `TS_SC_ALIVE` = `529` (moves every 1024 samples, also in silence: the deferred gen~'s ready detection). **`530..785` waveform ring**: frame `f` at slot `f mod 64`, 4 floats (rms of the Transients and Tones signal pre = analysis masks, then post = final masks; `ts_wave`). Written about every 1024 samples; the face polls at 15 fps, reads the 4-float header first and the whole buffer only for a new frame (a follower's face draws the LEADER's buffer), reads every new ring entry since its last poll and only redraws when the frame counter moves. The Sonogram's yellow / blue fraction comes from the post masks (identical to pre at Tilt 0 / Blend 50 %).

### Rack preset
`devices/Transient Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Transients` and `Tones`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_transplit.py && node tests/test_transplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Transient Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/transient_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio transplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
`tests/test_transplit.js`: reference sum identity at Blend 50 % (< -250 dB at every Size, both types), onset / tone behaviour, Bias monotonic, Tilt / Blend as gains, fractional Decay, `ts_wave`, GenExpr functions transpiled and compared with the reference, lint, link module, built device (parameter table, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_transplit_audio_tests.py`, the REAL expanded gen code of `build_transplit.gen_code()`, four instances per case: Transients, Tones, All and a SELF Tones reference; stimulus: noise bursts over a 440 Hz sine): `sum_*` (Transients + Tones null vs the input delayed 5120, all instances SELF), `drums_*`, `bias_*`, `noise_*`, `blend_hi` / `blend_lo`, `mix_50` (T + Q = 0.5 wet + 0.5 dry) and `mix_0` (= dry delayed), `silence`, `latency_*`, telemetry and waveform ring; rack cases (leader = Transients, Tones and All follow its ring): `rack_sum_<n>` (null < -90 dB at every Size, follower Tones EQUAL to the SELF instance, offset 0, aligned, no underrun), `rack_noise`, `rack_silence` (exact zeros), `rack_blend`, `rack_mix_50` / `rack_mix_0`, `rack_delay_<k>` (the follower's input delayed by 37 / 300 / 1301 samples: the content alignment finds offset -k and the output equals the SELF Tones delayed by k), `rack_delay_far` (2500 samples, beyond the +-2048 search: falls back, computes alone), `rack_fallback` (a 0.97 x input: fb 1, own split), `rack_pending` (no role message: silence), `rack_impulse` (All follower: exactly 5120 samples at unity).
Live: `scripts/vm_spectral.sh sumtest` (the rack-sum check below), `livetest transplit` (link + the rack block).

### Known rough edges
- Linking is verified with mocks and (per the suite test) in Live; between a parameter change and its application by the peers (80-160 ms) the chains do not sum exactly.
- A rack with more than 2 chains: chains >= 3 resolve to Tones. Instances that are not the first device of their chain still link by rack.
- Percussive onsets are smeared by the Hann window (about N/2 either side). Tones Smoothing is an energy-aware tone extension, not Bitwig-identical (their algorithm is not public). Decay / Smoothing are in this device's own blocks (hops of N/4), so their numbers do not map one-to-one to Bitwig's.
- `scripts/render_preview.py` shows the `live.numbox` units with one decimal (`8.0`); Live shows the `%.2f` unit text.
- Mix: the dry share runs through each chain's devices (Bitwig mixes after the chains).
- CPU is not measured (all work of a hop runs in the sample that completes it; Size 4k is the worst case).
- GenExpr lessons: a user function call nested in the arguments of `poke()` makes the whole codebox compile to silence; an LCG on a `History` with `2^32` literals produced `inf` (use `noise()`).

---

## 24. Architecture of `devices/Loud Split.amxd` + `devices/Loud Split.adg` (slug `loudsplit`)

Spectral Split suite member (framework: section 22). Three channels, chain order 1 Loud (red), 2 Mid (yellow), 3 Quiet (green). Per bin of the Mid spectrum, level above the Higher Threshold goes to Loud, below the Lower Threshold to Quiet, between to Mid; the three masks sum to exactly 1 for every setting (only Tilt weights the analysis, it never changes the output gain), so the chains sum back to the input delayed by the constant latency **5120** (4096 + the 1024-sample host block margin of the rack machinery). **Adopts the rack machinery** (section 22, reference device Transient Split): one instance per rack (the leader) computes the analysis and all three channels into a shared ring, the other chains read their channel from it, the STFT hop clock is shared, and the gen~ is created after the GUI.

### Presentation Layout (`480 x 169` px, Ableton-native: Reverb-like flat titled sections, Live theme colours; same look as Harmonic Split, section 25)
- Body panel `live_surface_bg` (last box), section panels `live_desktop` before it: `Detection` (`x = 4..296`), `Thresholds` (`x = 300..476, y = 4..89`), `Output` (`x = 300..476, y = 93..165`), each with a small title and hairline (`sc.make_section`).
- **One `v8ui` canvas** (`obj-viz`, `x = 8, y = 20, w = 284, h = 141`, `src/loudsplit_ui.js`, the only v8ui; hosts `spectral_link`, uses `src/spectral_theme.js`, 4 outlets):
  - **LCD** (`284 x 92`, `live_lcd_bg`, faint grid at 100 Hz / 1 kHz / 10 kHz and -40..-100 dB): log frequency 30 Hz..20 kHz, 128 columns, level -110..0 dB. PRE = Mid spectrum as thin per-column lines in the blend of the channel colours (Quiet grey, Mid orange, Loud cyan, all muted) over a faint fill; POST = the three channels as 1 px outlines with faint fills (this instance's output brighter); SONO = muted waterfall. `Post` and `Sono` buttons (`sptButton`, hidden `live.text` `Display Post` / `Display Style`, not linked) in the top-right corner.
  - **Threshold lines** (Higher / Lower, 1 px orange, follow Tilt, ride the loudest bin in Relative mode): drag vertically = threshold, **Alt / Shift / Ctrl-drag = Knee** (faint band), double-click = default. The small hollow handle (`H` / `L`) at the right end (`x = 274`, kept below the buttons) is the momentary band solo (filled while held). Value text at the left (`Higher -30.0 dB`).
  - **Under the LCD**: legend (`Loud`, `Mid`, `Quiet`, this instance's output bright, `Solo ...` while a handle is held) with the chain chip on the right; value rows `Rise 2.00` / `Fall 8.00` and `Tilt 0.0 dB/oct` (drag vertically, double-click resets; Rise / Fall logarithmic 0.13..125, Tilt +-6) plus the `Relative` button (`Relative Loudness Mode`). They are real, automatable parameters whose `live.*` objects are kept off the face (`presentation 0`).
- **`Thresholds` section**: `Higher` / `Hi Knee`, `Lower` / `Lo Knee`, `Rel High` / `Rel Low` as theme-bound LCD `live.numbox` (`appearance 4`) with `live.comment` labels; the unused pair is greyed by `act_abs` / `act_rel`. **`Output` section**: `Output` menu, `Size` tab, `Mix`. No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui.
- Channel section (Gain, Pan, On, Solo, FX chain) = the Rack chain's own controls; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack.

### Parameters (name, range, default)
`Higher Threshold` (short `Higher`) -110..0 dB, -30; `Lower Threshold` (`Lower`) -110..0 dB, -85; `Relative Higher Threshold` (`Rel Higher`) -110..0 dB below the loudest bin, -12; `Relative Lower Threshold` (`Rel Lower`) -110..0, -36 (our own defaults: in Relative mode 0 dB is the loudest bin of the block, so Loud = within 12 dB of it, Quiet = more than 36 dB below it); `Higher Knee` / `Lower Knee` 1.0..24.0 dB, 6.0; `Rise Time` 0.13..125.00 blocks (float), 2.00; `Fall Time` 0.13..125.00, 8.00; `Tilt` -6..+6 dB/oct about 1 kHz (analysis only), 0; `Relative Loudness Mode` (`Relative`) Absolute / Relative, Absolute; `Mix` 0..100 %, 100; `Size` 1k / 2k / 4k, 2k; `Output` Auto / Loud / Mid / Quiet / All. Linked across instances: everything except `Output` and the display toggles (`Mix` included). Renamed / removed vs the earlier version: ranges and defaults changed for every threshold / knee / Rise / Fall (was -90..0 / -24 / -48, knee 0..30, Rise / Fall Int 0..32 default 0); `Relative` long name `Relative Loudness` -> `Relative Loudness Mode`; short names are unchanged; new: `Mix`.

### Gen messages
`Higher` -> `thrhi`, `Lower` -> `thrlo`, `Rel Higher` -> `rthrhi`, `Rel Lower` -> `rthrlo`, `Hi Knee` -> `kneehi`, `Lo Knee` -> `kneelo`, `Relative` -> `relmode`, `Rise` -> `risebl`, `Fall` -> `fallbl`, `Tilt` -> `tiltdb`, `Mix` -> `mixamt` (`Mix / 100`; a Param called `mix` would shadow the builtin; renamed from `wetamt` for the rack template), `Size` -> `fftn`. `Output` is resolved by the face (`outsel 0|1|2|3` = All / Loud / Mid / Quiet).

### Mix
Each instance outputs `mix * wet_own + (1 - mix) * dry / K` with dry delayed by the same 5120 samples (`Delay dryL/dryR`), K = 3 when one chain of a rack (outsel 1..3), K = 1 for Output All / outside a rack, so the rack sum is `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`. `mix` is smoothed (one-pole, 500 samples) and an exact 1.0 is bit-identical to the previous output. Caveat: the dry share goes through each chain's devices (an FX in the Loud chain also processes dry / 3).

### DSP (`src/loudsplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `lsMasks()` in `src/loudsplit_config.js`)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R), driven by the rack template (`src/spectral_split_rack_main.genexpr`; the core only supplies the `ctl` / `frame` / `tele` hooks, `scripts/build_loudsplit.py` expands it with `sc.rack_subs(3, "lsd", 2, 512, 512)`). Roles, ring, shared clock, fallback and the deferred gen~ are exactly Transient Split's (section 22); the leader serves all three channels (`serve 7`), the Mid / Quiet / Loud instances that follow read their channel from its ring (and Output All sums the three rows). Data `lsd(2049, 8)`: 0 tilted level, 1 smoothed level, **2 Loud, 3 Mid, 4 Quiet (the template's channel rows `MKB + c`, chain order), 5 their sum**, 6 (bin 0) loudest bin, 7 the tilt table `log2(f / 1 kHz)` (`ls_tilttab`, rebuilt only when n or the sample rate changes, instead of a `log` per bin per hop). Per hop and bin (`ls_masks`):
- `lv = 20 log10(max(A, 1e-6)) + Tilt * log2(f / 1 kHz)` (A = Mid amplitude, a full-scale sine reads its amplitude; note Mid = (L+R)/2). `Relative`: `lev = lv - max_k lv` (loudest bin 0 dB, bins 1..N/2-1), else `lev = lv`.
- Rise / Fall: per-bin one-pole smoothing of `lev` in dB, coefficient `1 - exp(-1 / Rise)` while `lev` is above the state, `1 - exp(-1 / Fall)` below, fractional blocks clamped to 0.13..125 (0.13 is about instant, blocks = hops of N / 4 samples). The state restarts (`sAge = 0`, the template's frame age) when the leader's pipeline restarts (Size change, becoming leader) and when the device wakes from silence.
- `sH = smoothstep((ls - (Hi - KneeHi/2)) / KneeHi)`, `sL` likewise around `Lo = min(Lower, Higher)` (Knee 1..24 dB), `sH = min(sH, sL)`; `Quiet = 1 - sL`, `Mid = sL - sH`, `Loud = sH`. Exact sum 1, never negative.
- Silence sleep, constant 5120 latency and the Size-change mute as in Transient Split (the template's `muteLeft`; a Size change happens in the leader and the followers read the gap-faded ring).

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---loudsplitscope @samps 544`, bound on loadbang by `scope ---loudsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---loudsplitscope`)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Quiet post, `256..383` Mid post, `384..511` Loud post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `512` frame counter, `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` mean Loud mask, `518` latency (5120), `519` output selection (`outsel`: 0 all, 1 Loud, 2 Mid, 3 Quiet; the spectrum slots above stay indexed by channel NAME, not by outsel), **`520..529` rack scalars (template: computing, following, aligned, fallback, counter offset, underruns, lag, serve mask, role, alive counter = `LS_SC_CMP .. LS_SC_ALIVE`)**, **`530` effective Higher Threshold (dB, tilt-domain at 1 kHz; Relative: frame max + value), `531` effective Lower Threshold, `532` loudest bin level** (moved up from 520..522, which the rack scalars took). The columns, 517 and 530..532 are written by the computing instance only; followers' faces draw the LEADER's scope (`link.rack.displayScope()`). Written about every 1024 samples. The face polls at 15 fps, reads the 4-float header (frame, awake) first and the 544 floats only for a new frame, smooths the frame max for the Relative lines and only redraws when the frame counter moves. The face gets `setnames ---loudsplitscope ---ssring`; the gen~ is created after the GUI (`DSP loading...` / `DSP failed: ...` replaces the chain chip in the legend row until it runs).

### Rack preset
`devices/Loud Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Loud`, `Mid`, `Quiet` (colours cyan 21, orange 1, grey 55), each holding the device with `Output = Auto` (chain index, clamped to 3; outside a rack the sum = pass-through).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_loudsplit.py && node tests/test_loudsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Loud Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/loud_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio loudsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names; queues behind other harness runs)
```
`tests/test_loudsplit.js`: reference sum identity (< -250 dB, per-bin sum 1, non negative) at 1k / 2k / 4k and nine settings (Relative, Tilt +/-, Rise / Fall, knees 1 / 24, crossed thresholds), loud / mid / quiet partials, Relative invariance at -30 dB input, hard / smooth knees, thresholds, Tilt weights, Rise / Fall step responses in blocks (Rise 8 / 16 -> 10 / 20 blocks to Loud), silence, stereo image; ranges / defaults, fractional Rise / Fall (0.13..125 coefficients, 0.5 / 1.5 / 2 blocks to Loud), the Mix sum identity, Mix wiring and the off-face parameters; the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks vs `lsMasks` to 1e-9 for six settings, sum null < -250 dB, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint; the link module; the built device (header, latency, geometry, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table, wiring incl. the `active` route, `.adg`).
`tests/test_loudsplit.js` also covers the rack path (the embedded gen = lib + ss lib + rack functions + the expanded core, scope slots vs `LS_SC_*`, channel rows, no gen~ / static audio cord in the device, hub wiring, ring buffer length, `r #0-sstest`), the DSP pill in the paint-bounds test, and runs `tests/test_spectral_rack.js` / `tests/test_spectral_dsp.js`.
Audio cases (`scripts/run_loudsplit_audio_tests.py` runs the gen code the device embeds (`build_loudsplit.gen_code()`), probe `tests/loudsplit_probe.js`, three real core instances per case (role 0): outsel 1 = Loud, 2 = Mid, 3 = Quiet (the probe records them by name: rec channels Quiet, Mid, Loud); stimuli: `mix` = 500 Hz at -8 dB + 1.5 kHz at -36 dB + 3 kHz at -54 dB + faint noise, `ramp`, `steps`): `sum_1024/2048/4096`, `sum_defaults`, `sum_mix50` / `sum_mix0` (Mix 50 % / 0 %; at 0 each chain is dry / 3) and `sum_2048_rel / tilt_up / tilt_down / riskfall / knee / cross` (Quiet + Mid + Loud vs the input delayed 4096: about -149 dB, bound -90), `partials` (500 Hz 99 % Loud, 1.5 kHz 100 % Mid, 3 kHz 100 % Quiet, right = left / 2), `rel_loud` / `rel_quiet` (same split at -30 dB input; telemetry thresholds = frame max - 12 / - 36) / `abs_quiet` (the same tone at -38 dB is Mid), `thr_move`, `tilt_off` (3 kHz 99.8 % Quiet) / `tilt_up` (Tilt +6 dB/oct: 80 % Mid), `ramp` (-90 -> 0 dB sine: 100 % Quiet / Mid / Loud in the first quarter / middle / last quarter), `rise_0` / `rise_16` (Loud share in the 120 ms after a rise 0.995 vs 0.002), `fall_0` / `fall_16` (Loud share 40..160 ms after a fall 0.000 vs 0.433), `silence` (exact zeros, telemetry asleep), `latency_1024/2048/4096` (impulse out of the summed channels exactly 5120 samples later, unity) and telemetry (thresholds at slots 530..532). **Rack cases** (`rack_*`: Loud leads with `serve 7`, Mid / Quiet / All follow its ring, `s` = a SELF Mid / Quiet reference): `rack_sum_1024/2048/4096`, `rack_rel`, `rack_tilt`, `rack_riskfall`, `rack_cross`, `rack_mix_50`, `rack_mix_0` (Quiet + Mid + Loud null against the input delayed 5120 about -149 dB; the follower's channel equals the SELF channel to the float, error < -100 dB; leader cmp 1, follower fol 1, aligned, offset 0, no fallback, no underrun), `rack_silence` (exact zeros, the follower's alive counter moves), `rack_impulse` (All follower: latency exactly 5120, unity), `rack_delay_37/300/1301` (a follower whose input is delayed aligns by content: offset -k, output equals the SELF channel delayed by k), `rack_delay_far` (beyond the +-2048 search span: fallback 1, computes alone), `rack_pending` (no role yet: all silent, nothing computes or follows), `rack_fallback` (a 0.97x input: fallback 1, the follower's own split, energy ratio 0.94 to the leader's). All cases pass.

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not yet in real Live; the Alt / Shift knee drag and the solo dot release (`ondrag` with `but = 0`) are verified only in the Node reference, not in a real v8ui. Between a parameter change and its application by the peers (about 80-160 ms) the masks of the instances differ, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames.
- Classification is per bin on the raw Mid amplitude (no smoothing across bins), so the Hann neighbours of a partial sit 6 dB lower and can land in the next band down (a 500 Hz sine at -8 dB: 99 % Loud, 1 % Mid). Levels are Mid levels: a hard-panned signal reads 6 dB lower. Rise / Fall act on the dB level (an onset from silence takes about `2 * Rise` blocks to cross a threshold). Relative mode's frame maximum is per hop, so a single loud bin moves every relative threshold.
- Rack with more than 3 chains: chains >= 4 resolve to Quiet (Auto clamps).
- **CPU / time slicing (measured, see below).** All work of a hop still runs in the sample that completes it (the leader: the Mid analysis, `ls_masks`, one inverse FFT per served channel), no time slicing: per-hop cost is small (about 2 bins per sample at Size 1k) and spread evenly. `ls_masks` is mostly per-bin Data traffic (about 11 reads / writes per bin) rather than the `log10`; the tilt `log` was removed (table), the amplitude `log10` stays (the JS reference and the amplitude semantics are unchanged).
- **Measured, rack machinery (VM, Max 9.1, NonRealTime wall seconds per second of audio as % of one core; very noisy because the VM is shared with other sessions, +-1 %).** Compile of the device's gen (harness, min of 3 minus a trivial gen): old core 0.77 s (0.34 s on a quiet VM) -> new gen code **1.17 s** (rack functions + K = 3 inverse transforms); before, EVERY instance compiled it at load (an embedded codebox does not share a compile), now the three instances of a rack share one file-backed compile and the GUI never waits for it. CPU per instance alone (old core / new core in role 0): 1k 2.26 / 1.62 %, 2k 2.34 / 1.55 %, 4k 1.81 / 0.70 %. CPU per 3-chain rack, run 1 / run 2 (old = three instances compute; SELF = the new core in role 0, three instances; shared = leader + two followers): old 1k 4.45 / 0.98, 2k 3.00 / 3.23, 4k 6.27 / 4.95 %; SELF 4.83 / 5.70, 6.37 / 4.90, 4.45 / 6.08 %; **shared 2.11 / 4.19, 2.48 / 3.44, 3.43 / 3.61 %**. The two runs disagree by up to 4 % of a core (the VM is shared), so only the structure is certain: sharing does one analysis instead of three, the new per-instance template (fingerprint, 8 ring writes per sample on the leader) costs a little more than the old core, and the net CPU gain is not resolved by this method (Transient Split's 0.26-0.65 measured on a quieter VM is the better estimate; a Live CPU measurement was not possible). Split of one new instance (switching parts off): masks (`ls_masks`) about 1.5-2 %, FFTs 0.4-1.0 %, telemetry 0.0-0.6 % (the pieces do not add up to the total: noise). Face (Node, per call): paint 428 -> 417 us, poll with a new frame 155 -> 151 us, poll without a new frame **7.1 -> 0.5 us** (the header is read first: 528 -> 4 floats).
- **Shared Live checks** (`vm_spectral.sh sumtest` / `livetest`; not run by this device's own session): the device is the same shape as Transient Split: scope `+8..+17` rack scalars at 520..529 (`LS_SC_*`), latency 5120, hub `ss_genin`, `r #0-sstest` (`dsp_restart <chain>`, chain 0 / 1 / 2), ring `---ssring` of `16 + 8192 * 9` floats, ring prefix `loudsplit` for the gen file `/tmp/loudsplit_core_<hash>.gendsp`. Expected: chains Loud / Mid / Quiet, one leader (cmp [1, 0, 0], role [1, 2, 2], serve 7), followers aligned (offset 0, lag 64..), no underrun, fb 0; rack-sum null < -90 dB with probes before / after the rack (lag exactly 5120); Mix is `mixamt` in the gen (the parameter and face are unchanged).

---

## 25. Architecture of `devices/Harmonic Split.amxd` + `devices/Harmonic Split.adg` (slug `harmsplit`)

Spectral Split suite member (framework: section 22). Three channels, chain order 1 Nonharmonics (grey), 2 Harmonics A (orange), 3 Harmonics B (turquoise). Use as the FIRST device of EVERY chain of an Audio Effect Rack; `Output = Auto` resolves to the chain index (clamped to 3; outside a rack = all channels summed = pass-through). The per-bin masks of the three channels sum to exactly 1 (there is no gain control), so the chains sum to the input delayed by 4096.

### Presentation Layout (`440 x 169` px, Ableton-native: Reverb-like flat titled sections, Live theme colours)
- Body panel `live_surface_bg` (last box), three section panels `live_desktop` before it: `Detection` (`x = 4..292`), `Harmonics` (`x = 296..436, y = 4..96`), `Output` (`x = 296..436, y = 100..165`), each with a small title and a hairline (`sc.make_section`).
- **One `v8ui` canvas** (`obj-viz`, `x = 8, y = 20, w = 280, h = 141`, `src/harmsplit_ui.js`, the only v8ui; hosts `spectral_link`, uses `src/spectral_theme.js`):
  - **LCD** (`280 x 92`, `live_lcd_bg`, faint grid at 100 Hz / 1 kHz / 10 kHz and -20..-80 dB): log frequency 30 Hz..20 kHz, 128 columns, -100..0 dB with a 6 px strip below the -100 dB line for -120..-100. PRE = Mid spectrum as thin per-column lines in the blend colour of where the energy goes (grey Nonharmonics, orange Harmonics A, cyan Harmonics B, all muted) over a faint fill; POST = the three channels as 1 px outlines with faint fills (this instance's output brighter). Harmonic ticks in the A / B colours (Pattern rule, up to Max Harm, stop where denser than 3 px), the **root** as a small hollow circle with faint crosshairs and its frequency (`f0 32.7 Hz  h5` note when the series fundamental lies below the root; series positions counted from the root, odd-only series skip even ones), and the **interactive analysis parameters as 1 px orange lines with hollow handles**: vertical Low / High (`L` / `H`, drag horizontally) and the horizontal Detection Threshold (`T`, drag vertically; dragged all the way down it reads Automatic and sits on the bottom axis; while Automatic a faint dashed line shows the effective threshold, telemetry 524). The plot is dimmed outside Low..High (everything when Low >= High); the root / ticks are drawn only when the root lies inside the band. A `Post` toggle button (`sptButton`, hidden `live.text` `Display Post`) sits in the LCD's top-right corner. There is no tilt line.
  - **Under the LCD**: legend row (`Nonharm`, `Harm A`, `Harm B`, this instance's output bright) with the chain chip (`Chain 2/3, linked`) on the right, then two value rows `Low 80.0 Hz` / `High 1.50 kHz` and `Tilt 0.0 dB` / `Threshold Automatic` (Detection Low-cut / High-cut 19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz, 80 Hz / 1.5 kHz; Tilt +-12 dB analysis only; Threshold -120..0 dB). The four values are draggable (vertical drag, full range in 120 px, frequencies on a log scale); double-click resets (80 Hz, 1.5 kHz, 0 dB, Automatic).
  - Low-cut / High-cut / Tilt / Threshold are **automatable `live.numbox` objects that are not on the face** (`presentation 0`, `parameter_enable 1`): the canvas draws and edits them (`outlet 0 -> route lowcut highcut tilt thr dispost -> control`), so Bitwig's exact strings (`Automatic`, `1.50 kHz`) are shown.
- **`Harmonics` section**: `Pattern` menu (`1st only | Every 2nd .. Every 24th`, default Every 2nd), `NH Sens` (Nonharmonic Sensitivity, `1..100 %`, 50), `Max Harm` (Maximum Harmonics, `0..404`, 0 = Off; float, because Live's native Int stops at 255; the widget shows `0`), `Mix` (`0..100 %`, 100): theme-bound `live.menu` and borderless LCD `live.numbox` (`appearance 4`, value in `live_lcd_control_fg_alt`) with `live.comment` labels.
- **`Output` section**: `Output` menu (`Auto | Nonharm | Harm A | Harm B | All`), `Size` tab (`1k | 2k | 4k`, default 2k), `Latency 4096 smp` note.
- No `live.*` widget overlaps the v8ui; short names are unique (`Pattern`, `Max Harm`, `NH Sens`, `Tilt`, `Threshold`, `Low Cut`, `High Cut`, `Mix`, `Size`, `Output`). Chain order stays Nonharmonics, Harmonics A, Harmonics B.
- **Channel section = the rack**: Bitwig's per-channel Gain / Pan / On / Solo / FX chain are the Audio Effect Rack chain's own volume / pan / activator / solo and its devices; Pre FX / Post FX = devices before / after the rack.

### Parameters and gen messages
| Parameter | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Pattern` (Harmonics Pattern, enum index 0..23 = pattern 1..24) | 1st only, Every 2nd..24th | Every 2nd | `hpat` (`expr $f1 + 1`) |
| `NH Sens` | 1..100 % | 50 % | `sensamt` (`expr ($f1 - 1.) / 99.`) |
| `Max Harm` | 0..404 (0 = Off = 404) | 0 | `hmaxp` |
| `Tilt` | -12..+12 dB | 0 | `tiltamt` (dB) |
| `Threshold` | -120..0 dB (-120 = Automatic) | Automatic | `thrdb` |
| `Low Cut` | 19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz | 80 Hz | `flocut` |
| `High Cut` | 19.4 Hz..19.9 kHz | 1.5 kHz | `fhicut` |
| `Mix` | 0..100 % | 100 % | `wetamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Size` | 1k / 2k / 4k | 2k | `fftn` (`1024 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | Auto / Nonharm / Harm A / Harm B / All | Auto | face resolves it, `outsel 0..3` |

Linked keys (shared by every instance in the rack): all except `Output` and `Display Post`, now including `Mix` and `Pattern`.

### Mix
Each instance outputs `wetamt * wet_own + (1 - wetamt) * dry4096 * kdry` (`kdry` = 1/3 for one of the three chains, 1 for Output = All), so the rack sum is `mix * wet + (1 - mix) * dry`, dry delayed by the same 4096 samples. Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices (only the wet is the channel).

### DSP (`src/harmsplit_core.genexpr`; JS reference `hsFrame()` in `src/harmsplit_config.js`, mirrored line for line)
Mask-STFT of `spectral_split_lib` (hop N/4, Mid analysis, one real mask applied to L and R). Per hop:
- **`hs_prep`**: raw dB spectrum of the Mid amplitude and the **analysis spectrum** = raw + Tilt (`Tilt * log10(f / 1 kHz)` dB: +Tilt at 10 kHz, -Tilt at 100 Hz) + the **soft detection filters** (4th-order high-pass at Low-cut and low-pass at High-cut, `-10 log10(1 + (flo / f)^4) - 10 log10(1 + (f / fhi)^4)`, -3 dB at the cutoff, stored per bin in `hsd` channel 7). The salience spectrum `hc` = analysis dB mapped 0..1 over the 45 dB below the loudest analysis bin; `level` = loudest attenuated raw bin (dBFS, a full-scale sine reads 0). New: `hsd` channel 8 = the **spectral ripple** `yd` (hc minus its mean over +-8 bins) and channel 9 = the **band weight** `bw` of every bin (`clip((att + 12) / 9, 0, 1)`: 1 down to -3 dB of cut attenuation, 0 at -12 dB). **Tilt, Low-cut and High-cut act on the analysis only**: they say where the EVIDENCE for the series is (partials inside the band) and where the ROOT must lie; harmonics of a found series are sorted wherever they are (also above High-cut).
- **Root, series, numbering (2026-10-21, user report: a C1 square (32.7 Hz, odd harmonics) at Low 156.1 Hz / High 2.90 kHz detected nothing).** The tracker estimates the **series**: the harmonic series `F * h` that explains the partials inside the band. `F` (the series fundamental, tracked in `hst` 0, telemetry 525) may lie BELOW the band (missing or filtered fundamental) and the series may be odd-only. The **root** is the **lowest partial of that series that is present (prominent) and lies inside [Low, High]** (C1 square: the 5th harmonic, 163.5 Hz). The Pattern counts from it, **`h' = h - hr + 1`** (`hr` = the root's harmonic number in the series, `hst` 21 / telemetry 526; `hr = 1` when the fundamental itself is inside the band and present, so ordinary in-band notes are unchanged): `h' = 1` is the root, Every 2nd puts `h'` odd in A, Every 8th puts `h' = 1, 9, 17, ...` in A (C1 square: series 5, 13, 21 = 163.5, 425.1, 686.7 Hz), `1st only` puts only the root in A. **Max Harm counts `h'`** too. **Series partials below the root go to Nonharmonics** (decision: the root is defined as the lowest in-band partial, so the lower ones have no number; C1 square: 32.7 and 98.1 Hz). With a missing fundamental the root is the first present partial (220 Hz saw without its fundamental: series 220, root 440 = harmonic 2). A series whose lowest present partial is above High (2.5 kHz saw, High 1.5 kHz) or whose partials are all below Low has no root: everything is Nonharmonics. Telemetry `f0` (517) is the root; 525 / 526 / 527 are the series f0, `hr` and the odd-only flag.
- **`hs_sal`** (series estimation, `src/harmsplit_core.genexpr`; reference in `hsFrame()`): candidate fundamentals on the FIXED grid `19.4 Hz * 2^(i / 64)`, `i = 0..641`, from `max(19.4, Low / 32)` up to `High * sqrt2` (and `0.45 sr`); none when `Low >= High`. Only partials inside the band are evidence, weighted by their bin's band weight (so automating a cutoff fades partials in and out). Each candidate is scored as a full series (spacing `g`) and as an odd-only series (spacing `2 g`); the **effective spacing in bins** picks the statistic:
  - **>= 4.5 bins: peak / valley comb** over 10 positions from the lowest in-band harmonic (`h0 = max(1, ceil(0.51 Low / g))`; for an in-band fundamental `h0 = 1`: the old comb). Term `max(peak(hc +-1 bin) - max(hc half a spacing below, hc half a spacing above), 0)`, weights `bw / (1 + 0.12 j)`, score `sum / (sum w + 0.8 + 0.6 clip((3 - sum w) / 2, 0, 1))` (a candidate with fewer than about 3 weighted in-band positions is weak evidence: noise gets its maximum there, the extra regulariser took the white-noise maximum back under 0.30). The odd-only comb uses the odd harmonics among the first 7 positions (`HS_ODD_H`) and puts its valley an EMPTY EVEN position away (`min(g, max(g / 2, 2.5 bins))`; for a candidate that is a sub-series of the real one the old half-spacing valley was filled by neighbouring partials, but moving it to +-g for every g made a missing-fundamental saw lock to `g = 127`: partials 2 g, 3.5 g... leak into the +-1 bin peak window).
  - **2.2 .. 4.5 bins: spectral ripple.** Partials this close are not separate peaks in a Hann spectrum (at Size 2k and 48 kHz the C1 square's partials are 2.8 bins apart; valleys between them are only 4..15 dB deep, so peak / valley terms stay under 0.2). The series shows as a ripple of period `S` in the detrended spectrum: score `0.056 * r * sqrt(sum bw)`, `r` = correlation of `yd` with `cos(2 pi f / S)` over the first 192 weighted in-band bins (cos by recurrence). **One spacing `S` serves both phases: `r > 0` = peaks at multiples of `S` (full series, `g = S`), `r < 0` = peaks half way (odd-only series of `g = S / 2`)**; the coarse grid is scored at three sub-offsets (+-0.38 %, a coherent sum over 40+ partials needs the spacing within about 0.3 %), the winner's spacing is then refined in 11 steps of 0.2 % with a parabola. Measured (Node, 25 seeds x 3 sizes): ripple score of white noise stays under 0.30, the C1 square scores 0.67..0.73 at 2k.
  - **< 2.2 bins: not scored** (a ripple that fast aliases onto longer spacings: the first version scored from 1.1 bins and locked a 220 Hz saw to `g = 23.9` at 44.1 kHz). Consequence: **a full series needs `g >= 2.2 bins`** (Size 1k: 103 Hz, 2k: 51.6 Hz, 4k: 25.8 Hz at 48 kHz), an odd-only one `g >= 1.1 bins` (2k: 25.8 Hz). The C1 square works at 2k, the **C1 sawtooth needs Size 4k** (at 2k its partials are 1.4 bins apart: a 2048-sample frame holds 1.4 periods; nothing is reported at 2k). Sweep 45 Hz..1.4 kHz at 48 kHz: Size 4k finds saw / square / triangle in every step (0 / 79 errors), 2k fails only below 55 Hz (saw), 1k below about 200 Hz.
  - The **lowest local maximum within 78 % of the best** is taken (sub-octave check, as before), refined by a parabola over the three neighbouring candidates. A sub-multiple of the true `F` scores about half (a saw's `F / 2` has every second position empty; a square's `F / 2` almost nothing), so the series is the highest `F` that explains the in-band peaks.
- **`hs_decide`**: accept when salience >= 0.36 (>= 0.198 while tracking; `HS_ACCEPT`, white noise peaks at 0.30 in single frames and never locks: a lock needs two consecutive consistent hops), level >= the **effective threshold**; hold the old series for 8 hops, 2 consistent frames for a first lock or a jump of more than 0.05 octave (a new series also resets the held root).
  - **Automatic threshold** (Threshold at -120 dB): effective threshold = running peak level of the analysis (`lm`: instant attack, release 12 dB per second) minus 40 dB, never below -100 dB. So harmonic sorting follows the signal: a quiet sustained sound is still split (the threshold falls with it), while a note that dies 40 dB below the recent peak drops out to Nonharmonics once `lm` has decayed with it; very quiet noise never locks (the salience accept does that). Slots `hst` 17 (`lm`), 18 (effective threshold, telemetry 524), 19 (initialised). An explicit threshold (> -120) is used as is.
- The odd-only flag only counts while the series fundamental itself is unresolved (`g < 4.5` bins; telemetry 527): a resolved square or 25 % pulse is searched at every harmonic (absent ones stay Nonharmonics). Without this a 25 % pulse at 220 Hz lost its even harmonics to Nonharmonics.
- **`hs_harm`**: for each harmonic `h = 1..404` of the SERIES (odd-only: odd `h` only) the spectral peak and its prominence over the mean dB half an EFFECTIVE spacing either side. Resolved series (>= 4.5 bins): local spectral peak near `h * g` (window `max(2 bins, 15 % g)` capped at 0.45 spacing, running least-squares estimate of `g` from peaks with >= 10 dB prominence), parabolic peak frequency, present at >= 10 dB. Unresolved series: nominal position `h * g` (the spacing comes from the ripple search), level = louder of the two bins around it, **present at >= 4 dB** (measured prominences of the C1 square at 2k: 5..15 dB, Hann leakage of the neighbours 1.4 bins away). `hp` channel 4 = present flag.
- **`hs_post`**: voiced amount `v` -> smoothstep((level - effective threshold) / 4 dB), attack 0.6 / release 0.35 per hop.
- **`hs_masks`**: the **root** (lowest present in-band partial, see above; held while present with a 3 dB lower bar, a lower one replaces it at once) and the weights `hw[h] = smoothstep((prominence - pmE) / wd) * (h' <= Max Harm, 0 = 404) * v` for `h >= hr` (0 below the root), `pmE = 14 - 12 s` and `wd = 6` for a resolved series; both fall for an unresolved one (`rf = clip((S - 2) / 4, 0, 1)`: `pmE = (14 - 12 s) rf`, `wd = 1 + 5 rf`; the C1 square at 2k: 1.6 dB and 2 dB at 50 %), smoothed over hops (attack 0.7, release 0.4). **No band edge**: harmonics are sorted wherever they are. **Painted lobes instead of nearest-harmonic ownership** (an odd-only series at 1.4 bins per harmonic has absent harmonics between the present ones): every weighted harmonic paints a lobe into A or B by `max`: flat to `w0 = 0.7 + 1.3 s` bins from its refined centre, raised cosine to `w1 = w0 + 0.7 + 0.5 s`, `w1` capped at `0.35` of the effective spacing (`0.5` when the spacing is under 2.8 bins, so the lobes tile without overlap); then `mask(B) = min(mask(B), 1 - mask(A))`, `mask(Nonharmonics) = 1 - A - B`: exact sum 1 (identical to the old per-bin ownership whenever the spacing is >= 5 bins).
- **Pattern rule (`hsIsA(h', P)`)**: harmonic `h'` (counted from the root) goes to A when `(h' - 1) mod P == 0`, else B. **Pattern 1 is the special case: only the root goes to A.**
- **Nonharmonic Sensitivity** widens the lobes and lowers the prominence a partial needs (14 dB at 1 %, 2 dB at 100 % for a resolved series): lower values put more signal in Nonharmonics (monotonic, tested).
- Silence sleep, Size change mute and the constant latency 4096 are as in Transient Split; the dry for Mix is a `Delay` of 4096 (`dryL / dryR`, 4097 samples), the Mix change is smoothed (0.002 per sample).

### Telemetry (`buffer~ ---harmsplitscope @samps 544`, bound on loadbang by `scope ---harmsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---harmsplitscope`)
`0..127` Mid level per column, `128..255` Nonharmonics post, `256..383` A post, `384..511` B post (0..1 over -100..0 dB), `512` frame counter, `513` samplerate, `514` N, `515` awake, `516` input peak, `517` **root** Hz (the lowest present series partial inside the band, 0 = none), `518` salience, `519` voiced amount, `520` level dB, `521` latency, `522` output selection, `523` active harmonics (weight > 0.5), `524` effective detection threshold dB (the automatic one when Threshold = -120), `525` series fundamental Hz (may be below the band, 0 = none), `526` root harmonic number `hr` in the series, `527` odd-only series flag. Written about every 1024 samples. The face polls at 15 fps.

### Live check (`scripts/test_spectral_live.js`, `scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest harmsplit`)
Besides the shared link / audio checks, the Harmonic Split block makes four WAVs in the VM over ssh (stdlib python, `/tmp/hs_*.wav`: naive square 220 Hz mono 44.1 kHz, naive square 110 Hz on the LEFT channel only at 48 kHz, square 440 Hz at -20 dB, triangle 220 Hz), puts each in clip slot 1.. of the Harmonic Split track and requires every chain's f0 telemetry within 1 % of the true pitch within 9 s. Measured in Live 12.4.6 (gen sr 48000): 220 Hz [220.00, 220.01, 219.99], 110 Hz left-only [109.98, 110.02, 110.00], 440 Hz [439.6, 439.6, 439.8], triangle [220.00 x3]; salience 0.43..0.61 (square) and 0.48 (triangle), voiced 1.00.

### Rack preset
`devices/Harmonic Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Nonharmonics`, `Harmonics A`, `Harmonics B`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_harmsplit.py && node tests/test_harmsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Harmonic Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/harmonic_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio harmsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
`tests/test_harmsplit.js`: reference sum identity (< -250 dB at 1k / 2k / 4k, masks sum to 1 and are >= 0 in every frame); 220 Hz sawtooth with the device defaults (f0 within 0.5 %, odd harmonics 1..12 >= 98.5 % in A, even in B, Nonharmonics < 1 %, A / B energy about 3), Pattern 4 / 3 / 1, Maximum Harmonics 8 and Off, **root detection** (the telemetry `f0` is the ROOT; `series` and `hr` are asserted too: **the user's report, C1 square 32.7 Hz at Low 156.1 Hz / High 2.9 kHz / Every 8th / Size 2k, 48 and 44.1 kHz: root 163.5 Hz within 1 % in every settled frame, series 32.7, `hr` 5, salience > 0.42, series harmonics 5, 13, 21 in A and 7, 9, 11, 15..25 in B with > 0.9 share, 32.7 / 98.1 Hz in Nonharmonics, sum null < -250 dB; also Size 4k, -30 / -6 dB, a naive square; C1 sawtooth at Size 4k root 163.5 (and none at 2k); 110 Hz saw with Low 300: root 330, harmonics 1, 2 in Nonharmonics, 3, 5, 7, 9 in A, 4, 6, 8 in B; Max Harm 4 and Pattern `1st only` count from the root; white noise in that band never locks (3 seeds x 3 sizes); defaults unchanged for 220 Hz saw / square; a 2.5 kHz saw with High 1.5 kHz has no root**; the transpiled GenExpr equals the reference on the C1 square at 2k and the C1 saw / square at 4k), **the band decides where the evidence is and where the root lies** (the old assertions, now on the root) (bands 150..400 Hz, High 2 kHz, full range: 220 Hz found and harmonics 1, 2, 3, 8, 11, 20 sorted as with the defaults; 110 Hz saw with Low 300 Hz: root 330 (its 3rd harmonic, series 110); 220 Hz saw with High 100 Hz: f0 0; 2.5 kHz saw tracked with High 19.9 kHz, not with the default 1.5 kHz; Low >= High: no f0, all Nonharmonics; 60 Hz tracked with Low 19.4 Hz, not with Low 300 Hz; the detection level is measured through the filters; the GenExpr functions equal the reference also for narrow, empty and full bands), Detection Threshold explicit (-10, -60) and **Automatic** (a -70 dB sawtooth still split, very quiet noise never tracked, a note decaying 60 dB drops out), missing fundamental (series 220, root 440, `hr` 2), f0 60 / 110 / 440 / 1000 Hz, white noise (all Nonharmonics, the tracker never locks: 4 seeds x 3 sizes), **square / triangle / 25 % pulse** (square 110..880 Hz at 44.1 and 48 kHz, band-limited and naive, -6 / -30 dB: f0 within 1 %, salience > 0.42; triangle 110 / 220 Hz; pulse 110..880 Hz; full range up to 1.76 kHz; 55 Hz at Size 4k with Low 30 Hz; the band still limits a square; white noise highest salience 0.295 asserted < 0.31 over 6 seeds x 3 sizes (0.34 in the user's band; over 25 seeds x 2 s x 3 sizes x 4 bands single frames reach 0.30..0.42 in rare hops, as the old full range already did, but no lock), never locks; 220 Hz square through the split: Pattern 2 all odd harmonics in A, Pattern 4 harmonics 1, 5, 9 in A and 3, 7, 11 in B, sum null < -250 dB; the GenExpr equals the reference on a square), saw + noise (Max Harm 40: f0 stable and voiced, number of active harmonics stable, noise between the partials > 90 % in Nonharmonics, about 83 % of all the noise energy), Sensitivity monotonic, glide 150 -> 424 Hz (worst error < 0.1 %), stereo image, silence, Mix rack-sum formula, display strings (`Automatic`, `1.50 kHz`, ordinals); the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks and tracker state, now including the running level / effective threshold, equal to the reference to 1e-9 for four parameter sets, sum null < -250 dB, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint (incl. the Mix line and the series-estimation constants against the core literals); the link module; the built device (header, latency, 169 px, geometry, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table versus `HS_PARAMS`, ranges / defaults of every parameter, hidden automatable Low / High / Tilt / Threshold, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_harmsplit_audio_tests.py`, probe `tests/harmsplit_probe.js`, four real core instances per case: outsel 1, 2, 3, 0; stimulus: band-limited additive 220 Hz sawtooth (amplitude 0.25) with the right channel half the left; Goertzel per harmonic and half way between harmonics; device defaults Low 80 Hz / High 1.5 kHz / Threshold Automatic / Max Harm Off / Mix 100 %): `sum_1024/2048/4096_saw`, `sum_2048_sawnoise`, `sum_2048_pat4` (the three channels vs the input delayed 4096: about -149 dB, bound -90; `sum_2048_noise` is exact), `saw_p2` / `saw_p4` / `saw_p1` (harmonics 1..12 100.00 % in their channel, Nonharmonics 0.27 %, f0 220.00 Hz, 64 active harmonics, A / B energy 3.03 / 1.91 / 1.59, effective threshold = level - 40 dB), `saw_maxh8`, `sq_p2` / `sq_p4` / `sq_quiet` / `sq_f110` / `sq_f440` / `sq_f880` / `sq_wide_1760` (band-limited square: f0 within 1 %, salience 0.45..0.63, Nonharmonics < 0.5 %; Pattern 2 puts 99.8 % in A and 0 in B, Pattern 4 puts harmonics 1, 5, 9 in A and 3, 7, 11 in B), `tri_220` / `tri_110` (salience 0.48 / 0.39), `pulse_f220` / `pulse_f440`, `saw_cut` (band 150..400 Hz: harmonics 1..12 still 100 % sorted, f0 220.00), `band_low` (500..1500 Hz: f0 660, 220 Hz fundamental 100 % Nonharmonics), `band_high` (19.4..100 Hz: f0 0), `band_empty` (Low = High: f0 0, 100 % Nonharmonics), `band_wide` (2.5 kHz saw, High 19.9 kHz: f0 2500.00) / `band_default_2500` (default High 1.5 kHz: f0 0), `sawnoise` (noise between the partials 99.6 % in Nonharmonics), `nofund`, `noise`, `mix_0` (A energy / dry energy 0.11111 = 1/9, sum null -148.6 dB, All = dry exactly), `mix_50` (sum null -150.0 dB, All null -356 dB), `thr_above` / `quiet` (explicit thresholds: A + B share 0), `quiet_auto` (Automatic: -70 dB sawtooth split 99.73 %, effective threshold -100 dB = floor), `stereo`, `silence`, `latency_1024/2048/4096` (impulse out exactly 4096 samples later, unity). All 46 cases pass in the VM after the root-detection change (2026-10-21). New cases measured in Max: `c1_sq_2k` root 163.52 (series 32.70, `hr` 5, odd-only, salience 0.68; A partials 5 / 13 / 21 at 1.000 / 1.000 / 0.997, B partials >= 0.998, Nonharmonics 32.7 / 98.1 Hz 1.000), `c1_sq_44k` 163.51 (0.72), `c1_saw_4k` 163.51 (0.73, even partials in B >= 0.997), `c1_noise` no root, `saw110_low300` root 330 / series 110 / `hr` 3 (all shares 1.000), `nofund` root 440 / series 220. Live (`scripts/vm_spectral.sh livetest harmsplit`, gen sr 48000, all checks pass): C1 square root [163.50, 163.50, 163.50] on the three chains, series 32.70, `hr` 5, odd-only 1, salience 0.63 / 0.68 / 0.66; the older waves unchanged (220 Hz [219.97, 220.03, 220.01], 110 Hz left-only [109.99, 109.97, 109.97], 440 Hz [439.8, 439.9, 439.6], triangle 220.00 x3).

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not yet in real Live; for 80-160 ms after a parameter change the instances of a rack apply different values, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames. Size changes mute each instance on its own schedule.
- A rack with more than 3 chains: chains >= 4 resolve to Harmonics B (Auto clamps).
- **Steeply falling spectra near the band top**: a triangle scores 0.39 at 110 Hz, 0.48 at 220 Hz and only about 0.32..0.34 at 440 / 880 Hz with the default High 1.5 kHz (its harmonics 3, 5 are weak and above High-cut attenuated), so it is not tracked there (it is with the full range open: 0.48 up to 1.76 kHz). A naive (aliased) 880 Hz saw at 48 kHz is also not tracked (salience 0.35, octave error with the full range). The odd comb is limited to harmonics 1, 3, 5, 7 on purpose (see `hs_sal`).
- Very loud noise over a quiet harmonic signal (saw 0.1 + noise 0.3, about -10 dB SNR) is not tracked any more (salience about 0.23 < 0.36); the 0.36 accept is the price for never locking in noise.
- **Automatic threshold is a design choice, not Bitwig's** (their algorithm is not public): running peak level - 40 dB (12 dB/s release, floor -100 dB). It cannot gate on an absolute noise floor, so a steady loud noise bed above a quiet harmonic sound is handled by the salience accept, not by the threshold.
- `Max Harm` shows `0` in the widget (Live's unit format cannot print `Off`) and `Threshold` / `Low Cut` / `High Cut` / `Tilt` are only on the display (hidden automatable parameters).
- Maximum Harmonics up to 404 and the removal of the band edge mean harmonics are now sorted up to the Nyquist limit; prominence tests keep noise out, but weak upper partials near the noise level flicker in and out (active-harmonic count not stable above about harmonic 60).
- The tracker is monophonic: chords or several voices give a weak, flickering or sub-octave f0 (hysteresis and the 2-frame jump rule keep it from flickering, but it can lock to the dominant voice). A **pure sine** has no harmonic series (salience about 0.1, below the 0.36 accept), so it is not tracked and goes to Nonharmonics. Very low fundamentals need Size 4k (60 Hz at 1k is 1.3 bins per harmonic: lobes are capped at 0.35 of the spacing and the prominence test rejects most of them).
- **Resolution limits of the series estimate** (see `hs_sal`): a full series needs `g >= 2.2 bins` (51.6 Hz at Size 2k, 25.8 Hz at 4k, 103 Hz at 1k; 48 kHz), an odd-only one `g >= 1.1 bins`; below that nothing is reported (no wrong root; the first version aliased). Sorting an unresolved series is approximate: partials 2.8 bins apart overlap in the Hann main lobes, so the A / B shares at the partials are 0.9+ (C1 square at 2k), not 0.985+ like a resolved series. At Size 1k a 220 Hz sawtooth still tracks and splits cleanly (4.7 bins per harmonic), but harmonics closer than about 3 bins cannot be separated. Size 4k resolves everything from 26 Hz up (sweep 45 Hz..1.4 kHz: no error); its latency is the same constant 4096, but its 85 ms frame follows fast pitch changes more slowly.
- **Root numbering shifts with the band.** The pattern counts from the lowest present in-band partial, so moving Low-cut across a partial (or a fundamental sitting just below Low, e.g. 70 Hz with Low 80 Hz: root 140 Hz = harmonic 2) renumbers A / B. Before 2026-10-21 a fundamental just outside the edge was still tracked (soft half-octave edge); now the edge is hard for the root, soft only for the evidence. Octave errors of the series (F / 2 chosen) keep the root frequency but not the alternation (full-series pattern on a half-frequency series: B stays empty).
- Inter-harmonic noise inside a partial's lobe (about +-1.4 bins at 50 % sensitivity) goes to the harmonic channels with the partial: with white noise about 78 % of the noise energy stays in Nonharmonics, not 100 %.
- **CPU**: all the work of a hop (two FFTs, 2049 bins of analysis, the series search, 404 harmonic probes, the lobe painting, 4 x 128 telemetry columns) runs in the sample that completes the hop (no time slicing). The series search is about 640 candidates x 10 positions (peak / valley comb) plus the ripple correlation (about 66 spacings x 3 sub-offsets x 192 bins, the same at every Size) plus about 12 more correlations for the winner. Node (V8) reference, whole `hsFrame` per hop: 0.15..0.25 ms with the new estimator versus 0.05..0.13 ms before (hop = 5.3 / 10.7 / 21.3 ms at 1k / 2k / 4k, under 5 % of a hop). Real gen in Max (`scripts/measure_harmsplit_cpu.py`, NonRealTime wall seconds per second of audio, 4 cores + stimulus, VM): old core 0.39 / 0.17 / 0.18 (1k / 2k / 4k), new core 0.23 / 0.21 / 0.19, i.e. the difference is inside the launch-time noise of the method (no measurable cost, no time slicing needed). Gen lesson: a user function with a loop (`hs_cosr`) called from inside loops made the whole gen fail to compile silently (all outputs 0): the ripple correlation is inlined in `hs_sal`. No `audio_device` perf case.
- GenExpr lessons (see the `spectral_split_lib` header): no user function call in an `if` condition or in `poke()` arguments, single `return` at function level, variables first assigned inside an `if` are initialised before it.

---

## 26. Architecture of `devices/Freq Split.amxd` + `devices/Freq Split.adg` (slug `freqsplit`)

Bitwig Freq Split (Spectral) copy: a spectral SORTER that "divides the signal into frequency groups and sorts them into one of four channels" (1 red, 2 blue, 3 yellow, 4 magenta). Not a crossover: up to 1024 narrow splits rotate through the four channels. Use it as the FIRST device of EVERY chain of an Audio Effect Rack (the `.adg` does exactly that); ONE instance per rack (the leader, elected by the face through `spectral_link`) computes the masks and all four channels and publishes them in a ring; the other chains read their channel from it (rack machinery of section 22, reference Transient Split). Each instance outputs only its own channel (`Output` = Auto: the chain index, clamped to 4; outside a rack = all summed, computing alone). The four per-bin masks sum to 1, so the chains sum back to the input delayed by the constant latency **5120** (4096 + 1024 host-block margin). There is no signal analysis: masks depend only on the parameters, the FFT size and the sample rate.

### Bitwig -> Live mapping
- **Channel Section** (per-channel Gain `-inf..+18.1 dB`, Pan, On, Solo, per-channel FX chain, level meters) = the Audio Effect Rack's own chain volume / pan / Chain Activator / Solo and the devices after Freq Split in each chain. Not duplicated in the device. **Pre FX / Post FX chains** = devices before / after the rack. Because the rack's chain Volume sits after the device (and the Spectral Limiter is inside the device), Bitwig's note "each channel's Gain is applied before the limiter" does not hold: here the limiter acts before the chain gain.
- **Spectrum Display** (inspector Pre / Post) = the `Display` tab on the face (`Pre` default: the analysis spectrum, tinted by each column's channel mix; `Post`: the four channel outputs after masks and limiter). The display colours every frequency area with its channel colour(s) (stacked masks, crossfades blend).

### Parameters (all real automatable `live.*`; short names are unique)
| Short name | Range | Default | Gen message |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Splits` (Frequency Splits) | `1.00 .. 1024.00`, continuous, exponent 3 | `16.00` | `nsplit` |
| `Insert` (Split Insertion Direction) | `High` (arrow <-, at the right) / `Mid` (<->) / `Low` (->, at the left) | `High` | `sdir` 0 / 1 / 2 |
| `XFade` (Crossfade Amount) | `0 .. 100 %` | `0 %` | `xfadeamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Nudge` (Split Nudge) | `-4.00 .. +4.00`, wrapping, fractional crossfades | `0.00` | `nudgeamt` |
| `Spin` (Split Spin) | `-50.0 .. +50.0 %`, wrapping | `0 %` | `spinamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Bend` / `Pinch` | `-100 .. +100 %` each | `0 %` | `bendamt` / `pinchamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Limiter` (Spectral Limiter) / `Lim Thr` | Off / On; `-120 .. +12 dB` | Off / `0.0 dB` | `limon` / `limdb` |
| `Mix` | `0 .. 100 %` | `100 %` | `mixamt` (`* 0.01`) |
| `Size` | `1k / 2k / 4k` | **`4k`** | `fftn` (`1024 * 2^i`) |
| `Output` | `Auto / Ch 1..4 / All` | `Auto` | face resolves it, `outsel 0..4` |
| `Display` | `Pre / Post` | `Pre` | face only |
Linked between all instances of a rack: everything except `Output` and `Display` (`Mix` included: the rack-sum identity needs every instance on the same Mix). **Default Size is 4k** (was 2k): with up to 1024 splits on a log axis starting at 20 Hz a 2k bin (23 Hz) leaves the lowest regions below one bin; 4k (11.7 Hz) halves that. The latency stays 4096 at every size, only CPU grows (not measured).

### Frequency axis and pattern (`src/freqsplit_config.js` `fsPattern` / `fsMaskAt`; GenExpr `fs_pattern` / `fs_masks`)
- **Axis**: `x = 0..1` over log frequency 20 Hz .. 20 kHz (`f = 20 Hz * 1000^x`, sample-rate independent). Bins below 20 Hz / above 20 kHz clamp to the edge regions. Masks are evaluated at the **bin centres**, so regions narrower than a bin alias (modulation joy, intended); 1024 splits over 3 decades are about 0.0029 decade wide, i.e. 1 bin at about 1.7 kHz at 4k.
- **Splits n = K + f** (`K = floor(n)`, fraction `f`): the pattern has `F = K + 1` full regions of weight 1 plus one PARTIAL region of weight `f` inserted at the insertion point; the regions always span the whole axis (normalised by `T = F + f = n + 1`), so the others shrink as the new one grows. Slots in axis order: High `[1 x F, f]`, Mid `[1 x Lc, f, 1 x (F - Lc)]` (`Lc = ceil(F / 2)`), Low `[f, 1 x F]`. So a modulated `Splits` makes splits emerge from the right, the middle or the left. n = 1 gives 2 regions, n = 16 gives 17.
- **Channels**: slot base High `s`, Low `s + 3 - F` (top region = channel 4), Mid lower part from the bottom / upper part from the top; stable when the count grows (a partial region that becomes full recolours nothing). `ch = (base + floor(Nudge mod 4)) mod 4`; the fractional part `phi` crossfades the assignment: `mask_c = (1 - phi) m[c - a] + phi m[c - a - 1]` (still sums to 1). `Nudge +2` moves channel 1 -> 3 and 4 -> 2; +-4 wrap to 0.
- **Bin evaluation** (per bin, 2 `pow` + 1 `cos`): axis `x` -> inverse Bend / Pinch warp -> `q = wrap(p - Spin)` -> `t = q * T` -> slot and raised-cosine crossfade with its neighbour -> channel masks. Bend: midpoint `m = 0.5 + 0.4 Bend` (the dot's x), `u -> u^gamma`, `gamma = ln m / ln 0.5` (negative packs low, positive high). Pinch around `m`: `q = 3^Pinch`, the per-side normalised distance to `m` is raised to `q` (positive bunches toward the middle, negative toward the edges). Spin shifts the pattern by `Spin` (fraction of the axis) with wrap; the seam of the wrapped pattern is a hard edge.
- **Crossfade X**: at the boundary of two slots a raised-cosine transition of half width `X * 0.5 * min(width of the two slots)` (pattern units), so neighbouring transitions never overlap. Caveat: a split growing in at the middle (Mid) starts as a hard edge (the new thin region narrows the crossfade at that boundary).
- **Masks are cached**: refilled in a hop only when one of the controls, N or the sample rate changed (`chg` in the core). `fs_limit` and the Mix are as before / below.
- **Spectral Limiter** (`fs_limit`, only while on): per bin `g = min(1, thr / (mask * amp))`, `amp` = max(|L|,|R|); the channel mask is applied first (Bitwig: Gain before limiter; here the rack's chain gain comes after). A loud sine is capped to about 1.3 x the threshold (a Hann-windowed sine spreads over 3 bins). It breaks the sum identity (explicit gain); Output All limits the sum.
- **Mix identity in a rack**: `out = mix * wet_own + (1 - mix) * dry / K`, dry delayed by the same 5120 samples (`Delay dryL(5121)`, template), `K = 4` while the instance outputs one channel (Auto in a rack, `Ch n`), `K = 1` for Output All / outside a rack. The rack sums four chains: `mix * input + (1 - mix) * input`, identity for every Mix. Caveat: the dry share passes through each chain's devices (FX in a chain also process dry / 4; a chain's volume / mute changes the dry level too); a rack with fewer than four chains no longer sums to the input. `Mix` is slewed (one-pole 0.002 / sample, same in every instance). Output All is a normal dry/wet.

### Presentation Layout (`480 x 169` px, Ableton-native (Live Reverb style) look: Live theme colours via `src/spectral_theme.js` / `scripts/spectral_common.py`, section 22)
- **Section `Splits` (`x = 4..340`)**: header row (`y = 20`) `Splits` numbox, `Insert` label + header v8ui `obj-viz-top` (`x = 134, 84 x 15`, `src/freqsplit_top_ui.js` with `spectral_theme.js` prepended: three live.tab-style cells `<- <-> ->`, click = set `Insert`, selected one orange), `XFade` label + numbox. Bottom row (`y = 143`): `Nudge`, `Spin`, `Bend`, `Pinch` LCD numboxes (value-colour text, no frames).
- **Display** (`obj-viz`, v8ui, `x = 8, y = 38, w = 328, h = 100`, `src/freqsplit_ui.js`): near-black LCD (82 px plot, faint grid at 100 / 1k / 10k Hz) with the channel regions as faint tints (alpha 0.15, others 0.05 when this instance's channel is resolved), split lines in scale-text grey, region numbers in muted channel colours, the Pre spectrum as a thin muted outline with faint fill (Post: four muted channel curves), a hollow orange XY handle with faint crosshair, the limiter line (1 px orange + hollow `T` handle + dB value, only while on or dragged). Legend row under the LCD (`Red Blue Yellow Magenta` swatches, the output channel bright, `Chain 2/4, linked` right). The four channel colours are the only non-theme colours, always muted (`sptMuted` / low alpha). Interactions unchanged.
