Section panels (`obj-panel-spl/spc/out`) and the body panel are recoloured by the face (`sptApplyPanelTheme`).
- **Bottom row (`y = 146`)**: `Nudge`, `Spin`, `Bend`, `Pinch` numboxes with labels.
- **Right column (`x = 344..476`)**: section `Spectrum` (`Display` tab Pre | Post, `Limiter` live.text toggle in theme colours + threshold numbox) and section `Output` (`Output` menu, `Size` tab, `Mix` native `live.dial` without colour attributes, `Lat 5120 smp` note). Section panels then the body panel last; no `live.*` overlaps a v8ui; two v8uis in all.
- `scripts/render_preview.py` does not apply `parameter_units` strings (the preview shows `16.0` / `0.0` where Live shows `16.00` / `0.00 %`) and draws every numbox as a plain bar; the preview display state is the control defaults (16 splits, High, 0 %, Pre, Output Auto -> chain 2).

### Telemetry
`buffer~ ---freqsplitscope @samps 704` (bound on loadbang by `scope ---freqsplitscope`; the face gets `setbuffer ---freqsplitscope`): `0..127` Mid level per column, `128..639` the four channels after masks / limiter (4 x 128, 0..1 over -100..0 dB), `640` frame counter, `641` samplerate, `642` N, `643` awake, `644` input peak, `645` latency, `646` output selection, `647` limiter on. About every 1024 samples; the face polls at 15 fps. Split positions are not in the telemetry (the face computes them with the same `fsPattern`).

### Rack preset
`devices/Freq Split.adg`: one Audio Effect Rack, chains `Red`, `Blue`, `Yellow`, `Magenta`, each holding the device with `Output = Auto`.

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_freqsplit.py && node tests/test_freqsplit.js && python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/Freq Split.amxd"   # preview: previews/freq_split.png
scripts/vm_spectral.sh audio freqsplit        # real Max, in the VM (optional case names)
```
Two typecheck configs: `tsconfig.freqsplit.json` (config + link + canvas) and `tsconfig.freqsplit-top.json` (header strip).
`tests/test_freqsplit.js`: pattern invariants over 36000 parameter sets (every direction, counts 1..1024 incl. fractions, Bend / Pinch / Spin / Crossfade / Nudge: split positions sorted and on the axis, region count = splits + 1, masks sum to 1, in 0..1); warp monotonic with an exact inverse; default 17-region rotation; Nudge integer / fractional (linear crossfade) / wrap at +-4; Spin slides the pattern (equals the unspun pattern shifted), wraps, +-50 % identical; Bend / Pinch directions; Crossfade 0 = hard, 100 % wide; **fractional splits grow in at the top / middle / bottom, keep their channel and recolour nothing when they become full**; bin masks sum to 1 for 3 sizes x 3 rates x counts up to 1024; the 4-chain Mix identity; the Python mask port used by the audio runner equals the JS reference; the GenExpr functions transpiled to JS (masks vs reference, four-channel sum null, sines in the predicted channel, fractional Nudge shares evenly, limiter cap / pass, silence, telemetry bounds); GenExpr lint; the link module; the built device (header, latency, 169 px, geometry, no live.* over a v8ui, panels last, `---` buffer names, parameter table incl. units, wiring, `.adg`).
Audio cases (`scripts/run_freqsplit_audio_tests.py`, probe `tests/freqsplit_probe.js`, five real core instances per case: outsel 1..4 and 0; the Python port predicts where sines land; `--dump-masks` is the Node cross-check hook): `sum_*` (default / 1k / 2k, 1 split, 3.5, 1024 at Crossfade 50 %, 1024 Mid shaped, 7.5 Low with Nudge 2.5, 32 Mid Crossfade 100 %, 100.3 with Bend / Pinch / Spin / Nudge; the four channels vs the input delayed 4096, bound -90 dB), `iso_*` (sines at region centres, isolation > 40 dB: Nudge 0 / 1 / 2, Mid, Low, Spin, fractional count 16.5, a shaped layout), `nudge_half`, `iso_change` (nsplit and Nudge changed mid-run: catches a stale mask cache), `lim_on` / `lim_off`, `mix_30` / `mix_0` (rack sum identity; at Mix 0 each chain carries dry / 4, Output All the dry), `silence`, `latency_1k/2k/4k` (exactly 4096), `telemetry`. Measured in the VM (all 30 cases pass): every `sum_*` null -149.0 .. -150.2 dB (1024 splits, fractional counts / Nudge, Size 1k / 2k / 4k included), isolation 87.5 dB (a 112 Hz sine, 9 splits Low) .. 201 dB, 4096-sample latency exact at every size, `nudge_half` 0.0500 + 0.0500, limiter 0.8 sine -> 0.133 at -20 dB (limit 0.1) and the quiet sine 0.0300 unchanged, `mix_30` null -150.7 dB, `mix_0` chains at exactly 1/16 of the dry energy and Output All an exact identity.

### Known rough edges
- **Linking is verified only with mocks** (`tests/spectral_link_tests.js`), not in real Live by this device's own test. Between a parameter change and its application by the peers (about 80-160 ms) the masks of the instances differ, so the chains do not sum to exactly 1 for a few frames; Size changes mute each instance on its own schedule. `Mix` is linked, so a rack with unlinked instances breaks the dry identity.
- The shared Live test (`scripts/test_spectral_live.js`) was written for the old parameter set: `Spin` now spans +-50 % (a value of 30 is fine), the new parameters (`Mix`, `Display`, `Insert`) are not covered.
- Low-frequency resolution is limited by the bins (4k: 11.7 Hz). Regions narrower than a bin alias; a sine within about 3 bins of an edge is shared between two channels (Hann main lobe). With 16 splits the isolation of sines at region centres is 50+ dB.
- Mid insertion: the rotation has a seam in the middle (two neighbouring regions can share a channel when `F` is odd), and a split inserted exactly on a crossfaded boundary starts hard (see Crossfade).
- The limiter caps a loud sine near, not exactly at, the threshold. CPU is not measured: a hop does two FFTs plus (only when a control changed) the mask fill (2049 bins x 2 `pow`) in one sample; modulating `Splits` / `Spin` refills every hop. No `audio_device` perf case.


---

## 27. Architecture of `devices/Reverb+.amxd` + `devices/Reverb+ End.amxd` + `devices/Reverb+.adg` (reverb whose output runs through a Live device chain)

A sibling of Delay+ (section 15) that reuses its cross-device machinery. **Six algorithms** (Plate, Hall, Room, Chamber, Spring, Convolution with your own impulse-response file) in ONE gen core; the reverb's output goes through the devices between `[Reverb+]` and `[Reverb+ End]` and the processed result recirculates through a flat-magnitude loop back into the chain input (the "chain loop"; a Shifter in the chain makes a shimmer). The face (layout `base`, "Hybrid tabs") was chosen from prototypes; see "Face" below.

### Topology
- `[Reverb+] [user FX ...] [Reverb+ End]` in one Audio Effect Rack chain (`devices/Reverb+.adg` ships the rack with an empty nested Audio Effect Rack `Reverb+ FX` (one empty chain `FX`, drop your FX in there) between the pair and the End saved collapsed: `<IsFolded Value="true" />` on its `MxDeviceAudioEffect`, Live's per-device folded flag (`view.is_collapsed` 1 in the LOM). Written by `scripts/make_delayplus_sets.py --middle fxrack --fold-end`: a nested rack in a preset is a `GroupDevicePreset Id=<list index>` entry of the chain's `DevicePresets` (as in Live's own drum-rack presets), in a Set an `AudioEffectGroupDevice`. The test sets carry a `... Preset` track built the same way (`--track "Name::start.amxd::fxrack::fold"`), checked by `tests/adg_check.js` (the .adg XML) and `scripts/live_preset_check.js` (in Live: structure, End collapsed, link, L == 0, L follows a Limiter put in the inner chain, meter)). The Start gets the dry signal and outputs **only the reverb wet** into the chain; the End mixes the latency-aligned dry with the processed wet (equal-power `Dry/Wet`, `Output`, `Ducking`) and writes the processed wet back to the Start (`xfer` ch2/3) as the outer-loop return.
- **Chain loop (DERIVED: the user sets neither its time nor its amount; the chain is truly IN the reverb's feedback)**: the return from End does NOT go through the tank (a tank's resonance peaks, up to 20x its rms gain, forced a loop gain of 0.04..0.09 and each pass was about -26 dB, so a Shifter in the chain was heard once). It runs through a **recirculating path with a flat magnitude response**: return -> `lpr` ring (written when it arrives, `B + L` samples after it left; read `period - B - L` later, linearly interpolated, the period glides by one-pole 2000 samples) -> **4 Schroeder allpasses per channel** (`apd`, `apl`; lengths 149 / 233 / 367 / 523 L and 163 / 257 / 389 / 547 R samples at 48 kHz x `min(sr / 48000, 2)`, coefficient `0.3 + 0.4 x Diffusion`; |H| = 1 exactly) -> HF damping (one-pole at `HF Damping`, bypassed at 20 kHz and in Convolution, |H| <= 1) -> bass shelf (FDN algorithms: `x + (shelf - 1) lowpass_xover(x)`, `shelf = g^(1/bass - 1)`) -> gain `g` -> the feedback clip (`Clip Mode` Soft / Hard / Limit, `Clip Level`, Delay+ semantics, on this loop signal) -> **added to the chain input**: `chain input = wet x sqrt(1 - g^2) + loop`. The tank is fed from the pre-delayed input only (feed-forward). `Loop Feedback` / `Loop Time` are gone from the parameter table. A wet sample re-enters the chain exactly `period` samples after it left (the loop never passes the tank or the convolution engine). Freeze fades the loop gain with the input (10 ms): the frozen tank passes the chain once, nothing compounds through the chain. Unpaired, the return is 0 (`gLink`), the wet scale is 1 and the tank runs at the full Decay (a plain reverb). See "The derived chain loop" below for the formulas and the stability proof.
- **Unpaired Start** = a plain reverb (Dry/Wet and Output are applied inside the Start, no ducking; the output blends over 512 samples as the End appears / goes away). Delay+ passes dry when unpaired; a standalone reverb is more useful. **End without a Start** passes its input through. The Start reports latency 0 (its wet path is internal).
- Cross-device timing, `K` / `B` calibration (counter statistics, then an audio cross-correlation in the End), the pairing by bracket matching, the `Global("reverbplus_bus")` registry, `L` from `latency_in_samples` (racks counted as their longest chain) are copies of Delay+ (`src/reverbplus_link.js`, `src/reverbplus_end.genexpr`); buffer names are `---rpxfer` (5 ch x 65536), `---rphdr` (64), `---rpscope` (1024), `---rpspec`, `---rpir`, `---rpirspec`, `---rpirview` (`---` names, not `#0-`: in a top-level device `#0` is not expanded).

### The derived chain loop (Start gen block rate, `src/reverbplus_config.js` `RP_LOOP`, mirrored by `tests/test_reverbplus.js`)
The user sets neither the loop time nor its amount.
- **Loop time** `T = max(K[algo] x tc, MIN)`, `MIN = B + L + 4`, at most 0.25 s, whole samples (`rpLoopPeriod`). `tc` = the tank's characteristic period at the current Size: Hall / Room / Chamber the mean line length (`lsum / n`; 2945 / 578 / 1035 samples at Size 50 %), Plate the mean of the two branch loops, Spring the mean spring loop + 48 samples. `K` = Plate 0.3, Hall 1.25, Room 6, Chamber 3.5, Spring 1.5 (65..91 ms at the default Size 60 %: Plate 91 ms, Hall 86, Room 81, Chamber 85, Spring 65; 24..131 ms over the Size range). Convolution: 15 % of the processed IR length (`hdr[36]`), 40..150 ms. Below MIN the loop is clamped and `CLAMPED` (scope 11) is set. The target is snapped on every layout rebuild.
- **Loop gain** `g = min(10^(-3 (T + AP) / (Decay sr)), 0.97)` (`rpLoopGain`; `AP` = 1.5 x the allpass chain's summed length (mean of L and R; the mean group delay is the sum itself, the smear of each recirculation adds half of it): 1.9k samples at 48 kHz; Convolution's reference is `max(0.5 x IR length, 0.1 s)`). Typically 0.5..0.9 (Hall, Size 60 %: Decay 1 s 0.55, 2.5 s 0.77, 6 s 0.90). Without the `AP` term the smear of the allpasses (each recirculation is spread over about 0.1 s) made the measured RT60 3x Decay in the reference model.
- **Tank share**: while an End is paired (`lkS`, 512-sample ramp) the tank runs at `Decay x TANK_FRAC`, `TANK_FRAC = 0.3` (not for Convolution: the IR cannot be shortened); the chain loop carries the long tail (the telemetry RT60 slots 22..24 keep showing the user's Decay). With the tank at Decay the convolution of two equal decays lengthens the tail (`t e^(-at)`); at 0.3 the late decay is the loop's, i.e. Decay.
- **Energy accounting**: the recirculations add `1 / (1 - g^2)` to the wet's energy, so the wet enters the chain scaled by `sqrt(1 - g^2)` (`scs`, smoothed, 1 when unpaired): the wet's total energy stays that of the tank alone (reference model 0.98..1.03). The first pass is therefore quieter at long Decays, like any constant-energy reverb.
- **Stability proof** (a chain with unity gain, any frequency): the allpasses have `|H| = 1` exactly (`tests/test_reverbplus.js` runs the gen's own `rp_ap`: unit energy and a flat spectrum within 0.1 %), the damping one-pole has `|H| <= 1`, the bass shelf `|1 + (shelf - 1) H_lp| <= max(1, shelf)`, so `|loop(w)| <= g max(1, g^(1/bass - 1)) = max(g, g^(1/bass)) < 1` (`rpLoopMagnitude` checked on a 4000-point grid for bass 0.25..4, g 0.3..0.97, Diffusion 0..1). Undamped and unshelved the magnitude is exactly g at every frequency. By the small-gain theorem the closed loop is stable and every recirculation loses at least `g^2` of energy. A chain with gain G > 1/g is bounded by the clip (it saturates and sustains at the clip level: Soft / Hard / Limit), not by the gain.
- **Reference model** (`tests/reverbplus_loopsim.js`, used by `tests/test_reverbplus.js`): a Hall-like FDN (real lengths, Hadamard, per-line RT60 gains, the gen's output normalisation, fed from the input only) plus the loop (delay, the gen's allpass lengths / coefficient, gain, chain gain, soft clip, wet scale). Tests: RT60 / Decay 0.886..1.143 over 3 sizes x 3 Decays x 3 loop times (limit 15 %); total energy / the tank's 0.98..1.03; Decay 60 s with chain gain 1 / 1.5 / 3 never runs away (peak < 1.5); a unity chain loses at least `g^2` per recirculation; the gen's constants equal the config's.
- **Telemetry**: `LOOP_MS` (9) = the loop time in use, `LOOP_GAIN` (30) = `g` (typically 0.5..0.9), `TANK_RT` (31) = the RT60 the tank runs at. The face shows none of it (it only warns when the loop is clamped, `CLAMPED`); the audio and Live tests read them.

- **Plate**: Dattorro figure-8 (4 input diffusers, per branch modulated allpass -> delay -> damping -> decay allpass -> delay, cross-fed, 14 output taps), lengths at 29761 Hz scaled to the rate and by `0.75 x size`. RT60: the per-branch gain is `10^(-3 tau_branch / RT60)` (the loop crosses both branches).
- **Hall**: 16-line FDN (lengths 1031..4861 samples @48k, Hadamard mixing, `1/sqrt(n)`), stereo input through 4 Schroeder allpass diffusers per side (gain `0.7 Diffusion`), output = signed sums of the lines (two different sign patterns for L / R), line delays modulated by `Mod Depth` (6 samples at 100 %) at `Mod Rate`, 16 early-reflection taps. **Room**: 8 lines (263..919), shorter diffusers, early reflections 3..38 ms. **Chamber**: 8 lines (541..1553), dense early cluster 2..58 ms at level 1.1. All three share the one FDN code (`nl` = 16 or 8).
- **FDN line filter** (per line, in the loop): two cascaded first-order LOW SHELVES (DC gain `s = sqrt(g_low / g_mid)` each, 1 above `Crossover`; 12 dB/oct), then the mid gain `g_mid`, then `HF Damping` as a one-pole on the whole line signal. `g_low` uses `Decay x Bass Mult`. So the RT60 is the asked one in the middle band, `Bass Mult` times longer well below the crossover, shorter above the damping frequency. **Why shelves and not a complementary low / high split with a gain per band**: with different gains on the two bands, and the damping on the high band only, the loop gain exceeds 1 near the crossover (+0.5 dB at 700 Hz with Damping 2 kHz measured as a 7 s ring in a 1.5 s Chamber; an unstable tank at Decay 60 s). A first-order shelf is passive for any gain ratio (|1 + (s - 1) LP1| <= max(1, s)).
- **Early reflections** (Hall, Room, Chamber): 16 taps from one mono line: time = first + (last - first) x ((k + 0.5 + 0.35 jitter)/16)^power (deterministic jitter, alternating L / R, signs from a hash), level `exp(-t / tau)`, normalised to unit energy x the set's level; times scale with Size and glide.
- **Spring**: two loops (`D` = 32 ms x size scale and 1.37x that), each a 32-stage chain of first-order allpasses with coefficient `-(0.2 + 0.6 Diffusion)` (low frequencies delayed more: the chirp / drip) inside a feedback delay, damped by a one-pole; outputs cross-mixed (0.35). `Size` = spring length, `Diffusion` = dispersion.
- **Convolution**: below.
- **Algorithm change**: fade out 40 ms (output and tank input), clear ALL algorithm memory in 16384-sample slices per sample (about 140 samples), rebuild the line layout, fade in 40 ms. The tank input fades with the output because a running signal would otherwise hit the empty lines of the new algorithm as a step (the `switch` audio case measured a 100x click without it).
- **Freeze**: `ig` (10 ms) fades the input and the return out; the same smoothing blends every loop gain to exactly 1, the damping one-pole to a pass-through (the bass split then sums back to the input exactly) and the modulated delays to integer lengths, so the FDN / Plate / Spring loops are lossless (Hadamard is orthogonal, allpasses have unit gain). Convolution has no Freeze (inactive).
- **Safety**: the wet output is linear below 2 and soft-limited to 6 above; `fixnan`.
### Rack sharing and deferred DSP (phase 2 optimisation, 2026-10-22)
- **Design (measured, see below):** the core is `src/freqsplit_core.genexpr` expanded through `src/spectral_split_rack_main.genexpr` (hooks `ctl` = Params -> History, `frame` = mask refill + limiter, `tele` = columns). Mask rows: `fsd` rows 0..3 raw masks, 4 ones, **5..8 final channel masks, 9 final All** (the template applies row 5 + c; `fs_masks` writes both sets, `fs_limit` overwrites 5..9 per frame while the limiter is on; the limiter switch is part of the refill key). The leader serves all four channels (`serve` 15): one forward FFT, four mask / inverse FFT / OLA passes per hop; followers run no FFT. Masks depend only on the parameters, so hop phase never mattered for the static sum; the gain is CPU plus coherent switching: a linked control change is applied once, by the leader (audio case `rack_change` nulls < -90 dB through a change that reaches the instances 3 ms apart).
- **Alternatives:** hop clock alone (every instance computes its own channel) costs the same as the old design (measured `hop_alone` / old about 1.0-1.4 within noise): no benefit. A leader that derives channel 4 as (input - channels 1..3) would need 3 inverse FFTs instead of 4 (measured with `serve3`, see numbers) but needs a change to the shared template (a skip flag for one served channel, a time-domain subtract after the loop), and `ss_mid` (Mid amplitude + atan2 of every bin) runs every hop in the template although Freq Split only needs it for telemetry (`no_mid` variant): both are recommendations for `spectral_split_rack_main.genexpr`, not done device-locally to avoid forking it. The template's `Data cols(512)` holds 4 slots; the `tele` hook writes the 5 telemetry slots in two `ss_tele` calls.
- **Telemetry layout moved:** 0..639 columns (leader only), 640 frame, 641 sr, 642 N, 643 awake, 644 level, 646 latency, 647 selection, 648..657 rack scalars (computing, following, aligned, fallback, counter offset, underruns, lag, serve, role, alive), 658 limiter (`FS_SC_*`, `FS_RACK_CHANNELS`, `FS_GEN_PARAMS` in the config). The face reads the leader's scope while following (`displayScope()`), header first (frame, awake): a poll without a new frame costs 0.5 us (was 8.9 us, Node mock).
- **Deferred DSP:** the device ships without its gen~ (hub `ss_genin` = `gate 1 1`, `ss_plugin` / `ss_plugout`, ring `---ssring`, `setnames ---freqsplitscope ---ssring`, test hook `receive #0-sstest` -> `dsp_restart <chain>`); `src/spectral_dsp.js` creates it after `init_link` (pill `DSP loading...` / `DSP failed`, silent inside a rack until it runs). The header strip only redraws when the arrow changes.
- **Numbers (VM, shared and noisy, harness `scripts/measure_freqsplit.py`):** gen compile 0.46 s (old core) -> 0.94 s (rack core, created after the GUI, one file-backed compile per rack instead of 4 embedded ones). CPU per 4-chain rack (percent of one core): 1k old 4.5 / shared 3.9 / serve3 2.8 / no_mid 3.9; 2k and 4k see the raw log (the measurement noise of +-1 % is as large as the effect at these sizes; the structural saving is 8 transforms per hop-group -> 5, i.e. about 0.6x).
- **Tests:** `node tests/test_freqsplit.js` also requires `tests/test_spectral_rack.js` / `test_spectral_dsp.js`; audio runner: six instances per case (outsel 1..4, 0 and a SELF reference of channel 2), `rack_*` cases (sums at 1k / 2k / 4k, shaped / fractional, isolation, limiter through the ring, Mix 30 / 0, silence, pending, delayed follower 37 / 300 / 1301 samples, beyond the span, different input fallback, impulse latency 5120, leader telemetry, staggered change, Size change). All pass. `self_stagger` prints the sum of the old design for the same staggered change.
- **For the shared Live checks:** mark Freq Split `rack: true` in `scripts/test_spectral_live.js` (config constants `FS_SC_CMP/FOL/ALIGNED/FB/ROLE/SERVE/ALIVE/LAG/UNDER/LATENCY` exist; latency `FS_LATENCY` = 5120, trace tag ` freqsplit dsp ready`); expect one leader, serve 15, followers aligned offset 0, no fallback, no underrun. `sumtest` still covers Transient Split only. Not verified in Live.
- **Rough edge:** a rack follower with Output All and the limiter on gets the sum of the four limited channels (not the limited All); the leader only serves the four channel rows.


### Convolution (`src/reverbplus_conv.genexpr`: functions of Data arguments, run in Node by `tests/test_reverbplus.js` against direct convolution)
- **Two-stage uniform partitioning, stereo IR (mono IR -> both channels), time-sliced**: head = 128-sample blocks, FFT 256, 32 partitions = IR[0, 4096); tail = 2048-sample blocks, FFT 4096, up to 140 partitions = the rest (IR max 290816 samples = 6.06 s at 48 kHz; longer is truncated with a 4096-sample fade). One packed FFT of `L + jR` per block, unpacked into per-channel spectra for the frequency-domain delay lines (`fdh` 32 slots, `fdt` 142 slots), the multiply-accumulate result packed back into ONE inverse FFT. Jobs (load, bit reversal, one butterfly stage per job, unpack, 33 MAC chunks of 64 bins, output) run one per 4 (head) / 8 (tail) samples, so no sample runs more than one FFT pass or one MAC chunk (`rpc_pass`, the twiddle table is `fft_init` of the shared cepstrum library). The tail's output block `b = m + 2` only needs input blocks <= `m` (H = 2 x P2), which gives every job a whole block of slack.
- **Latency 256 samples (2 x 128)**, hidden inside the pre-delay: the effective pre-delay is `max(Pre-Delay, 5.3 ms)` (`rpEffectivePredelayMs`, scope `PREDELAY_MS`); the gen reads the pre-delay line `256` samples shorter.
- **IR spectra** are computed by the engine in JS (`rpIrSpectra`: head partitions of 128, tail of 2048, `[Lre, Lim, Rre, Rim]` per bin 0..N/2, float32) and written into `---rpirspec` (two banks of `BANK_FLOATS` = 1163952 floats, 9.3 MB total). `---rpirview` gets the processed IR's 512-point dB envelope and 512 min / max pairs for displays.
- **Bank swap**: the engine writes the idle bank, then the header (bank, counts, length, loaded, channels, sr mismatch) and the VERSION last; the gen fades the convolution output out over 25 ms when it sees a new version, swaps `cp` (bank / counts) at zero and fades in 25 ms (about a 50 ms crossfade, one convolution's cost). The FDL keeps the INPUT spectra, so the new IR convolves the recent past correctly.
- **IR pipeline** (`rpIrProcess`): Reverse, Start trim (% of the IR), Stretch (resample by Stretch % x device rate / file rate, Hermite; a sample-rate mismatch flags `IR RESAMPLED`), IR Decay (exponential fade reaching -60 dB at that fraction of the IR; 100 = untouched), length cap, normalisation to mean channel energy 1 (a Dirac stays a Dirac). Done in slices on a Task (`rpIrWork`: 6 ms per 8 ms tick); changing an IR control reprocesses after 200 ms from the file as loaded (kept in the engine).
- **IR file**: `IR File` is a `live.drop` in `parameter_type 4` / parameter mode (Better Sampler's handling: a live.drop only outputs in parameter mode, Live saves the path and replays it on load; `dropfile` ignores a replay of the path already loaded). The face's `Browse` goes `cmd browse_ir` -> engine -> `opendialog sound` -> `browsed` (also stored in the live.drop with `set`). `replace <path>` goes to `buffer~ ---rpir 1000 2`; its done bang sends `ir_loaded`; the engine reads it in 64k-frame chunks.

### Engine / face split and the protocol (`src/reverbplus_engine.js`)
Unlike Delay+, nothing visual owns the machinery. `obj-engine` (a v8ui NOT in presentation, 5 outlets) owns the parameter store (`rpRaw`), the link layer, the deferred gen~ (AGENTS 1d: `GEN_DSP_TEXT` / hash / `RP_GEN_PARAMS`, `/tmp/reverbplus_start_<hash>.gendsp`, hub `gate 1 1` = `rp_genin`, `rp_plugin` / `rp_plugout`, resend every 150 ms until scope `FRAMES` moves, trace in `Global("reverbplus_trace")`), the IR pipeline, the panel colours (`RP_PANEL_THEME`, `max.getcolor`) and the greying of inactive parameters. Faces are display v8uis built from `config + reverbplus_face_common.js + the layout's files` and receive `set_<key>` for the keys they subscribe to, `names`, and the state bus (`s ---rpstate` -> every face): `state dsp <ready> <error|->`, `state link <paired> <status> <latency> <partner>`, `state ir <status> <loaded> <len> <sr> <channels> <srMismatch> <name|->`. A face drives parameters with `outlet(0, "param", key, value)` (build: `route param cmd` -> `route <every key>` -> the `live.*` object; `cmd browse_ir | clear_ir` -> engine). Test hook: `receive #0-rptest` -> engine (`dropfile <path>`, `test_report` -> `Global("reverbplus_test").report_<id>`).

### Telemetry (`RP_SCOPE` in the config; `tests/test_reverbplus.js` checks every slot the core writes; 64 samples)
Start writes every 64 samples: `FRAMES` 0, `SR`, `ALGO` (running), `FREEZE`, `PAIRED`, `B`, `L`, `K`, `MIN_MS` (minimum loop), `LOOP_MS` (in use), `PREDELAY_MS` (effective), `CLAMPED`, peaks of dry in `IN_PK` 12 / wet out `WET_PK` 14 / loop return `RET_PK` 15 (the faces' idle detection), `ASLEEP` 13 (the tank's idle sleep, below), `CLIP_DB` 18, theoretical RT60 `RT60_LO` / `_MID` / `_HI` 22..24 (the face's predicted-decay curve), IR flags 25..28 (loaded, length, version, sample-rate mismatch), `ALGO_STATE` 29 (0 normal, 1 fading out, 2 clearing, 3 fading in), `LOOP_GAIN` 30, `TANK_RT` 31, the re-time (mute) counters `MUTE_N` / `MUTE_L` / `MUTE_B` / `MUTE_K` 32..35 (a mute is counted only when it starts) and `GLINK` 36 (the live gain of the mute). **Removed 2026-10-22** (nothing read them but the telemetry test): the measured RT60 estimator, the 256-entry decay ring, the 256-block waveform ring, 16 tank-line energies, 16 early-reflection taps, the input / wet RMS slots, the tank / End-out peaks and the tank-line count (a per-sample min / max, a per-512-sample log, a per-64-sample 16-line loop of `log10` and, in the FDN, Plate and Spring loops, a per-line energy one-pole each); the buffer shrank from 1024 to 64 samples and the high slots moved down. **Spectra**: instead of 64 band filters in the gen the Start only pokes two 4096-sample rings (`---rpspec`: wet = what leaves the Start, dry = the input; 2 pokes per sample) and `rpSpectrum32(buf, which)` (Hann FFT 4096 + 32 log bands, 40 Hz..18 kHz) runs in the face while the Reverb tab is shown. The End writes `---rpscopeE` (linked, K, L, wet / dry peak, duck gain, B, out peaks).

### Build / Test / Preview
```bash
npm run -s typecheck && python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py && node tests/test_reverbplus.js && node tests/test_reverbplus_face.js && for v in "Reverb+" "Reverb+ End"; do python3 scripts/render_preview.py "devices/$v.amxd"; done   # previews/reverb.png, reverb_end.png (+ reverb_tone / tail / conv: see "Face")
scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh audio [case ...]    # real Max suite in the VM (shared harness, NonRealTime)
scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh run                 # Live: live + test under the Live lock (reverbplus_test.als = 3 tracks, rack [Reverb+, Limiter 6 ms, Reverb+ End])
```
`tests/test_reverbplus.js`: config math, the IR pipeline, the conv functions vs direct convolution (head only, head + tail, bank 1, one-sample tail), GenExpr lint (function order, single return, no `e<digit>`, no user call nested in `poke()`, no function-local variable named like a global Data), every `poke(scope, ..)` / `hdr` slot against `RP_SCOPE` / `RP_HDR`, **every layout** in `scripts/reverbplus_layouts` built into a temp dir (contract validation + device structure + every face v8ui run with `preview()` and `paint()` against a mock mgraphics), `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js` sub-test, the built Start and End devices.

### Measured (VM, real Max, NonRealTime at 48 kHz; `scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh audio`; Live 12.4.6 for the Live numbers)
- **RT60** (Schroeder backward integration, line through -5..-25 dB, 1 kHz band, Q 1.5; impulse into the Start alone, no filters, Damping 20 kHz): Decay 1.5 s / 3 s / Size 100 % / Size 0 % (s):
  Plate 1.407 / 3.058 / 1.521 / 1.513; Hall 1.454 / 2.974 / 1.473 / 1.428; Room 1.406 / 2.839 / 1.456 / 1.348; Chamber 1.456 / 2.803 / 1.412 / 1.371; Spring 1.528 / 3.022 / 1.474 / 1.642. Worst error 10 % (Room, Size 0), all within the 15 % gate. Plate reads 6 % short because the allpass delays count in the loop time but not as pure delay.
- **Bass Mult 2** (RT60 at 100 Hz / at 1 kHz, Crossover 350 Hz): Hall 1.79, Chamber 1.74, Room 1.60 (short lines: the shelf has fewer passes to act). **HF Damping 2 kHz**: RT60 at 6 kHz / 700 Hz: Hall 0.43, Plate 0.70, Room 0.25, Chamber 0.27, Spring 0.29.
- **Stability**: Decay 60 s with Bass Mult 4 (Hall, Chamber) or 0.25 (Plate, Room, Spring): the level falls steadily (0.8..2.7 dB per second depending on the Bass Mult), never grows.
- **Level** (energy of the impulse response of an amp-1 impulse, 0 dB = unit gain): Plate +1.6..+2.4 dB, Hall -4.3..-5.1, Room -2.9..-3.6, Chamber -1.1..-2.4, Spring -5.3..-7.4.
- **Freeze**: the level trend over 2 s (four 500 ms windows) is 0.08..0.27 dB (Plate 0.14, Hall 0.11, Room 0.13, Chamber 0.27, Spring 0.08); the window-to-window scatter of the noise-like frozen tail is 0.09..0.40 dB. Plate: while frozen the tank's delay reads (delay 1, decay allpass 2, delay 2) snap to whole samples like the modulated allpass already did (`fz` blend); fractional linear-interpolated reads inside the loop low-passed it and made the level creep (0.54 dB / 2 s before).
- **Algorithm switch** Hall -> Chamber on a 220 Hz sine: maximum second difference 0.00030 after the switch against 0.00022 steady (before the input fade it was 0.026, 100x), state back to 0, algorithm 3 running.
- **Chain loop (derived)**, paired Start -> unity chain (`delay~ 256`, gain 1) -> End, Dry/Wet 100 %, RT60 of the 1 kHz band from an impulse (`lrt_*`; Decay s / Size): Plate 1.372 / 2.831 / 0.880 (1.5 s / Size 0.6, 3 s / 1, 1 s / 0), Hall 1.389 / 2.812 / 0.960, Room 1.383 / 2.828 / 1.003, Chamber 1.342 / 2.875 / 0.955, Spring 1.410 / 2.796 / 0.877. Worst error 12.3 % (Spring, Decay 1 s), all inside the 15 % gate. **Measured per-pass loop gain** (`period_*`, `lrt_*` telemetry): 0.54..0.83 (Hall 0.557 at Decay 1.5 s, 0.686 at 3 s, 0.609 at 1 s / Size 0; at Decay 2 s / Size 0.3 Plate 0.73, Hall 0.71, Room 0.75, Chamber 0.75, Spring 0.78), against 0.048..0.089 before; the loop time equals K x tc to the sample. Tank RT60 telemetry = 0.3 x Decay. **Shimmer** (`shimmer`: Hall, Decay 3 s, a 200 ms 468.75 Hz burst, a +12 st granular shifter in the chain, unity gain, latency 1024): tone energy relative to 2 f: 4 f -2.5 dB, 8 f -5.2 dB (8 f vs 4 f -2.7 dB; predicted `g^2` per pass = -2.5 dB at g 0.747), the unshifted f -38.9 dB; energy centroids 0.15 / 0.26 / 0.36 s after the burst (2 f, 4 f, 8 f appear successively, one loop period apart plus the shifter delay), level -30.3 dB at 0.5..1.5 s, -89.2 dB at 3..4 s, peak 0.40. Convolution on a Dirac: the first recirculation lands exactly the derived loop time later (1920, MIN 2468 with a 2400-sample chain, flagged; no +256: the loop does not pass the convolution engine), its ratio to the wet impulse = loop gain x allpass^4 (0.0014 / 0.0006). Chain gain 1.5 (`lg15_*`, Decay 4, noise burst): the loop gain 1.5 g exceeds 1, so the loop saturates at the clip and sustains (level -1.6..+0 dB steady, peaks 1.50..1.73, no growth, no NaN). Chain gain 20 with a Hard clip at -6 dB (`loopclip`): after the tank has decayed the chain input sits exactly at the 0.5012 ceiling, output peak 14.7. Freeze with the loop paired (`freezeL_hall`): 0.06 dB / 2 s. Unpaired Start (`rt_*`, unchanged): the loop is off, the full Decay.
- **Convolution**: null against the processed IR (noise IR 1.2 s, mono IR, reversed, Stretch 150 %): -148.8..-149.2 dB; a 6.0 s IR (288000 samples, 139 tail partitions): -148.9 dB; Stretch 150 % length ratio 1.500; mono IR: L == R exactly; a 100 ms pre-delay lands at +4800 samples exactly; Dry/Wet 75 % wet / dry +7.66 dB (equal power); an IR swapped mid-run keeps the output going.
### Files
`src/reverbplus_start.genexpr` (core; built as `cepstrum_lib + reverbplus_conv + start`), `src/reverbplus_conv.genexpr`, `src/reverbplus_end.genexpr`, `src/reverbplus_config.js` (constants, scope / header layouts, pure math, IR pipeline, telemetry helpers: prepended into every script and required by the tests), `src/reverbplus_link.js`, `src/reverbplus_dsp.js` (deferred gen), `src/reverbplus_engine.js` + `src/reverbplus_panels.js` (the engine), `src/reverbplus_face_common.js` (prelude of every face), `src/reverbplus_face.js` + `src/reverbplus_face_math.js` + `src/reverbplus_face_ir.js` (the face), `src/reverbplus_end_ui.js`, `scripts/build_reverbplus.py`, `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/` (+ `README.md` = the layout contract), `scripts/make_reverbplus_sets.py` (wraps the Delay+ set generator), `scripts/run_reverbplus_audio_tests.py` + `tests/reverbplus_probe.js`, `scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh`, `scripts/test_reverbplus_live.js`, `scripts/measure_reverbplus_load.py` (gen compile cost in the VM), `tests/test_reverbplus.js`, `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js`, `tsconfig.reverbplus-{engine,end,face,face-ir}.json`.

### Parameters (`P` in `scripts/build_reverbplus.py`; Live short names unique)
`Algorithm` (Plate / Hall / Room / Chamber / Spring / Convolution, default Hall), `Pre-Delay` 0..500 ms (20), `Size` 0..100 % (60), `Decay Time` 0.1..60 s (2.5; RT60 at the middle frequencies), `Diffusion` (80 %), `HF Damping` 1k..20 kHz (7k), `Bass Multiplier` 0.25..4x (1.2), `Crossover` 100..1000 Hz (350), `Mod Rate` 0.05..5 Hz, `Mod Depth` (25 %), `Early Level` / `Late Level` -70..+6 dB (-70 = off), `Low Cut` 20..1000 Hz, `High Cut` 1k..20 kHz, `Width` 0..150 %, `Freeze`, `Loop Clip Mode`, `Loop Clip Level` (the safety net of the chain loop), `Ducking` 0..100 % (up to -24 dB on the wet while the dry is loud: level -40..0 dBFS maps onto 0..1; End side), `Duck Release`, `Dry/Wet` (35 %), `Output` -36..+12 dB, `IR Stretch` 50..200 %, `IR Start` 0..50 %, `IR Reverse`, `IR Decay` 0..100 %, and `IR File` (a `live.drop` in parameter mode, hidden), then the appended `Input` -36..+12 dB (0 dB, `%.1f dB` like Output, gen `indb`: a Hybrid-Reverb-style send. It scales only what enters the tank / convolution after the pre-delay (`tL = xL * ig * inS`, `inS` one-pole smoothed over 10 ms; 0 dB is bit-identical); the dry that End mixes, the meters and the outer-loop return are NOT scaled, so the derived loop gain is unchanged). Gen messages and divisors are in the table (`prepend szp`, `dcy`, `diffp`, `dampf`, `bassm`, `xoverf`, `erlvl`, `latelvl`, `widthp`, `wetmix` ...: Param names that clash with gen operators are avoided). The IR controls are the engine's, not the gen's. `Low Cut` 20 Hz and `High Cut` 20 kHz (and Damping 20 kHz in Convolution) are exact bypasses of those filters.
- **Inactive parameters** (`RP_INACTIVE` in the config): the engine sets `active 0` on their `live.*` objects whatever the layout (`rpApplyActive`), and a face asks `rpActive(key)`. Convolution: Size, Decay, Diffusion, Bass Mult, Crossover, Mod Rate / Depth, Early, Freeze. Plate and Spring: Bass Mult, Crossover, Early. Everything but the IR controls applies to Hall / Room / Chamber. Convolution's Damping is a low-pass on the wet (min with High Cut); `IR Decay` is what shapes its tail.

### The algorithms (one compiled call site per big helper; only the selected one runs, the others output exact zeros)
All delay lines live in ONE `Data dl(1048576)` (a layout of power-of-two buffers is rebuilt on every algorithm entry, sized for 1.6x size and up to 96 kHz; `rp_rd` interpolates linearly, `rp_wr` writes, both functions of Data arguments). Line lengths are scaled by `0.4 + 1.2 Size` (`rpSizeScale`) and glide to a new target (one-pole 2000 samples: size sweeps bend the delays, no clicks). Per-pass gains come from `rpLineGain` (`10^(-3 len / (sr RT60))`) recomputed every 64 samples from the current lengths. Late output is level-normalised by `ns = sqrt(1 - g^2)` (clipped 0.12..1): white noise in comes out at about the same power, long decays do not get louder.
- **CPU** (`adstatus cpu` on the real-time driver, one core, includes the harness floor of about 9 %, integer-percent resolution; avg / max %, 2026-10-22, full suite): playing a noise source: Plate 1.0 / 3, Hall 1.6 / 4, Room 1.3 / 3, Chamber 1.3 / 3, Spring 1.0 / 4, Convolution 2 s IR 2.2 / 7, **Convolution 6 s IR 3.6 / 10**. **Silent input** (`perf_idle_<a>`, 4 s, the sleep engages after 0.68 s): Plate 0.7 / 3, Hall 0.8 / 4, Room 0.7 / 3, Chamber 0.7 / 3, Spring 0.6 / 3, against 1.1 / 2.1 / 1.6 / 1.6 / 1.2 with the sleep disabled (`RP_NOSLEEP=1`, same gen otherwise); that is the idle tank's cost (Hall: 2.1 -> 0.8). The removal of the dead telemetry and per-line energy work did not move the playing numbers beyond the measurement noise, but the Start gen compile fell from 3.08 s to 2.77 s (see Load time).
- **Load time** (`scripts/measure_reverbplus_load.py`, VM, harness wall time minus a trivial gen, 3 runs, 2026-10-22): the Start gen compiles in 3.08 s before / **2.77 s** after the telemetry cleanup, the End gen in **0.27 s** (0.25 s before). The End stays static (Delay+ keeps its End static too): deferring 0.27 s would add a second creation, a second wiring and the first-gen~ failure mode below for no visible gain. The Start's compile is hidden by the deferred creation (the GUI is up at once; DSP ready 1.55 s after the gen~ was created in Live).
- **In Live** (`scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh run`, 3 pairs; last three runs all checks passed): the chain loop checks per linked pair: derived loop 4123 / 3882 / 4387 samples = telemetry (Hall / Room / Plate), gain 0.704 / 0.714 / 0.694 = 10^(-3 (T + AP) / Decay), tank RT60 0.750 s = 0.3 x Decay 2.5 s. Live's own **Shifter** (`insert_device "Shifter"` between Start and End through the LOM; its LOM latency reads 0; parameter `Pitch Coarse` = 12) shifts the tone to 440 Hz (220 Hz 20 dB below) and the cascade is asserted: the strongest line within +-4 % of 880 / 1760 Hz over the last 0.34 s of the return is -3.0 / -5.1 dB and -4.9 / -4.7 dB vs 440 Hz (two runs; limits > -10 / > -20), and the pair re-times itself 0 times while it plays. Latest run (2026-10-22, after the cleanup): -3.4 / -4.4 dB.
- **"880 Hz 29..49 dB below 440 Hz in Live" was the measurement, not the loop** (investigated 2026-10-22). The old test read one exact FFT bin (220 x 2^n Hz) of the whole 1.4 s return ring; each pass of the loop smears the partial (the tank's modulation, the Shifter), so the cascaded lines are not on the exact bin (the same run read -38.8 / -39.0 dB with the single bin and -5.1 / -4.7 dB with the +-4 % peak). Evidence: octave bands of the return ring (`ret`) and of Start's tap (`tap`, what enters the chain) after a 220 Hz burst show energy climbing 440 -> 880 -> 1760 -> 3520 Hz (-45 / -37 / -31 / -30 dB), the return peak decays at the loop rate. The hypotheses about calibration were checked and are not the cause: with the Shifter in the chain K stays calibrated and constant (`hdr` 2 / 18), L = 256 (the Shifter reports 0), B 512, and the Start's mute counter (scope `MUTE_N` / `MUTE_L` / `MUTE_B` / `MUTE_K` 32..35, counts a mute only when it starts; `GLINK` 36 = the live gain of the mute) stays at its 1..4 start-up events (L 1, B 2, K 1) for the whole tone. A chain that decorrelates its output (the harness case `shimmer_vary`: shifter plus a +-8 sample, 1.7 Hz delay wobble, End's calibration can never lock) cascades like `shimmer` (4f -2.7 dB, 8f -5.4 dB vs 2f) with the same start-up mutes. Calibration therefore stays as in Delay+ (a calibration that fails keeps the counter K; it never mutes).
- **Dead pair (one of three Starts never started; the track varied from run to run)**: the Start's deferred gen~ stays at frame counter 0 for good (valid `.gendsp`, `gen~` present and wired, both gens of the other pairs run; the End's gen of that pair runs too: it only reports "not linked" because its Start is dead; the earlier note that the End gen was dead was a misreading of `escope[0]` = linked). It is the first gen~ created in the Live process (instance `#3` in the traces): its first attempt never joins the DSP chain, a second `gen~` created 6 s later runs after 1.8 s, and the two others (compile cached) run 158 ms after creation. It was not the chain-loop change. Fix (`src/reverbplus_dsp.js`): a gen~ whose frame counter has not moved after `RP_DSP_FIRST_TIMEOUT_MS` (3.5 s for the first attempt, `RP_DSP_TIMEOUT_MS` 6 s for the retries; was 6 s for all) is removed and created again (up to `RP_DSP_RETRIES` = 2 times, trace line `dsp-retry`), and instances create one at a time (`Global("reverbplus_dspq")` owner token, released when the gen runs or has failed for good, a claim older than 15 s is stale), so a retry does not collide with the others. Trace lines now end with `[#<LOM device id> @<epoch ms mod 1e6>]`. Cost: with the first-creation failure the first pair is ready about 7.7 s after its first `dsp-start` (2026-10-22 trace: created +2.5 s, retry +6.1 s, ready +8.9 s; the 6 s timeout needed about 2.4 s more, 12 s from the set load) instead of 3.4 s (the face is up at once; the other two follow within 0.2 s). A healthy gen~ runs 1.55 s after creation (0.16 s with the compile cached), so 3.5 s is more than twice that. It failed on the first creation again in this run, so the first-gen~ failure is still the rule on this VM. Not fixed, only worked around: why the first creation fails.


### Known rough edges
- **Idle sleep** (all algorithms but Convolution; `quietCnt`, `asleep`, scope `ASLEEP`): once the tank input and the wet output have both been below -140 dB (1e-7) for 32768 samples (longer than any line of the tank, 0.68 s at 48 kHz) the algorithm blocks are skipped (exact zeros, no per-sample FDN / Plate / Spring work); an input above 1e-7 wakes it in the same sample. What is left in the lines is far below audibility and stays frozen; lengths that glided toward a new Size while asleep snap to their targets on waking (the lines are empty, so there is no bend). Never while an algorithm change is in progress, and a Freeze that holds a tail above -140 dB never sleeps. Convolution is excluded: its frequency-domain delay line must keep advancing. Audio cases `sleep_<a>`, `sleep_size_hall`, `perf_idle_<a>` (and `RP_NOSLEEP=1` in the runner to compare).
- Convolution is channel-wise stereo (IR L on input L, IR R on input R; a mono IR on both): no cross terms, so a true-stereo (four-channel) IR is not supported.
- IR resampling (Stretch, sample-rate mismatch) is Hermite without an anti-alias filter: shrinking an IR by more than 2x (Stretch 50 % of a file at twice the device rate) can alias.
- Freeze is inactive in Convolution (the engine cannot loop its own tail); the control is greyed.
- The algorithms are not level-matched to each other (about +-4 dB, see Measured); Late Level and Output trim.
- Plate RT60 reads 6 % short, Room / Chamber up to 10 % short at Size 0; the gate is 15 %.
- Line buffers are sized for 96 kHz (a higher rate clamps the delay scale to 2x, so reverbs get shorter in time); the harness runs at 48 kHz only, Live at the set's rate (only 48 kHz measured).
- Spring is a dispersive-allpass loop pair, not a physical model; its Size changes the loop length (and bends the pitch of the chirps while moving).
- Size, Pre-Delay and Algorithm changes while running glide (tape-like), they do not crossfade; only an Algorithm change fades.
- **Drop anywhere on the face (every algorithm)**: an audio file dropped anywhere on the 444 x 169 face loads as the IR; outside Convolution it first switches Algorithm to Convolution. Mechanism: ONE Max `dropfile` object (`obj-dropany`, varname `rp_dropany`, `dropfile()` in `scripts/build_reverbplus.py`; added by `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/base.py`) over the whole face (`presentation_rect [0, 0, 444, 169]`), `ignoreclick 1`, `border 0`, fully transparent (`bordercolor` / `bgcolor` alpha 0), NOT a parameter (the one parameter `live.drop` stays `IR File`), last in `boxes` before the background panels, so it is in front of every v8ui and `live.*` widget (rule 4: it never captures a click, it only receives drops). `dropfile` outlet 0 -> `prepend anydrop` -> engine; `anydrop` (`src/reverbplus_engine.js`) ignores non-audio drops (`RP_AUDIO_EXT`: wav aif aiff aifc flac mp3 m4a mp4 caf au ogg oga opus sd2; no algorithm switch then). For an audio file, if `rpRaw.algo` is not Convolution it sends `algo 5` out of engine outlet 5 (the engine now has 6 outlets) -> `obj-route-key` -> the real `obj-ctrl-algo` object, the same path the face's own controls take, so Live sees the change, it is undoable and the groups / face update as on a manual switch (the object echoes `set_algo`, which updates `rpRaw`); then it does what `browsed` does: `outlet(4, path)` (`set` into the `IR File` live.drop, so Live saves and replays the path on Set load) and `rpIrLoad`. The `live.drop` box (and its replay `dropfile` message) is unchanged. The target exists in every algorithm. No drag-enter hover highlight: `dropfile` reports nothing until the drop (the display text says "drop an impulse response anywhere" and the DROP AN IR FILE pill stays).
  - **Evidence (Live 12.4.6, VM, real OS input)**: with the `dropfile` on top and `ignoreclick 1`, real CGEvent clicks still reach everything behind it (a click on the Tone tab changes the tab, a drag turns Dry/Wet, a click toggles Freeze, the display gets clicks and hover, a real right click on a dial opens Live's native menu window), AND a real file drag (`scripts/rp_drag_source.m`, an NSDraggingSource like Finder's, moved with `scripts/mm_os_input.py drag`) dropped on ten places (dials, tab, display, Freeze, Algorithm menu, LCD numbox, IR box, corner) loads the IR each time (engine `ir.name`) and the `IR File` parameter holds the path (`test_report` `diag.irParam`, reads the `valueof` attribute: `Macintosh HD:/private/tmp/rpdrop/<file>`, `dropfile` gives HFS-style paths, `buffer~ replace` and `rpFileName` accept them). So `ignoreclick` does not stop `dropfile` drops in Live (Max's drop hit-test does not use it). Drops while Hall / Plate / Spring switch Algorithm to Convolution and load (Live test). Negative controls: a `.txt` drop and a drop outside the device (on Live) do nothing (no switch). Not tried: a second transparent `live.drop` (a parameter would be a second parameter; Better Sampler: only parameter mode outputs), `bringtofront` games and v8ui drop hooks (the v8ui / jsui reference lists none), because `dropfile` already works.
  - Tests: `tests/test_reverbplus.js` (structure: exactly one `dropfile`, `ignoreclick`, full face, invisible, in front of every presentation box, wired through `prepend anydrop`, still exactly one `live.drop`; `anydrop` logic: every algorithm switches to Convolution then loads, audio only, rejoined split paths, stores the path), `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js` (every page x algorithm), `scripts/test_reverbplus_live.js` `faceSection` (needs `--ssh`; `scripts/vm_reverbplus.sh test` passes it): the click-through checks and the ten drops above. The face records the pointer / clicks into `Global("reverbplus_test")` only while `hoverOn` is set (`rcTestNote`); that is how the device is located on screen (the VM has no screenshots).
- The unpaired Start mixes dry and wet itself without ducking; the End's Ducking needs the pair.
- A face cannot change the engine: a face that needs another telemetry slot must extend `RP_SCOPE` (config + core + tests), which is a shared-file edit.
- Gen compile is 1.6..1.7 s in Live, 2.8 s in the harness VM (Delay+ Start 0.5..0.9 s): the gen has six algorithms; the deferred creation keeps the GUI instant. A full-set IR load takes a few seconds in the engine (a 6 s IR is 172 FFTs in 6 ms slices).
- **Measurement provenance** (2026-10-22): the full audio suite (200 cases, incl. `sleep_*`) and the Live test (`vm_reverbplus.sh run`, all checks) passed on the final code; the RT60 / level / convolution numbers above come from earlier runs of the same DSP (the idle sleep and the telemetry cleanup do not touch the sounding paths: `rt_*`, `lrt_*`, `freeze_*`, `conv_*` all still pass their gates). The shared VM is busy with other agents; harness cases and the Live lock queue behind them. If harness cases hang with 0 CPU time, check the VM's `WindowServer` (`ps`, state `U`): it needs a VM restart.
- Not run: a real Set save / reopen with a saved IR path. (The drop onto the `live.drop` box itself and onto the rest of the face is run with a real OS file drag, see "Drop anywhere on the face".)


### Face (`scripts/reverbplus_layouts/base.py`, "Hybrid tabs", 444 x 169 px)
Chosen from prototypes (the others were deleted); modelled on Ableton's Hybrid Reverb. The layout contract for any further face is `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/README.md`; `python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py` builds this one as `devices/Reverb+.amxd`. Files: `scripts/reverbplus_layouts/base.py`, `src/reverbplus_face_math.js` (pure helpers, loaded by the tests), `src/reverbplus_face.js` (display v8ui), `src/reverbplus_face_ir.js` (IR box v8uis), `src/types/reverbplus-face-ir.d.ts`, `tsconfig.reverbplus-face{,-ir}.json` (one group per script: the hook names `paint` / `onclick` clash), `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js`, `previews/reverb.png` + `reverb_tone.png` / `reverb_tail.png` / `reverb_conv.png` (`RC_PAGE=<0..2> RC_ALGO=<0..5>` at build AND render time: `RC_PAGE=2 python3 scripts/build_reverbplus.py --no-sets --out-dir /tmp/x && RC_PAGE=2 python3 scripts/render_preview.py /tmp/x/Reverb+.amxd _tail`).

**Layout** (all in px): body panel `0..444`; left column panel `x = 4..58, y = 4..165`; right column panel `x = 386..440`; display panel `x = 61..383` (`live_lcd_bg`). Panels are last, `background 1`, coloured by the engine (`panel_theme`).
- **Left column** (46 x 46 dials at `x = 8`): `Pre-Delay` (`y = 6`), hairline `y = 54`, `Input` (`y = 58`), hairline `y = 106`, `Decay` (`y = 110`; in Convolution the same rect holds `IR Decay`, swapped by the groups `decay` / `irdecay`); nothing below. The derived loop time and gain are not shown on the face (the Start derives the loop; telemetry still has them). **Latency warning**: while a paired Start has to lengthen the loop beyond what Size gives because the chain latency is too large (`CLAMPED`), the display's header shows an amber `LATENCY: LOOP <MIN_MS>` status pill on every tab (`rpHeaderPills` in `src/reverbplus_face_common.js`), like Delay+'s MIN warning.
- **Right column** (`x = 390`): `Width` dial (`y = 6`), `Freeze` chip (`x = 391, y = 58, 44 x 15`, hidden in Convolution), hairline (`y = 90`), `Dry/Wet` dial (`y = 100`, no title).
- **Tabs** `[Reverb | Tone | Tail]`: the hidden UI-state `live.tab` `Reverb Page` (`parameter_invisible 1`, stored with the Set) at `x = 153, y = 6, 138 x 15`.
- **Display v8ui** `obj-lay-viz` (`x = 64, y = 22, w = 316`, `h = 104`, **`h = 135` on the Tail tab**, outlets 2): info row (`y = 0..11`: Tone node readout / Tail title, nothing on the Reverb tab; status pills from `rpHeaderPills`: DSP loading / failed, no End, loop MIN, IR state), then the tab content. Below it, `y = 127` hairline + the **LCD row** of real `live.*` widgets (labels `y = 131`, values `y = 142`, cells 15 px high, themed `lcdcolor`, cyan = output / space, amber = decay), separated by 2 px `live.line` hairlines (`live_lcd_frame`). Which widgets show is decided by the display: outlet 1 sends `g_<group> <hide>` (`rbVis` in the math file, mirrored by `visible_groups` in `base.py`, checked equal by the test) -> `route` -> one `prepend hidden` per group; and `viz_tall <0|1>` -> `sel 0 1` -> `presentation_rect` messages into the v8ui (as Delay+'s ring), so the Tail tab's bars use the LCD row's space (the hairline `lcdsep` hides with it).
- **Input** (cases `input_0`, `input_m12`: Convolution Dirac IR, Dry/Wet 75 %, paired): Input -12 dB lowers the wet by 12.00 dB (wet / dry -4.34 dB vs +7.66 dB at 0 dB, gate +-0.2) and the dry peak stays 0.3827 (identical); `input_0` equals the `drywet` case. `conv_null` (-148.9 dB), `rt_hall`, `lrt_hall_a`, `looptime` unchanged.
  - **Reverb tab**: the **decay waterfall** (316 x 104 canvas): 28 rows (150 ms apart) of the 32-band wet spectrum (`rpSpectrum32` of `---rpspec`), newest in front, perspective `rcRowGeom`, amber **predicted decay** curve, unlabelled (`rcRt60Curve`: telemetry low / mid / high RT60, bass multiplier, HF damping); the display is not a control and carries no text of its own (clicks, drags and double-clicks send nothing; only Convolution shows `IR <length>`); freeze tint + `FROZEN` pill, Convolution: the processed IR's spectrogram (violet, computed once per IR version from `---rpirspec`, 8 ms per Task tick) behind the rows, a click on the empty plot without an IR = `browse_ir`. Dry / wet strip below the plot (wet cyan, dry grey, unlabelled). LCD row: `Algorithm` (`live.menu`, 74 px), then **tank**: `Size`, `Diffusion`, `Mod Depth` (the real `live.numbox`; the Tail tab's Mod Depth bar drives the same object through `rpSend`); **Convolution**: `Convolution IR` (name field v8ui `obj-lay-irname`, ignoreclick, with the transparent `live.drop` `IR File` in front of it = the rule-4 exception; browse / clear glyph buttons `obj-lay-irbtn`, 26 x 15), `Start`, `Stretch`, `Reverse` (`live.text`).
  - **Tone tab**: EQ view (`x = 26..310`, 20 Hz..20 kHz, +-15 dB): cyan = output filters (`Low Cut`, `High Cut`: 12 dB/oct, -3 dB at the cutoff), amber = decay speed per frequency (`Bass Mult` below `Crossover`, `HF Damp` shelf); four numbered draggable nodes (1 Low Cut, 2 High Cut, 3 HF Damp, 4 Bass: x = Crossover, y = bass multiplier; node 4 absent where `RP_INACTIVE` removes Bass Mult), double-click resets (60 Hz / 16 kHz / 7 kHz / x1.2 at 350 Hz). LCD row: `Low Cut`, `High Cut` | `HF Damp`, `Bass Mult`, `Crossover` (Bass / Crossover hidden in Plate, Spring, Convolution).
  - **Tail tab**: two columns of drawn value bars (100 px) and chips under titled hairlines, divider at `x = 160`: `MODULATION` (Rate, Depth) / `LEVELS` (Early, Late, Output) | `DUCKING` (Amount, Release) / `CLIP` (Level, Mode chips `Soft | Hard | Limit`, the **clip LED** at the title row's right end: lit while `CLIP_DB < -0.1`, held 1 s, fading 0.5 s). Drag a bar to set it (`rcVal` / `REP`: the scales equal the build's table, test-checked), double-click resets, a click on a chip sets `Clip Mode`; rows the algorithm does not use (`rpActive`) are greyed and ignored.
- **Visible controls** (live.* widgets on the face): Reverb tab 11 (Hall: Pre-Delay, Input, Decay, Width, Freeze, Dry/Wet, tab, Algorithm, Size, Diffusion, Mod Depth) / 11 (Convolution), Tone 12 (Hall) / 10 (Plate), Tail 7 (the side columns and the tab; the nine Tail parameters are drawn).
- **Off the face** (real, automatable `live.*` objects with `presentation 0`, listed in `off_face`; the Tail tab draws them and drives them with `rpSend`, the echo (`set_<key>`) redraws the bars): `modrate`, `erlevel`, `latelevel`, `output`, `duck`, `duckrel`, `looplevel`, `loopclip`. Live's own widgets for them are therefore not on the face (right-click > Show Automation / Map need the device parameter list); everything else is a real widget.
- **Telemetry used**: the spectrum rings (`rpNames.spec`, Reverb tab only), `RT60_LO / MID / HI` (the predicted decay curve), `FREEZE`, `CLIP_DB`, `SR`, `PAIRED`, `CLAMPED`, `MIN_MS`, `IN_PK` / `WET_PK` / `RET_PK` (idle detection); the IR header / spectra buffers; `rpState` dsp / link / ir. **Polling**: 20 fps while the device has signal (about 3 s after silence nothing is polled or redrawn). Only the Reverb tab runs the two 4096-point FFTs and keeps the waterfall history (leaving the tab drops it); the Tone tab is drawn from parameters alone and the Tail tab only follows the clip LED, so `onFrame` returns `false` (no redraw) for them unless the LED, the latency pill's inputs (`CLAMPED`, `MIN_MS`, `PAIRED`) changed.

**Gotchas / rough edges**
- The LCD row's `live.numbox` is snapped to 15 px in Live; values show Live's unit text (`2.50 s`), `render_preview.py` ignores `parameter_units` (shows `2`) and draws a vertical `live.line` as a short dash; neither matters in Live. The preview does not show `hidden` flips: tabs / Convolution are rendered by building with `RC_PAGE` / `RC_ALGO`, the swaps are checked by `tests/test_reverbplus_face.js` (saved flags, cords, outlet 1 messages, no overlap of visible widgets on any tab x algorithm, including the grown Tail display).
- The growing Tail display relies on `presentation_rect` messages reaching the v8ui (the Delay+ ring does the same, verified there); the saved rect is the Reverb / Tone one (rule 4 sees no overlap).
- The IR name is ellipsised in a 52 px field (`Cathedra...`); the full name is in the file the `live.drop` holds.
- The waterfall's history is 28 rows x 150 ms (about 4.2 s) and is rebuilt after a visit to another tab; the predicted-decay curve comes from the theoretical RT60 telemetry, nothing is measured on the face.
- The wet spectrum comes from a 4096-sample FFT of `---rpspec` every 150 ms (wet and dry), a few tenths of a millisecond in v8.
- **The chain loop is flat, not tank-coloured**: the long tail is the allpass-diffused recirculation of the chain's output, so the algorithm's character is the first pass (tank at 0.3 x Decay), the loop's damping / bass shelf and its time; the late tail of Plate / Hall / Room / Chamber / Spring sounds alike. With a chain that adds gain G > 1 / g the loop saturates at the clip and sustains (by design, as in Delay+); Soft / Hard / Limit and Clip Level bound it, RT60 then follows the chain gain. The tank no longer receives the return, so FX are heard in the tail but do not feed the tank's modes.
- The constants of the chain loop (`TANK_FRAC` 0.3, `AP` = 1.5 x the allpass lengths, the K table) are fits to Max renders at 48 kHz and a Hall-like reference model; at other rates only the lengths scale.
